# Command reference

Every NeptuneBASIC command, grouped by area. Generated by `python tools/gen_reference.py` from the command tables and [commands.txt](commands.txt); edit that file, not this one.

**Reading the entries:** a command that returns a value is shown as a function call, `Name(a, b)`, with the type it returns. Other commands are statements, `Name a, b`. A parameter's type is shown by the mark after its name: `%` a whole number, `#` a number with a fractional part, `$` text. Optional parameters are in brackets with their default value. **(ext)** marks commands NeptuneBASIC adds to the classic command set. Every entry has an example. To learn the language itself (`If`, `Type`, `Function`, ...), read [the manual](MANUAL.md).

1059 commands and 60 modern names.

## Program and system

### RuntimeError

`RuntimeError message$`

Stops the program with a runtime error showing message.

```basic
; stop with a message if a required value is missing
lives = 0
If lives <= 0 Then RuntimeError "The player has no lives left"
Print "never reached"
```

### CommandLine

`CommandLine()` → string

Returns the arguments passed to the program after the source or program name, joined by spaces.

```basic
; read the arguments given after the program name
args = CommandLine()
If args = "" Then Print "No arguments given" Else Print "Arguments: " + args
```

### GetEnv

`GetEnv(name$)` → string

Returns the value of the environment variable name, or an empty string if it is not set.

```basic
; read an environment variable (empty if not set)
user = GetEnv("USERNAME")
If user = "" Then Print "USERNAME is not set" Else Print "Hello, " + user
```

### SetEnv

`SetEnv name$, value$`

Sets the environment variable name to value for this program and any programs it starts.

```basic
; set a variable, then read it back
SetEnv "GAME_MODE", "hard"
Print GetEnv("GAME_MODE")   ; prints hard
```

### SystemProperty

`SystemProperty(property$)` → string

Returns a system property as a string: "appdir", "tempdir", "currentdir", "homedir", "windowsdir" and "systemdir" give folders ending in a path separator, "os" the operating system name and "cpucount" the number of CPU cores. Unknown properties return an empty string.

```basic
; ask the system about itself
Print "Operating system: " + SystemProperty("os")
Print "CPU cores: " + SystemProperty("cpucount")
Print "Temp folder: " + SystemProperty("tempdir")
```

### ExecFile

`ExecFile file$`

Opens file with the program the operating system associates with it (a document, web address or program); does not wait for it to finish.

```basic
; open a web address with the default browser (does not wait)
ExecFile "https://example.com"
Print "Browser requested"
```

## Console input and output

### Print

`Print [value=""]`

Writes value (any type, default an empty string) to the console followed by a new line.

```basic
; print text, numbers and an empty line
score = 120
Print "Score: " + score
Print 3.5
Print            ; an empty line
```

### Write

`Write value`

Writes value (any type) to the console without a new line.

```basic
; Write does not start a new line, so these join up
Write "Loading"
Write "..."
Print " done"
```

### Input

`Input([prompt$=""])` → string

Shows prompt on the console, waits for the user to type a line and returns it as a string without the line ending.

```basic
; ask the player a question and use the answer
name = Input("What is your name? ")
Print "Welcome, " + name
```

### DebugLog

`DebugLog message$`

Writes message to the debug log (standard error), followed by a new line.

```basic
; send a message to the debug log instead of the console
health = 75
DebugLog "Player health is " + health
```

## Maths

### Sqr

`Sqr(value#)` → float

Returns the square root of value.

```basic
; distance between two points with Pythagoras
dx = 3.0
dy = 4.0
distance = Sqr(dx * dx + dy * dy)
Print distance   ; 5
```

### Sin

`Sin(degrees#)` → float

Returns the sine of an angle in degrees.

```basic
; height of a bobbing object, angle in degrees
angle = 90
Print Sin(angle)   ; 1
Print Sin(0)       ; 0
```

### Cos

`Cos(degrees#)` → float

Returns the cosine of an angle in degrees.

```basic
; angles are in degrees
Print Cos(0)     ; 1
Print Cos(180)   ; -1
```

### Tan

`Tan(degrees#)` → float

Returns the tangent of an angle in degrees.

```basic
; tangent of 45 degrees is about 1
Print Tan(45)
```

### ASin

`ASin(value#)` → float

Returns the arc sine of value, in degrees.

```basic
; the angle whose sine is 0.5
angle = ASin(0.5)
Print angle   ; about 30 (floats are approximate)
```

### ACos

`ACos(value#)` → float

Returns the arc cosine of value, in degrees.

```basic
; the angle whose cosine is 0.5
angle = ACos(0.5)
Print angle   ; about 60
```

### ATan

`ATan(value#)` → float

Returns the arc tangent of value, in degrees.

```basic
; the angle whose tangent is 1
angle = ATan(1)
Print angle   ; 45
```

### ATan2

`ATan2(y#, x#)` → float

Returns the angle in degrees of the point (x, y) from the origin, from -180 to 180; note the y parameter comes first.

```basic
; direction from the origin to the point (x=1, y=1); y comes first
angle = ATan2(1, 1)
Print angle   ; 45
```

### Exp

`Exp(value#)` → float

Returns e raised to the power value.

```basic
; e raised to the power 1
Print Exp(1)   ; 2.71828
```

### Log

`Log(value#)` → float

Returns the natural logarithm of value.

```basic
; natural logarithm, the opposite of Exp
Print Log(Exp(2))   ; 2
```

### Log10

`Log10(value#)` → float

Returns the base-10 logarithm of value.

```basic
; how many digits: 1000 has a base-10 logarithm of 3
Print Log10(1000)   ; 3
```

### Floor

`Floor(value#)` → float

Returns value rounded down to the nearest whole number, as a float.

```basic
; round down to a whole number (still a float)
Print Floor(3.7)    ; 3
Print Floor(-3.2)   ; -4
```

### Ceil

`Ceil(value#)` → float

Returns value rounded up to the nearest whole number, as a float.

```basic
; round up to a whole number (still a float)
Print Ceil(3.2)    ; 4
Print Ceil(-3.7)   ; -3
```

### Min

`Min(a#, b#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the smaller of a and b.

```basic
; the smaller of two values
damage = Min(50, 20)
Print damage   ; 20
```

### Max

`Max(a#, b#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the larger of a and b.

```basic
; the larger of two values
health = Max(0, -5)
Print health   ; 0
```

### Clamp

`Clamp(value#, low#, high#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns value limited to the range low to high.

```basic
; keep a value between 0 and 100
health = Clamp(130, 0, 100)
Print health   ; 100
```

### Lerp

`Lerp(a#, b#, t#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the value a fraction t of the way from a to b (t = 0 gives a, t = 1 gives b).

```basic
; halfway between 10 and 20
x = Lerp(10, 20, 0.5)
Print x   ; 15
```

## Random numbers

### SeedRnd

`SeedRnd seed%`

Seeds the random number generator with seed so the same sequence of numbers is produced each run; MilliSecs() is a common seed.

```basic
; the same seed always gives the same random numbers
SeedRnd 1234
roll_a = Rand(1, 100)
SeedRnd 1234
roll_b = Rand(1, 100)
Print roll_a = roll_b   ; 1 (true)
```

### Rnd

`Rnd(from#, [to#=0])` → float

Returns a random float between from and to; with only one parameter it returns a float between 0 and from.

```basic
; a random float between 0 and 1, and between -5 and 5
SeedRnd MilliSecs()
chance = Rnd(1)
offset = Rnd(-5, 5)
Print chance
Print offset
```

### Rand

`Rand(from%, [to%=1])` → integer

Returns a random integer between from and to inclusive; with only one parameter it returns an integer between 1 and from.

```basic
; roll a six-sided die
SeedRnd MilliSecs()
roll = Rand(6)
Print "You rolled " + roll
Print "Between 10 and 20: " + Rand(10, 20)
```

### RndSeed

`RndSeed()` → integer

Returns the seed last set with SeedRnd.

```basic
; read back the seed that was set
SeedRnd 42
Print RndSeed()   ; 42
```

## Strings

### Left

`Left(string$, count%)` → string

Returns the first count characters of string.

```basic
; the first 3 characters
word = "Neptune"
Print Left(word, 3)   ; Nep
```

### Right

`Right(string$, count%)` → string

Returns the last count characters of string.

```basic
; the last 4 characters
word = "Neptune"
Print Right(word, 4)   ; tune
```

### Mid

`Mid(string$, offset%, [count%=-1])` → string

Returns count characters of string starting at character offset (1 is the first character); with count left at -1 it returns the rest of the string.

```basic
; 3 characters starting at character 2, and the rest of the text
word = "Neptune"
Print Mid(word, 2, 3)   ; ept
Print Mid(word, 4)      ; tune
```

### Len

`Len(string$)` → integer

Returns the number of characters in string.

```basic
; count the characters
name = "Neptune"
Print Len(name)   ; 7
```

### Upper

`Upper(string$)` → string

Returns string in upper case.

```basic
; shout the text
Print Upper("game over")   ; GAME OVER
```

### Lower

`Lower(string$)` → string

Returns string in lower case.

```basic
; compare text without caring about case
answer = "YES"
If Lower(answer) = "yes" Then Print "Confirmed"
```

### Trim

`Trim(string$)` → string

Returns string with spaces and other white space removed from both ends.

```basic
; remove spaces from both ends
name = Trim("   Bob  ")
Print "[" + name + "]"   ; [Bob]
```

### LSet

`LSet(string$, size%)` → string

Returns string padded with spaces on the right, or cut, to exactly size characters.

```basic
; pad on the right to make columns line up
Print LSet("Name", 10) + "|"    ; Name      |
Print LSet("Neptune", 4) + "|"  ; Nept|
```

### RSet

`RSet(string$, size%)` → string

Returns string padded with spaces on the left, or cut from the left, to exactly size characters.

```basic
; pad on the left to right-align numbers
Print RSet("42", 6)          ; "    42"
Print RSet("123456789", 4)   ; 6789
```

### Instr

`Instr(string$, find$, [from%=1])` → integer

Returns the position of find in string, searching from character from (default 1), or 0 if it is not found.

```basic
; find where text appears (0 if it does not)
sentence = "the quick brown fox"
pos = Instr(sentence, "quick")
Print pos   ; 5
If Instr(sentence, "cat") = 0 Then Print "no cat"
```

### Replace

`Replace(string$, from$, to$)` → string

Returns string with every occurrence of from replaced by to.

```basic
; swap every occurrence of one piece of text for another
text = "red car, red house"
Print Replace(text, "red", "blue")   ; blue car, blue house
```

### String

`String(string$, count%)` → string

Returns string repeated count times.

```basic
; repeat text to draw a bar
health = 7
Print "[" + String("#", health) + "]"
```

### Chr

`Chr(code%)` → string

Returns the one-character string for the character code code.

```basic
; character code 65 is the letter A
letter = Chr(65)
Print letter   ; A
```

### Asc

`Asc(string$)` → integer

Returns the character code of the first character of string, or -1 for an empty string.

```basic
; the character code of the first letter
code = Asc("A")
Print code   ; 65
```

### Hex

`Hex(value%)` → string

Returns value as an 8-digit hexadecimal string.

```basic
; show a number as 8 hex digits
Print Hex(255)        ; 000000FF
Print Hex(16711680)   ; 00FF0000
```

### Bin

`Bin(value%)` → string

Returns value as a 32-digit binary string.

```basic
; show the bits of a number as 32 binary digits
Print Bin(5)   ; 00000000000000000000000000000101
```

### TypeOf

`TypeOf(value)` → string

**(ext)** Returns the name of the type of value as a string, such as "Int", "Float" or "String".

```basic
; find out what kind of value a variable holds
score = 10
speed = 2.5
name = "Bob"
Print TypeOf(score)   ; Int
Print TypeOf(speed)   ; Float
Print TypeOf(name)    ; String
```

### FormatFloat

`FormatFloat(value#, decimals%)` → string

**(ext)** Returns value as a string with decimals digits after the decimal point (0 to 9).

```basic
; show a number with exactly 2 decimals
price = 3.14159
Print FormatFloat(price, 2)   ; 3.14
```

## Files and folders

### FileType

`FileType(path$)` → integer

Returns 1 if path is a file, 2 if it is a folder, or 0 if it does not exist.

```basic
; 0 = does not exist, 1 = file, 2 = folder
kind = FileType("save.dat")
If kind = 0 Then Print "No save file"
If kind = 1 Then Print "Found a file"
If kind = 2 Then Print "That is a folder"
```

### FileSize

`FileSize(path$)` → integer

Returns the size of the file at path in bytes, or 0 if it does not exist.

```basic
; write a small file and measure it
file = WriteFile("size_test.txt")
WriteLine file, "hello"
CloseFile file
Print FileSize("size_test.txt") + " bytes"
DeleteFile "size_test.txt"
```

### DeleteFile

`DeleteFile path$`

Deletes the file at path.

```basic
; remove a file if it exists
file = WriteFile("old_save.dat")
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "old_save.dat"
Print FileType("old_save.dat")   ; 0
```

### CopyFile

`CopyFile from$, to$`

Copies the file from to the path to.

```basic
; make a backup of a save file
file = WriteFile("save.dat")
WriteInt file, 100
CloseFile file
CopyFile "save.dat", "save_backup.dat"
Print FileType("save_backup.dat")   ; 1
DeleteFile "save.dat"
DeleteFile "save_backup.dat"
```

### CreateDir

`CreateDir path$`

Creates the folder path.

```basic
; make a folder for save games
CreateDir "saves"
If FileType("saves") = 2 Then Print "Folder ready"
DeleteDir "saves"
```

### DeleteDir

`DeleteDir path$`

Deletes the folder path; it must be empty.

```basic
; a folder must be empty before it can be deleted
CreateDir "temp_stuff"
DeleteDir "temp_stuff"
Print FileType("temp_stuff")   ; 0
```

### CurrentDir

`CurrentDir()` → string

Returns the current folder, ending in a path separator.

```basic
; show the folder the program is working in
Print "Working in " + CurrentDir()
```

### ChangeDir

`ChangeDir path$`

Makes path the current folder.

```basic
; move into a folder, then back again
start = CurrentDir()
CreateDir "levels"
ChangeDir "levels"
Print CurrentDir()
ChangeDir start
DeleteDir "levels"
```

### ReadDir

`ReadDir(dirname$)` → integer

Opens the folder dirname for listing and returns a handle, or 0 if it cannot be opened.

```basic
; list everything in the current folder
dir = ReadDir(".")
If dir <> 0
    name = NextFile(dir)
    While name <> ""
        Print name
        name = NextFile(dir)
    Wend
    CloseDir dir
EndIf
```

### NextFile

`NextFile(dir%)` → string

Returns the next name in the folder listing dir ("." and ".." come first), or an empty string when there are no more; use FileType to tell files from folders.

```basic
; list the files (not the folders) of the current folder
dir = ReadDir(".")
name = NextFile(dir)
While name <> ""
    If FileType(name) = 1 Then Print name
    name = NextFile(dir)
Wend
CloseDir dir
```

### CloseDir

`CloseDir dir%`

Closes the folder listing dir.

```basic
; always close a folder listing when finished
dir = ReadDir(".")
If dir <> 0 Then CloseDir dir
```

## Streams

### ReadFile

`ReadFile(path$)` → integer

Opens the file at path for reading and returns a stream handle, or 0 if it cannot be opened.

```basic
; read a text file line by line
file = ReadFile("highscores.txt")
If file = 0
    Print "Could not open the file"
Else
    While Not Eof(file)
        Print ReadLine(file)
    Wend
    CloseFile file
EndIf
```

### WriteFile

`WriteFile(path$)` → integer

Creates (or empties) the file at path for writing and returns a stream handle, or 0 on failure.

```basic
; create a text file and write two lines
file = WriteFile("notes.txt")
If file <> 0
    WriteLine file, "First line"
    WriteLine file, "Second line"
    CloseFile file
EndIf
```

### OpenFile

`OpenFile(path$)` → integer

Opens an existing file at path for reading and writing and returns a stream handle, or 0 on failure.

```basic
; open an existing file to read and change it
file = OpenFile("save.dat")
If file <> 0
    Print "Old value: " + ReadInt(file)
    SeekFile file, 0       ; go back to the start
    WriteInt file, 200     ; overwrite it
    CloseFile file
EndIf
```

### CloseFile

`CloseFile stream%`

Closes the file stream.

```basic
; close the file so everything is saved
file = WriteFile("log.txt")
WriteLine file, "Game started"
CloseFile file
```

### Eof

`Eof(stream%)` → integer

Returns true once stream has no more data to read (for a TCP stream, when the connection has closed).

```basic
; keep reading until the end of the file
file = ReadFile("names.txt")
If file <> 0
    While Not Eof(file)
        Print ReadLine(file)
    Wend
    CloseFile file
EndIf
```

### ReadLine

`ReadLine(stream%)` → string

Reads a line of text from stream and returns it without the line ending.

```basic
; read the first line of a file
file = ReadFile("names.txt")
If file <> 0
    firstname = ReadLine(file)
    Print "First name: " + firstname
    CloseFile file
EndIf
```

### WriteLine

`WriteLine stream%, text$`

Writes text to stream followed by a line ending.

```basic
; write a line of text to a file
file = WriteFile("log.txt")
WriteLine file, "Player entered level 1"
CloseFile file
```

### ReadByte

`ReadByte(stream%)` → integer

Reads one byte (0-255) from stream and returns it.

```basic
; read a single byte (0 to 255)
file = WriteFile("byte.dat")
WriteByte file, 200
CloseFile file
file = ReadFile("byte.dat")
Print ReadByte(file)   ; 200
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "byte.dat"
```

### ReadShort

`ReadShort(stream%)` → integer

Reads a 2-byte unsigned integer (0-65535) from stream and returns it.

```basic
; read a 2-byte number (0 to 65535)
file = WriteFile("short.dat")
WriteShort file, 40000
CloseFile file
file = ReadFile("short.dat")
Print ReadShort(file)   ; 40000
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "short.dat"
```

### ReadInt

`ReadInt(stream%)` → integer

Reads a 4-byte integer from stream and returns it.

```basic
; read a 4-byte whole number
file = WriteFile("score.dat")
WriteInt file, 123456
CloseFile file
file = ReadFile("score.dat")
score = ReadInt(file)
Print score   ; 123456
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "score.dat"
```

### ReadFloat

`ReadFloat(stream%)` → float

Reads a 4-byte float from stream and returns it.

```basic
; read a 4-byte decimal number
file = WriteFile("speed.dat")
WriteFloat file, 2.5
CloseFile file
file = ReadFile("speed.dat")
speed = ReadFloat(file)
Print speed   ; 2.5
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "speed.dat"
```

### ReadString

`ReadString(stream%)` → string

Reads a string stored as a 4-byte length followed by its characters (as written by WriteString) from stream.

```basic
; read text saved with WriteString
file = WriteFile("name.dat")
WriteString file, "Captain"
CloseFile file
file = ReadFile("name.dat")
name = ReadString(file)
Print name   ; Captain
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "name.dat"
```

### WriteByte

`WriteByte stream%, value%`

Writes value to stream as one byte.

```basic
; save a value from 0 to 255
file = WriteFile("level.dat")
WriteByte file, 7
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "level.dat"
```

### WriteShort

`WriteShort stream%, value%`

Writes value to stream as a 2-byte integer.

```basic
; save a value from 0 to 65535
file = WriteFile("coins.dat")
WriteShort file, 1500
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "coins.dat"
```

### WriteInt

`WriteInt stream%, value%`

Writes value to stream as a 4-byte integer.

```basic
; save a whole number
file = WriteFile("score.dat")
WriteInt file, 99999
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "score.dat"
```

### WriteFloat

`WriteFloat stream%, value#`

Writes value to stream as a 4-byte float.

```basic
; save a decimal number
file = WriteFile("position.dat")
WriteFloat file, 12.75
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "position.dat"
```

### WriteString

`WriteString stream%, value$`

Writes value to stream as a 4-byte length followed by its characters.

```basic
; save text with its length, so ReadString can read it back
file = WriteFile("player.dat")
WriteString file, "Captain"
WriteInt file, 3
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "player.dat"
```

### ReadAvail

`ReadAvail(stream%)` → integer

Returns how many bytes can be read from stream without waiting: for a file, the bytes left before its end.

```basic
; how many bytes are left to read
file = WriteFile("data.dat")
WriteInt file, 1
WriteInt file, 2
CloseFile file
file = ReadFile("data.dat")
Print ReadAvail(file)   ; 8
skipped = ReadInt(file)
Print ReadAvail(file)   ; 4
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "data.dat"
```

### FilePos

`FilePos(stream%)` → integer

Returns the current read/write position in the file stream, in bytes from the start.

```basic
; see how far through the file we are
file = WriteFile("pos.dat")
WriteInt file, 10
Print FilePos(file)   ; 4
WriteInt file, 20
Print FilePos(file)   ; 8
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "pos.dat"
```

### SeekFile

`SeekFile(stream%, pos%)` → integer

Moves the read/write position of the file stream to pos bytes from the start and returns the new position.

```basic
; jump to the second number in a file
file = WriteFile("numbers.dat")
WriteInt file, 10
WriteInt file, 20
CloseFile file
file = OpenFile("numbers.dat")
newpos = SeekFile(file, 4)   ; skip the first 4 bytes
Print ReadInt(file)          ; 20
CloseFile file
DeleteFile "numbers.dat"
```

### CopyStream

`CopyStream src_stream%, dest_stream%, [buffer_size%=16384]`

Copies all remaining data from src_stream to dest_stream, buffer_size bytes at a time.

```basic
; copy one file to another using streams
source = ReadFile("level1.dat")
If source <> 0
    copy = WriteFile("level1_copy.dat")
    CopyStream source, copy    ; default buffer size
    CloseFile copy
    CloseFile source
EndIf
```

## Banks

### CreateBank

`CreateBank([size%=0])` → integer

Creates a bank (a block of memory) of size bytes, filled with zeros, and returns its handle.

```basic
; make a bank of 16 bytes (filled with zeros)
bank = CreateBank(16)
Print BankSize(bank)   ; 16
FreeBank bank
```

### FreeBank

`FreeBank bank%`

Deletes bank.

```basic
; free a bank when you no longer need it
bank = CreateBank(1024)
FreeBank bank
```

### BankSize

`BankSize(bank%)` → integer

Returns the size of bank in bytes.

```basic
; ask how big a bank is
bank = CreateBank(256)
Print "Bank holds " + BankSize(bank) + " bytes"
FreeBank bank
```

### ResizeBank

`ResizeBank bank%, size%`

Changes the size of bank to size bytes, keeping its contents; new bytes are zero.

```basic
; grow a bank; the old data is kept and new bytes are zero
bank = CreateBank(4)
PokeInt bank, 0, 77
ResizeBank bank, 8
Print PeekInt(bank, 0)   ; 77
Print PeekInt(bank, 4)   ; 0
FreeBank bank
```

### CopyBank

`CopyBank src_bank%, src_offset%, dest_bank%, dest_offset%, count%`

Copies count bytes from src_bank at src_offset to dest_bank at dest_offset.

```basic
; copy 4 bytes from one bank into another
source = CreateBank(8)
target = CreateBank(8)
PokeInt source, 0, 1234
CopyBank source, 0, target, 4, 4   ; from source at 0 to target at 4, 4 bytes
Print PeekInt(target, 4)           ; 1234
FreeBank source
FreeBank target
```

### PeekByte

`PeekByte(bank%, offset%)` → integer

Returns the byte (0-255) at offset in bank.

```basic
; read one byte from a bank
bank = CreateBank(4)
PokeByte bank, 2, 200
Print PeekByte(bank, 2)   ; 200
FreeBank bank
```

### PeekShort

`PeekShort(bank%, offset%)` → integer

Returns the 2-byte unsigned integer at offset in bank.

```basic
; read a 2-byte number from a bank
bank = CreateBank(4)
PokeShort bank, 0, 5000
Print PeekShort(bank, 0)   ; 5000
FreeBank bank
```

### PeekInt

`PeekInt(bank%, offset%)` → integer

Returns the 4-byte integer at offset in bank.

```basic
; read a 4-byte number from a bank
bank = CreateBank(8)
PokeInt bank, 4, 100000
Print PeekInt(bank, 4)   ; 100000
FreeBank bank
```

### PeekFloat

`PeekFloat(bank%, offset%)` → float

Returns the 4-byte float at offset in bank.

```basic
; read a 4-byte decimal from a bank
bank = CreateBank(8)
PokeFloat bank, 0, 1.5
Print PeekFloat(bank, 0)   ; 1.5
FreeBank bank
```

### PokeByte

`PokeByte bank%, offset%, value%`

Writes value as one byte at offset in bank.

```basic
; store a value from 0 to 255 at offset 0
bank = CreateBank(4)
PokeByte bank, 0, 255
Print PeekByte(bank, 0)
FreeBank bank
```

### PokeShort

`PokeShort bank%, offset%, value%`

Writes value as a 2-byte integer at offset in bank.

```basic
; store a 2-byte number at offset 2
bank = CreateBank(4)
PokeShort bank, 2, 60000
Print PeekShort(bank, 2)
FreeBank bank
```

### PokeInt

`PokeInt bank%, offset%, value%`

Writes value as a 4-byte integer at offset in bank.

```basic
; store a 4-byte number at offset 0
bank = CreateBank(4)
PokeInt bank, 0, -5
Print PeekInt(bank, 0)   ; -5
FreeBank bank
```

### PokeFloat

`PokeFloat bank%, offset%, value#`

Writes value as a 4-byte float at offset in bank.

```basic
; store a decimal number at offset 4
bank = CreateBank(8)
PokeFloat bank, 4, 3.25
Print PeekFloat(bank, 4)   ; 3.25
FreeBank bank
```

### ReadBytes

`ReadBytes(bank%, stream%, offset%, count%)` → integer

Reads up to count bytes from stream into bank starting at offset and returns the number of bytes read.

```basic
; load the bytes of a file into a bank
file = ReadFile("data.bin")
If file <> 0
    bank = CreateBank(64)
    count = ReadBytes(bank, file, 0, 64)   ; up to 64 bytes, into the bank from offset 0
    Print count + " bytes read"
    CloseFile file
    FreeBank bank
EndIf
```

### WriteBytes

`WriteBytes(bank%, stream%, offset%, count%)` → integer

Writes count bytes from bank starting at offset to stream and returns the number written.

```basic
; save the contents of a bank to a file
bank = CreateBank(4)
PokeInt bank, 0, 42
file = WriteFile("bank.dat")
written = WriteBytes(bank, file, 0, 4)   ; 4 bytes from bank offset 0
Print written + " bytes written"
CloseFile file
FreeBank bank
DeleteFile "bank.dat"
```

## Time and timers

### MilliSecs

`MilliSecs()` → integer

Returns the number of milliseconds since the program started.

```basic
; measure how long something takes
start = MilliSecs()
Delay 50
elapsed = MilliSecs() - start
Print "Took " + elapsed + " ms"
```

### Delay

`Delay millisecs%`

Pauses the program for millisecs milliseconds.

```basic
; pause for half a second
Print "Wait..."
Delay 500
Print "Done"
```

### CurrentDate

`CurrentDate()` → string

Returns today's date as a string in the form "dd Mon yyyy".

```basic
; show today's date, for example 07 Nov 2026
Print "Today is " + CurrentDate()
```

### CurrentTime

`CurrentTime()` → string

Returns the current time as a string in the form "hh:mm:ss".

```basic
; show the time, for example 14:30:05
Print "The time is " + CurrentTime()
```

### CreateTimer

`CreateTimer(hertz%)` → integer

Creates a timer that ticks hertz times per second and returns its handle; use it with WaitTimer to run a program at a steady frame rate.

```basic
; a timer that ticks 60 times per second
timer = CreateTimer(60)
FreeTimer timer
```

### WaitTimer

`WaitTimer(timer%)` → integer

Waits until timer next ticks and returns the number of ticks that have passed since the last call (at least 1).

```basic
; run a loop at a steady 60 steps per second
timer = CreateTimer(60)
For frame = 1 To 3
    ticks = WaitTimer(timer)   ; at least 1; more if the program fell behind
    Print "Step " + frame + ", ticks passed: " + ticks
Next
FreeTimer timer
```

### FreeTimer

`FreeTimer timer%`

Deletes timer.

```basic
; delete the timer when finished with it
timer = CreateTimer(30)
ticks = WaitTimer(timer)
FreeTimer timer
```

## Networking

### OpenTCPStream

`OpenTCPStream(server$, port%)` → integer

Connects to server (a host name or IP address) on port and returns a TCP stream handle, or 0 if the connection fails. Use the stream commands to send and receive.

```basic
; connect to a server and send a line
stream = OpenTCPStream("127.0.0.1", 5000)
If stream = 0
    Print "Could not connect"
Else
    WriteLine stream, "hello"
    CloseTCPStream stream
EndIf
```

### CloseTCPStream

`CloseTCPStream stream%`

Closes the TCP stream.

```basic
; close a connection when finished
stream = OpenTCPStream("127.0.0.1", 5000)
If stream <> 0 Then CloseTCPStream stream
```

### CreateTCPServer

`CreateTCPServer(port%)` → integer

Starts a TCP server listening on port and returns its handle, or 0 on failure.

```basic
; start listening on port 5000
server = CreateTCPServer(5000)
If server = 0 Then Print "Could not start the server" Else Print "Listening"
If server <> 0 Then CloseTCPServer server
```

### CloseTCPServer

`CloseTCPServer server%`

Stops the TCP server.

```basic
; stop the server
server = CreateTCPServer(5001)
If server <> 0 Then CloseTCPServer server
```

### AcceptTCPStream

`AcceptTCPStream(server%)` → integer

Returns a stream for the next client that has connected to server, or 0 if none is waiting.

```basic
; check for a new client (0 means nobody is waiting)
server = CreateTCPServer(5002)
If server <> 0
    client = AcceptTCPStream(server)
    If client <> 0
        Print "A player connected"
        CloseTCPStream client
    EndIf
    CloseTCPServer server
EndIf
```

### TCPStreamIP

`TCPStreamIP(stream%)` → integer

Returns the integer IP address of the other end of the TCP stream; use DottedIP to show it.

```basic
; show who connected
server = CreateTCPServer(5003)
If server <> 0
    client = AcceptTCPStream(server)
    If client <> 0 Then Print "From " + DottedIP(TCPStreamIP(client))
    CloseTCPServer server
EndIf
```

### TCPStreamPort

`TCPStreamPort(stream%)` → integer

Returns the port of the other end of the TCP stream.

```basic
; the port of the other end of a connection
server = CreateTCPServer(5004)
If server <> 0
    client = AcceptTCPStream(server)
    If client <> 0 Then Print "Remote port: " + TCPStreamPort(client)
    CloseTCPServer server
EndIf
```

### TCPTimeouts

`TCPTimeouts read_millis%, accept_millis%`

Sets how long TCP reads wait for data (read_millis) and how long AcceptTCPStream waits for a client (accept_millis), in milliseconds.

```basic
; wait up to 2 seconds for data, and 100 ms for a new client
TCPTimeouts 2000, 100
server = CreateTCPServer(5005)
If server <> 0
    client = AcceptTCPStream(server)   ; gives up after 100 ms
    CloseTCPServer server
EndIf
```

### CreateUDPStream

`CreateUDPStream([port%=0])` → integer

Opens a UDP stream on port (0 picks a free port) and returns its handle, or 0 on failure.

```basic
; open a UDP stream on any free port
udp = CreateUDPStream()
If udp <> 0
    Print "Using port " + UDPStreamPort(udp)
    CloseUDPStream udp
EndIf
```

### CloseUDPStream

`CloseUDPStream stream%`

Closes the UDP stream.

```basic
; close the UDP stream when finished
udp = CreateUDPStream(6000)
If udp <> 0 Then CloseUDPStream udp
```

### SendUDPMsg

`SendUDPMsg stream%, dest_ip%, [dest_port%=0]`

Sends the data written to the UDP stream as one message to the integer IP address dest_ip and dest_port (0 uses the stream's own port).

```basic
; write data into the stream, then send it as one message
udp = CreateUDPStream()
WriteInt udp, 42
WriteString udp, "hello"
SendUDPMsg udp, 2130706433, 6001   ; to 127.0.0.1, port 6001
CloseUDPStream udp
```

### RecvUDPMsg

`RecvUDPMsg(stream%)` → integer

Receives the next UDP message on stream so it can be read with the stream commands, and returns the sender's integer IP address, or 0 if there is none.

```basic
; receive a message and read it with the stream commands
udp = CreateUDPStream(6002)
If udp <> 0
    sender = RecvUDPMsg(udp)
    If sender <> 0
        Print "Message from " + DottedIP(sender)
        Print ReadInt(udp)
    EndIf
    CloseUDPStream udp
EndIf
```

### UDPStreamIP

`UDPStreamIP(stream%)` → integer

Returns the local integer IP address of the UDP stream.

```basic
; the local address of the UDP stream
udp = CreateUDPStream()
If udp <> 0
    Print "Local IP: " + DottedIP(UDPStreamIP(udp))
    CloseUDPStream udp
EndIf
```

### UDPStreamPort

`UDPStreamPort(stream%)` → integer

Returns the local port of the UDP stream.

```basic
; the local port the stream is using
udp = CreateUDPStream(0)
If udp <> 0
    Print "Port: " + UDPStreamPort(udp)
    CloseUDPStream udp
EndIf
```

### UDPMsgIP

`UDPMsgIP(stream%)` → integer

Returns the integer IP address of the sender of the last message received on the UDP stream.

```basic
; where the last message came from
udp = CreateUDPStream(6003)
If udp <> 0
    If RecvUDPMsg(udp) <> 0 Then Print "From " + DottedIP(UDPMsgIP(udp))
    CloseUDPStream udp
EndIf
```

### UDPMsgPort

`UDPMsgPort(stream%)` → integer

Returns the port of the sender of the last message received on the UDP stream.

```basic
; the port the last message was sent from, so you can reply
udp = CreateUDPStream(6004)
If udp <> 0
    If RecvUDPMsg(udp) <> 0 Then Print "Reply to port " + UDPMsgPort(udp)
    CloseUDPStream udp
EndIf
```

### UDPTimeouts

`UDPTimeouts recv_millis%`

Sets how long RecvUDPMsg waits for a message, in milliseconds (recv_millis).

```basic
; wait up to 50 ms for a message
UDPTimeouts 50
udp = CreateUDPStream(6005)
If udp <> 0
    If RecvUDPMsg(udp) = 0 Then Print "Nothing arrived"
    CloseUDPStream udp
EndIf
```

## Multiplayer games

### StartNetGame

`StartNetGame()` → integer

Joins a game found on the local network, or hosts a new one if there is none. Returns 1 if it joined, 2 if it is hosting, or 0 on failure. Uses TCP, with no dialog.

```basic
; join a game on the network, or host one if none is found
result = StartNetGame()
If result = 1 Then Print "Joined a game"
If result = 2 Then Print "Hosting a new game"
If result = 0 Then Print "Could not start"
StopNetGame
```

### HostNetGame

`HostNetGame(game_name$)` → integer

Hosts a multiplayer game called game_name that others can join, and returns 2 on success or 0 on failure.

```basic
; host a game that others can join
If HostNetGame("Space Race") = 2
    Print "Hosting"
    StopNetGame
Else
    Print "Could not host"
EndIf
```

### JoinNetGame

`JoinNetGame(game_name$, server_ip$)` → integer

Joins the game game_name at server_ip (an empty address searches the local network) and returns 1 on success or 0 on failure.

```basic
; join a game on the local network (empty address = search)
If JoinNetGame("Space Race", "") = 1
    Print "Joined"
    StopNetGame
Else
    Print "No game found"
EndIf
```

### StopNetGame

`StopNetGame`

Leaves or stops the current multiplayer game.

```basic
; leave the game when finished
If HostNetGame("Space Race") = 2 Then StopNetGame
```

### CreateNetPlayer

`CreateNetPlayer(name$)` → integer

Creates a local player called name in the current game and returns its player id.

```basic
; add the local player to the game
If HostNetGame("Space Race") = 2
    player = CreateNetPlayer("Alice")
    Print "My player id is " + player
    StopNetGame
EndIf
```

### DeleteNetPlayer

`DeleteNetPlayer player%`

Removes the local player from the game.

```basic
; remove the local player from the game
If HostNetGame("Space Race") = 2
    player = CreateNetPlayer("Alice")
    DeleteNetPlayer player
    StopNetGame
EndIf
```

### NetPlayerName

`NetPlayerName(player%)` → string

Returns the name of player.

```basic
; show the name of a player
If HostNetGame("Space Race") = 2
    player = CreateNetPlayer("Alice")
    Print NetPlayerName(player)   ; Alice
    StopNetGame
EndIf
```

### NetPlayerLocal

`NetPlayerLocal(player%)` → integer

Returns true if player was created on this computer.

```basic
; true for players created on this computer
If HostNetGame("Space Race") = 2
    player = CreateNetPlayer("Alice")
    If NetPlayerLocal(player) Then Print "That is me"
    StopNetGame
EndIf
```

### SendNetMsg

`SendNetMsg(type%, data$, from_player%, [to_player%=0], [reliable%=1])` → integer

Sends a message of type (1-99) with the text data from from_player to to_player (0 sends to all players). Returns true on success. reliable is accepted for compatibility: messages are always sent reliably.

```basic
; send a type-1 message from me to everyone (to_player = 0)
If HostNetGame("Space Race") = 2
    me = CreateNetPlayer("Alice")
    ok = SendNetMsg(1, "hello everyone", me, 0)
    If ok Then Print "Sent"
    StopNetGame
EndIf
```

### RecvNetMsg

`RecvNetMsg()` → integer

Fetches the next received message and returns true, or false if there is none; read it with NetMsgType, NetMsgFrom, NetMsgTo and NetMsgData.

```basic
; handle every message that has arrived
While RecvNetMsg()
    Print "Type " + NetMsgType() + " from player " + NetMsgFrom() + ": " + NetMsgData()
Wend
```

### NetMsgType

`NetMsgType()` → integer

Returns the type of the last received message: 1-99 for player messages, 100 for a player joining, 101 for a player leaving and 200 when the session is lost.

```basic
; tell player messages apart from joins and leaves
While RecvNetMsg()
    kind = NetMsgType()
    If kind = 100 Then Print "A player joined"
    If kind = 101 Then Print "A player left"
    If kind = 200 Then Print "The session was lost"
    If kind >= 1 And kind <= 99 Then Print "Player message"
Wend
```

### NetMsgFrom

`NetMsgFrom()` → integer

Returns the player id that sent the last received message (for type 100, the new player).

```basic
; find out who sent the message
While RecvNetMsg()
    sender = NetMsgFrom()
    Print NetPlayerName(sender) + " says " + NetMsgData()
Wend
```

### NetMsgTo

`NetMsgTo()` → integer

Returns the player id the last received message was sent to, or 0 if it was sent to everyone.

```basic
; 0 means the message was sent to everyone
While RecvNetMsg()
    If NetMsgTo() = 0 Then Print "Broadcast: " + NetMsgData()
Wend
```

### NetMsgData

`NetMsgData()` → string

Returns the text of the last received message.

```basic
; read the text of the message
While RecvNetMsg()
    Print "Received: " + NetMsgData()
Wend
```

### DottedIP

`DottedIP(ip%)` → string

Returns the integer IP address ip as a dotted string such as "192.168.0.1".

```basic
; turn an integer address into text
Print DottedIP(3232235521)   ; 192.168.0.1
```

### CountHostIPs

`CountHostIPs(host_name$)` → integer

Looks up the IP addresses of host_name (an empty string for this computer) and returns how many were found.

```basic
; look up this computer's addresses
count = CountHostIPs("")
Print count + " address(es) found"
For index = 1 To count
    Print DottedIP(HostIP(index))
Next
```

### HostIP

`HostIP(index%)` → integer

Returns address number index (from 1) found by the last CountHostIPs, as an integer IP address.

```basic
; show the first address of a host
If CountHostIPs("localhost") > 0
    Print DottedIP(HostIP(1))
EndIf
```

## Plugins and libraries

### CallDLL

`CallDLL(dll_name$, proc_name$, [in_bank%=0], [out_bank%=0])` → integer

Calls the function proc_name in the shared library dll_name, passing the contents of in_bank and out_bank (0 for none); the function can write results into out_bank. Returns the function's integer result. The library stays loaded.

```basic
; call a function in a shared library, passing it a bank
input = CreateBank(8)
PokeInt input, 0, 21
output = CreateBank(8)
result = CallDLL("mylib", "Double", input, output)   ; the function can write into output
Print "Function returned " + result
Print "Output: " + PeekInt(output, 0)
```

## Graphics and display

### Graphics3D

`Graphics3D width%, height%, [depth%=0], [mode%=0]`

Opens a window of width by height pixels for 3D and 2D graphics. mode 0 or 2 gives a window, 1 fullscreen, 3 a scaled window; depth is accepted for compatibility.

```basic
; Open a 800 by 600 window that can draw 3D and 2D
Graphics3D 800, 600, 32, 2   ; mode 2 = window, 1 = fullscreen, 3 = scaled window
camera = CreateCamera()
RenderWorld
Flip
```

### Graphics

`Graphics width%, height%, [depth%=0], [mode%=0]`

Opens a window of width by height pixels for 2D graphics; mode 0 or 2 gives a window, 1 fullscreen, 3 a scaled window.

```basic
; Open a 640 by 480 window for 2D drawing only
Graphics 640, 480, 32, 2   ; mode 2 = window
Text 10, 10, "Hello"
Flip
```

### EndGraphics

`EndGraphics`

Accepted for compatibility; does nothing.

```basic
; Close down graphics when the program is finished
Graphics 640, 480
Text 10, 10, "Bye"
Flip
EndGraphics
```

### AppTitle

`AppTitle title$, [close_prompt$=""]`

Sets the window title to title. close_prompt is accepted for compatibility.

```basic
; Set the title shown on the window
Graphics 640, 480
AppTitle "My First Game"
```

### SetFullscreen

`SetFullscreen enable%`

**(ext)** Switches between a window (enable = 0) and borderless fullscreen (enable = 1) at any time.

```basic
; Switch between a window and fullscreen while the program runs
Graphics 640, 480
SetFullscreen 1   ; 1 = borderless fullscreen
SetFullscreen 0   ; 0 = back to a window
```

### IsFullscreen

`IsFullscreen()` → integer

**(ext)** Returns true if the program is running fullscreen.

```basic
; Find out if the program is running fullscreen
Graphics 640, 480
If IsFullscreen() Then Print "Fullscreen" Else Print "In a window"
```

### GfxMode3D

`GfxMode3D(mode%)` → integer

Returns true if graphics mode number mode exists and supports 3D.

```basic
; Check that the first graphics mode can be used for 3D
If GfxMode3D(1) Then Print "Mode 1 supports 3D"
```

### GfxDriver3D

`GfxDriver3D(driver%)` → integer

Returns true if graphics driver number driver supports 3D (only driver 1 exists).

```basic
; Check that graphics driver 1 can be used for 3D
If GfxDriver3D(1) Then Print "Driver 1 supports 3D"
```

### WBuffer

`WBuffer enable%`

Accepted for compatibility; depth precision is handled by the renderer.

```basic
; Accepted for compatibility: depth precision is handled for you
Graphics3D 800, 600
WBuffer 1
```

### ActiveTextures

`ActiveTextures()` → integer

Returns the number of different textures currently applied to entities.

```basic
; Count the textures that are in use by entities
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
EntityTexture cube, tex
Print "Textures in use: " + ActiveTextures()
```

### GraphicsDepth

`GraphicsDepth()` → integer

Returns the colour depth of the display in bits (always 32).

```basic
; Print the colour depth of the display in bits
Graphics 640, 480
Print "Colour depth: " + GraphicsDepth()
```

### CountGfxModes

`CountGfxModes()` → integer

Returns the number of listed graphics modes (screen sizes).

```basic
; Print every listed screen size
For mode = 1 To CountGfxModes()
    Print GfxModeWidth(mode) + " x " + GfxModeHeight(mode)
Next
```

### CountGfxModes3D

`CountGfxModes3D()` → integer

Returns the number of listed 3D graphics modes.

```basic
; Count the screen sizes that can be used for 3D
Print "3D modes: " + CountGfxModes3D()
```

### GfxModeWidth

`GfxModeWidth(mode%)` → integer

Returns the width in pixels of graphics mode number mode, or 0 if it does not exist.

```basic
; Print the width of the first graphics mode
Print "Width: " + GfxModeWidth(1)
```

### GfxModeHeight

`GfxModeHeight(mode%)` → integer

Returns the height in pixels of graphics mode number mode, or 0 if it does not exist.

```basic
; Print the height of the first graphics mode
Print "Height: " + GfxModeHeight(1)
```

### GfxModeDepth

`GfxModeDepth(mode%)` → integer

Returns the colour depth of graphics mode number mode (32), or 0 if it does not exist.

```basic
; Print the colour depth of the first graphics mode
Print "Depth: " + GfxModeDepth(1)
```

### GfxModeExists

`GfxModeExists(width%, height%, [depth%=0])` → integer

Returns true if width by height is one of the listed graphics modes; any size works in a window.

```basic
; Check if a screen size is in the list of modes
If GfxModeExists(1024, 768) Then Print "1024x768 is listed" Else Print "1024x768 is not listed"
```

### GfxMode3DExists

`GfxMode3DExists(width%, height%, [depth%=0])` → integer

Returns true if width by height is one of the listed 3D graphics modes; any size works in a window.

```basic
; Check if a screen size is in the list of 3D modes
If GfxMode3DExists(1280, 720) Then Print "1280x720 works for 3D"
```

### CountGfxDrivers

`CountGfxDrivers()` → integer

Returns the number of graphics drivers (always 1).

```basic
; Print how many graphics drivers there are
Print "Drivers: " + CountGfxDrivers()
```

### GfxDriverName

`GfxDriverName(driver%)` → string

Returns the name of graphics driver number driver, or an empty string if it does not exist.

```basic
; Print the name of the first graphics driver
Print GfxDriverName(1)
```

### SetGfxDriver

`SetGfxDriver driver%`

Accepted for compatibility; there is only one graphics driver.

```basic
; Choose graphics driver 1 (the only one)
SetGfxDriver 1
```

### Windowed3D

`Windowed3D()` → integer

Returns true if 3D graphics can run in a window (always true).

```basic
; Check that 3D can run in a window
If Windowed3D() Then Print "3D can run in a window"
```

### GfxDriverCaps3D

`GfxDriverCaps3D()` → integer

Returns the 3D capability level of the graphics driver (always 110).

```basic
; Print the 3D capability level of the graphics driver
Print "Capability level: " + GfxDriverCaps3D()
```

### HWTexUnits

`HWTexUnits()` → integer

Returns the number of texture layers an entity can use at once.

```basic
; Print how many texture layers one entity can use at once
Print "Texture layers: " + HWTexUnits()
```

### HWMultiTex

`HWMultiTex enable%`

Accepted for compatibility; does nothing.

```basic
; Accepted for compatibility: does nothing
HWMultiTex 1
```

### Dither

`Dither enable%`

Accepted for compatibility; does nothing.

```basic
; Accepted for compatibility: does nothing
Dither 1
```

### TotalVidMem

`TotalVidMem()` → integer

Returns the total video memory in bytes; a fixed figure of 1 GB, since it cannot be read.

```basic
; Print the total video memory in megabytes
megabytes = TotalVidMem() / 1048576
Print "Video memory: " + megabytes + " MB"
```

### AvailVidMem

`AvailVidMem()` → integer

Returns the free video memory in bytes; a fixed figure of 1 GB, since it cannot be read.

```basic
; Print the free video memory in megabytes
megabytes = AvailVidMem() / 1048576
Print "Free video memory: " + megabytes + " MB"
```

### VWait

`VWait [frames%=1]`

Accepted for compatibility; Flip already waits for the display.

```basic
; Accepted for compatibility: Flip already waits for the display
Graphics 640, 480
VWait 1
```

### ScanLine

`ScanLine()` → integer

Returns the display scan line; always 0.

```basic
; The scan line is always 0
Print "Scan line: " + ScanLine()
```

### TrisRendered

`TrisRendered()` → integer

Returns the number of triangles drawn by the last RenderWorld.

```basic
; Show how many triangles the last RenderWorld drew
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
cube = CreateCube()
MoveEntity cube, 0, 0, 5
RenderWorld
Text 10, 10, "Triangles: " + TrisRendered()
Flip
```

### GraphicsWidth

`GraphicsWidth()` → integer

Returns the width of the graphics window in pixels.

```basic
; Draw a line across the whole width of the window
Graphics 640, 480
Line 0, 240, GraphicsWidth() - 1, 240
Flip
```

### GraphicsHeight

`GraphicsHeight()` → integer

Returns the height of the graphics window in pixels.

```basic
; Put some text halfway down the window
Graphics 640, 480
middle = GraphicsHeight() / 2
Text 10, middle, "Halfway down", 0, 1   ; last 1 = centred vertically on that point
Flip
```

### UpdateWorld

`UpdateWorld [elapsed_time#=1]`

Updates animations, collisions and physics for one frame; elapsed_time scales how far animations advance (default 1).

```basic
; The main loop of a 3D game
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
cube = CreateCube()
MoveEntity cube, 0, 0, 5
While Not KeyHit(1)   ; 1 = Escape
    TurnEntity cube, 0, 1, 0
    UpdateWorld   ; moves animations, collisions and physics on by one frame
    RenderWorld
    Flip
Wend
```

### RenderWorld

`RenderWorld [tween#=1]`

Renders the 3D world from every active camera, covering any 2D drawn before it. tween (0 to 1) blends each entity between its CaptureWorld position and its current one, for smooth fixed-rate loops; 1 draws the current positions.

```basic
; Draw the 3D world, then 2D text on top
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
cube = CreateCube()
MoveEntity cube, 0, 0, 5
RenderWorld   ; tween 1 (the default) draws the current positions
Text 10, 10, "Score: 0"
Flip
```

### Flip

`Flip [vwait%=1]`

Shows the finished frame on screen and ends the frame; vwait (default 1) waits for the vertical blank.

```basic
; Show the finished frame on screen
Graphics 640, 480
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    Text 10, 10, "Press Escape to quit"
    Flip   ; waits for the display, so the loop runs at screen speed
Wend
```

### WireFrame

`WireFrame enable%`

Draws all 3D geometry as wireframe when enable is 1, solid when 0.

```basic
; Toggle wireframe drawing with the space bar
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
cube = CreateCube()
MoveEntity cube, 0, 0, 5
showWires = 0
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If KeyHit(57) Then showWires = 1 - showWires   ; 57 = space
    WireFrame showWires   ; 1 = wireframe, 0 = solid
    TurnEntity cube, 0, 1, 0
    RenderWorld
    Flip
Wend
```

## Gamma and quality

### GraphicsQuality

`GraphicsQuality level%`

**(ext)** Sets the renderer quality preset: level 0 low, 1 medium, 2 high, 3 ultra (QUALITY_LOW to QUALITY_ULTRA). Use a low level for old graphics cards.

```basic
; Use a low quality preset for old graphics cards
Graphics3D 800, 600
GraphicsQuality QUALITY_LOW   ; 0 low, 1 medium, 2 high, 3 ultra
```

### GetGraphicsQuality

`GetGraphicsQuality()` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the current quality level set with GraphicsQuality (0-3).

```basic
; Read the current quality level
Graphics3D 800, 600
GraphicsQuality 2
Print "Quality level: " + GetGraphicsQuality()   ; 2 = high
```

### PostEffect

`PostEffect effect%, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Turns a rendering effect on (enable = 1) or off (enable = 0): EFFECT_BLOOM, EFFECT_SSAO, EFFECT_FXAA, EFFECT_SHADOWS or EFFECT_LIGHT_SCATTER.

```basic
; Turn on bloom, and turn off shadows
Graphics3D 800, 600
PostEffect EFFECT_BLOOM, 1
PostEffect EFFECT_SHADOWS, 0
```

### AntiAlias

`AntiAlias enable%`

Turns anti-aliasing (smoothing of jagged edges) on or off.

```basic
; Smooth out jagged edges
Graphics3D 800, 600
AntiAlias 1   ; 1 = on, 0 = off
```

### SetGamma

`SetGamma src_red%, src_green%, src_blue%, dest_red#, dest_green#, dest_blue#`

Sets the gamma ramp so that the colour levels src_red, src_green, src_blue (0-255) are shown as dest_red, dest_green, dest_blue. Call UpdateGamma to apply it.

```basic
; Make green brighter, then apply the change
Graphics 640, 480
SetGamma 128, 128, 128, 128, 160, 128   ; level 128 of each colour is shown as red 128, green 160, blue 128
UpdateGamma
```

### UpdateGamma

`UpdateGamma [calibrate%=0]`

Applies the gamma changes made with SetGamma; calibrate is accepted for compatibility.

```basic
; Apply changes made with SetGamma
Graphics 640, 480
SetGamma 255, 255, 255, 255, 255, 255
UpdateGamma
```

### GammaRed

`GammaRed(red%)` → float

Returns the output level the red level red (0-255) is mapped to by the gamma ramp.

```basic
; Read how the red level 128 is shown
Graphics 640, 480
Print "Red 128 is shown as " + GammaRed(128)
```

### GammaGreen

`GammaGreen(green%)` → float

Returns the output level the green level green (0-255) is mapped to by the gamma ramp.

```basic
; Read how the green level 128 is shown
Graphics 640, 480
Print "Green 128 is shown as " + GammaGreen(128)
```

### GammaBlue

`GammaBlue(blue%)` → float

Returns the output level the blue level blue (0-255) is mapped to by the gamma ramp.

```basic
; Read how the blue level 128 is shown
Graphics 640, 480
Print "Blue 128 is shown as " + GammaBlue(128)
```

## 2D drawing and text

### Locate

`Locate x%, y%`

Moves the text cursor used by Print and Write to x, y in pixels.

```basic
; Move the text cursor, then print there
Graphics 640, 480
Locate 100, 50   ; x, y in pixels
Print "Printed at 100, 50"
Flip
```

### Viewport

`Viewport x%, y%, width%, height%`

Restricts 2D drawing to the rectangle at x, y with size width by height in pixels.

```basic
; Only draw inside a 200 by 100 box
Graphics 640, 480
Viewport 50, 50, 200, 100
Color 255, 0, 0
Rect 0, 0, 640, 480   ; only the part inside the box is drawn
Viewport 0, 0, 640, 480   ; back to the whole window
Flip
```

### Text

`Text x%, y%, string$, [center_x%=0], [center_y%=0]`

Draws string at x, y in the current font and colour; center_x and center_y set to 1 centre it horizontally or vertically on that point.

```basic
; Draw left aligned text, and text centred on a point
Graphics 640, 480
Text 10, 10, "Top left"
Text 320, 240, "Centre", 1, 1   ; centred across and down
Flip
```

### Color

`Color red%, green%, blue%`

Sets the drawing colour for 2D commands, with red, green, blue from 0 to 255.

```basic
; Draw a red rectangle
Graphics 640, 480
Color 255, 0, 0   ; red, green, blue from 0 to 255
Rect 100, 100, 200, 50
Flip
```

### ColorRed

`ColorRed()` → integer

Returns the red part (0-255) of the current drawing colour.

```basic
; Read the red part of the drawing colour
Graphics 640, 480
Color 200, 100, 50
Print "Red: " + ColorRed()
```

### ColorGreen

`ColorGreen()` → integer

Returns the green part (0-255) of the current drawing colour.

```basic
; Read the green part of the drawing colour
Graphics 640, 480
Color 200, 100, 50
Print "Green: " + ColorGreen()
```

### ColorBlue

`ColorBlue()` → integer

Returns the blue part (0-255) of the current drawing colour.

```basic
; Read the blue part of the drawing colour
Graphics 640, 480
Color 200, 100, 50
Print "Blue: " + ColorBlue()
```

### ClsColor

`ClsColor red%, green%, blue%`

Sets the colour Cls clears to, with red, green, blue from 0 to 255.

```basic
; Clear the screen to dark blue every frame
Graphics 640, 480
ClsColor 0, 0, 80
Cls
Flip
```

### Cls

`Cls`

Clears the current buffer to the ClsColor colour.

```basic
; Clear the screen, then draw a fresh frame
Graphics 640, 480
Cls
Text 10, 10, "A fresh screen"
Flip
```

### Origin

`Origin x%, y%`

Sets the point x, y (pixels) that 2D drawing commands are measured from.

```basic
; Draw relative to the middle of the screen
Graphics 640, 480
Origin 320, 240
Oval -20, -20, 40, 40   ; centred on the screen
Flip
```

### Rect

`Rect x%, y%, width%, height%, [solid%=1]`

Draws a rectangle at x, y of width by height pixels, filled when solid is 1 (default) or as an outline when 0.

```basic
; A filled box and an outlined box
Graphics 640, 480
Rect 50, 50, 100, 60   ; filled
Rect 200, 50, 100, 60, 0   ; 0 = outline only
Flip
```

### Oval

`Oval x%, y%, width%, height%, [solid%=1]`

Draws an ellipse inside the box x, y, width, height (pixels), filled when solid is 1 (default) or as an outline when 0.

```basic
; A filled circle and an outlined ellipse
Graphics 640, 480
Oval 50, 50, 80, 80   ; filled
Oval 200, 50, 120, 60, 0   ; 0 = outline only
Flip
```

### Line

`Line x1%, y1%, x2%, y2%`

Draws a line from x1, y1 to x2, y2 in pixels.

```basic
; Draw a diagonal line across the window
Graphics 640, 480
Line 0, 0, 639, 479   ; from the top left to the bottom right
Flip
```

### Plot

`Plot x%, y%`

Draws a single pixel at x, y.

```basic
; Draw a single white pixel in the middle
Graphics 640, 480
Color 255, 255, 255
Plot 320, 240
Flip
```

## Fonts

### LoadFont

`LoadFont(fontname$, [height%=12], [bold%=0], [italic%=0], [underline%=0])` → integer

Loads a font fontname at height pixels, optionally bold, italic or underlined (1 = on). Returns the font handle, or 0 if it cannot be loaded.

```basic
; Load a large bold font and use it
Graphics 640, 480
bigFont = LoadFont("Arial", 32, 1)   ; name, height, bold
If bigFont <> 0 Then SetFont bigFont
Text 10, 10, "Big title"
Flip
```

### SetFont

`SetFont font%`

Makes font the font for Text and Print. Font 0 selects the built-in font (ext).

```basic
; Switch fonts, then go back to the built-in one
Graphics 640, 480
titleFont = LoadFont("Arial", 40)
SetFont titleFont
Text 10, 10, "Title"
SetFont 0   ; 0 = the built-in font
Text 10, 80, "Normal text"
Flip
```

### FreeFont

`FreeFont font%`

Frees font.

```basic
; Free a font when it is no longer needed
Graphics 640, 480
titleFont = LoadFont("Arial", 40)
SetFont 0
FreeFont titleFont
```

### FontWidth

`FontWidth()` → integer

Returns the width of the widest character in the current font, in pixels.

```basic
; Print the width of the widest character
Graphics 640, 480
Print "Widest character: " + FontWidth() + " pixels"
```

### FontHeight

`FontHeight()` → integer

Returns the height of the current font in pixels.

```basic
; Space lines of text by the font height
Graphics 640, 480
lineHeight = FontHeight()
For row = 0 To 4
    Text 10, 10 + row * lineHeight, "Line " + row
Next
Flip
```

### StringWidth

`StringWidth(string$)` → integer

Returns the width of string in pixels in the current font.

```basic
; Centre text by measuring it
Graphics 640, 480
message = "Game Over"
Text (GraphicsWidth() - StringWidth(message)) / 2, 200, message
Flip
```

### StringHeight

`StringHeight(string$)` → integer

Returns the height of string in pixels in the current font.

```basic
; Draw a box around some text
Graphics 640, 480
message = "Press Enter"
Rect 8, 8, StringWidth(message) + 4, StringHeight(message) + 4, 0
Text 10, 10, message
Flip
```

## 2D images

### SaveImage

`SaveImage(image%, file$, [frame%=0])` → integer

Saves frame of image to an image file; returns 1 on success, 0 on failure.

```basic
; Make an image, draw on it, and save it to a file
Graphics 640, 480
picture = CreateImage(64, 64)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(picture)
Color 255, 0, 0
Rect 0, 0, 64, 64
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
If SaveImage(picture, "red.png") Then Print "Saved" Else Print "Could not save"
```

### LoadImage

`LoadImage(file$)` → integer

Loads an image file for 2D drawing. Returns the image handle, or 0 on failure.

```basic
; Load a picture and draw it
Graphics 640, 480
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
If coin = 0 Then RuntimeError "coin.png not found"
DrawImage coin, 100, 100
Flip
```

### LoadAnimImage

`LoadAnimImage(file$, width%, height%, first%, count%)` → integer

Loads an image strip of frames, each width by height pixels, starting at frame first and loading count frames. Returns the image handle, or 0 on failure.

```basic
; Load a strip of 4 frames, each 32 by 32 pixels
Graphics 640, 480
explosion = LoadAnimImage("explosion.png", 32, 32, 0, 4)   ; width, height, first frame, number of frames
If explosion <> 0 Then DrawImage explosion, 100, 100, 2   ; draw frame 2
Flip
```

### FreeImage

`FreeImage image%`

Frees image.

```basic
; Free an image when it is no longer needed
Graphics 640, 480
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
FreeImage coin
```

### DrawImageEx

`DrawImageEx image%, x#, y#, [frame%=0], [rotation#=0], [scale_x#=1], [scale_y#=1], [alpha#=1]`

**(ext)** Draws frame of image at x,y rotated by rotation degrees clockwise and scaled by scale_x and scale_y about its handle, with alpha from 0 (invisible) to 1 (solid).

```basic
; Draw a ship turned 45 degrees, half size and half see-through
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
MidHandle ship
DrawImageEx ship, 320, 240, 0, 45, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5   ; frame, rotation, scale x, scale y, alpha
Flip
```

### DrawImage

`DrawImage image%, x%, y%, [frame%=0]`

Draws frame of image at x,y in pixels, with its mask colour transparent.

```basic
; Draw an image at the mouse position
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
MidHandle ship
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    DrawImage ship, MouseX(), MouseY()
    Flip
Wend
```

### DrawBlock

`DrawBlock image%, x%, y%, [frame%=0]`

Draws frame of image at x,y in pixels without transparency.

```basic
; Draw an image without transparency
Graphics 640, 480
tree = LoadImage("tree.png")
DrawBlock tree, 100, 100   ; the transparent parts are drawn solid
Flip
```

### DrawImageRect

`DrawImageRect image%, x%, y%, rect_x%, rect_y%, rect_width%, rect_height%, [frame%=0]`

Draws the part of image starting at rect_x,rect_y and rect_width by rect_height pixels at x,y, with its mask colour transparent.

```basic
; Draw only the top left 16 by 16 pixels of an image
Graphics 640, 480
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
DrawImageRect coin, 100, 100, 0, 0, 16, 16   ; x, y, then the part: left, top, width, height
Flip
```

### DrawBlockRect

`DrawBlockRect image%, x%, y%, rect_x%, rect_y%, rect_width%, rect_height%, [frame%=0]`

Draws the part of image starting at rect_x,rect_y and rect_width by rect_height pixels at x,y, without transparency.

```basic
; Draw only part of an image, without transparency
Graphics 640, 480
tree = LoadImage("tree.png")
DrawBlockRect tree, 100, 100, 0, 0, 32, 32
Flip
```

### CreateImage

`CreateImage(width%, height%, [frames%=1])` → integer

Creates a blank image width by height pixels with frames frames. Returns the image handle.

```basic
; Make a blank 3 frame image and fill the first frame
Graphics 640, 480
sprite = CreateImage(32, 32, 3)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(sprite, 0)
Color 0, 255, 0
Oval 0, 0, 32, 32
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
DrawImage sprite, 100, 100, 0
Flip
```

### CopyImage

`CopyImage(image%)` → integer

Returns a new image that is a copy of image.

```basic
; Make a copy, then resize the copy without changing the original
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
smallShip = CopyImage(ship)
ScaleImage smallShip, 0.5, 0.5
DrawImage ship, 100, 100
DrawImage smallShip, 300, 100
Flip
```

### GrabImage

`GrabImage image%, x%, y%, [frame%=0]`

Copies the area of the current buffer at x,y (minus the image handle) into frame of image.

```basic
; Take a picture of part of the screen
Graphics 640, 480
Color 255, 255, 0
Oval 0, 0, 50, 50
snapshot = CreateImage(50, 50)
GrabImage snapshot, 0, 0   ; copy the 50 by 50 area at 0, 0
Cls
DrawImage snapshot, 200, 200
Flip
```

### RotateImage

`RotateImage image%, angle#`

Rotates image by angle degrees.

```basic
; Turn an image a quarter turn
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
RotateImage ship, 90   ; degrees
DrawImage ship, 100, 100
Flip
```

### TFormImage

`TFormImage image%, a#, b#, c#, d#`

Transforms image by the 2x2 matrix a,b,c,d (for skewing, scaling and rotating).

```basic
; Skew an image sideways with a 2x2 matrix
Graphics 640, 480
tree = LoadImage("tree.png")
TFormImage tree, 1, 0, 0.5, 1   ; a, b, c, d
DrawImage tree, 100, 100
Flip
```

### TFormFilter

`TFormFilter enable%`

Turns smoothing on (1) or off (0) for RotateImage, ScaleImage, ResizeImage and TFormImage.

```basic
; Smooth images when they are rotated or scaled
Graphics 640, 480
TFormFilter 1   ; 1 = smooth, 0 = sharp
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
RotateImage ship, 30
DrawImage ship, 100, 100
Flip
```

### TileImage

`TileImage image%, [x%=0], [y%=0], [frame%=0]`

Fills the whole current buffer with frame of image, offset by x,y, with its mask colour transparent.

```basic
; Fill the whole screen with a repeating pattern
Graphics 640, 480
pattern = LoadImage("checker.png")
TileImage pattern
Flip
```

### TileBlock

`TileBlock image%, [x%=0], [y%=0], [frame%=0]`

Fills the whole current buffer with frame of image, offset by x,y, without transparency.

```basic
; Fill the screen with a pattern that scrolls
Graphics 640, 480
pattern = LoadImage("checker.png")
scroll = 0
While Not KeyHit(1)
    scroll = scroll + 1
    TileBlock pattern, scroll, 0   ; offset x, y
    Flip
Wend
```

### ImageRectCollide

`ImageRectCollide(image%, x%, y%, frame%, rect_x%, rect_y%, rect_width%, rect_height%)` → integer

Returns 1 if any solid (non-mask) pixel of frame of image drawn at x,y lies inside the rectangle at rect_x,rect_y of size rect_width by rect_height (pixel-accurate).

```basic
; Check if the ship touches a rectangle (a door)
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
; image, x, y, frame, then the rectangle: left, top, width, height
If ImageRectCollide(ship, 100, 100, 0, 90, 90, 40, 40) Then Print "Ship touches the door"
```

### ImageWidth

`ImageWidth(image%)` → integer

Returns the width of image in pixels.

```basic
; Centre an image using its width
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
DrawImage ship, (GraphicsWidth() - ImageWidth(ship)) / 2, 100
Flip
```

### ImageHeight

`ImageHeight(image%)` → integer

Returns the height of image in pixels.

```basic
; Put an image on the bottom edge
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
DrawImage ship, 100, GraphicsHeight() - ImageHeight(ship)
Flip
```

### ImageXHandle

`ImageXHandle(image%)` → integer

Returns the X offset of image's handle in pixels.

```basic
; Print where the handle is horizontally
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
MidHandle ship
Print "Handle x: " + ImageXHandle(ship)
```

### ImageYHandle

`ImageYHandle(image%)` → integer

Returns the Y offset of image's handle in pixels.

```basic
; Print where the handle is vertically
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
MidHandle ship
Print "Handle y: " + ImageYHandle(ship)
```

### ImageFrames

`ImageFrames(image%)` → integer

Returns the number of frames in image.

```basic
; Draw every frame of an animated image side by side
Graphics 640, 480
explosion = LoadAnimImage("explosion.png", 32, 32, 0, 4)
For frame = 0 To ImageFrames(explosion) - 1
    DrawImage explosion, 40 + frame * 40, 100, frame
Next
Flip
```

### HandleImage

`HandleImage image%, x%, y%`

Sets the handle (drawing origin) of image to x,y pixels from its top-left corner.

```basic
; Put the handle at the bottom middle so the image stands on a point
Graphics 640, 480
tree = LoadImage("tree.png")
HandleImage tree, ImageWidth(tree) / 2, ImageHeight(tree)
DrawImage tree, 320, 400   ; the bottom middle of the tree is at 320, 400
Flip
```

### MidHandle

`MidHandle image%`

Sets the handle of image to its centre.

```basic
; Put the handle at the centre of the image
Graphics 640, 480
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
MidHandle coin
DrawImage coin, 320, 240   ; the centre of the coin is at 320, 240
Flip
```

### AutoMidHandle

`AutoMidHandle enable%`

When enable is 1, images loaded or created afterwards get their handle at their centre; 0 puts it at the top-left corner.

```basic
; Make every image loaded after this centred
Graphics 640, 480
AutoMidHandle 1
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
ring = LoadImage("ring.png")
DrawImage coin, 200, 200
DrawImage ring, 300, 200
Flip
```

### MaskImage

`MaskImage image%, red%, green%, blue%`

Sets the colour of image (red, green, blue 0-255) that is drawn as transparent. The default is black.

```basic
; Make pure magenta the transparent colour
Graphics 640, 480
sprite = LoadImage("ship.png")
MaskImage sprite, 255, 0, 255   ; red, green, blue
DrawImage sprite, 100, 100
Flip
```

### ScaleImage

`ScaleImage image%, x_scale#, y_scale#`

Scales image by x_scale and y_scale.

```basic
; Make an image twice as wide and half as tall
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
ScaleImage ship, 2, 0.5   ; x scale, y scale
DrawImage ship, 100, 100
Flip
```

### ResizeImage

`ResizeImage image%, width#, height#`

Resizes image to width by height pixels.

```basic
; Make an image exactly 64 by 64 pixels
Graphics 640, 480
tree = LoadImage("tree.png")
ResizeImage tree, 64, 64
DrawImage tree, 100, 100
Flip
```

### RectsOverlap

`RectsOverlap(x1%, y1%, width1%, height1%, x2%, y2%, width2%, height2%)` → integer

Returns 1 if the rectangle x1,y1,width1,height1 overlaps the rectangle x2,y2,width2,height2.

```basic
; Check if the player box touches the enemy box
playerX = 100 : playerY = 100
enemyX = 120 : enemyY = 110
If RectsOverlap(playerX, playerY, 32, 32, enemyX, enemyY, 32, 32) Then Print "Hit!"
```

### ImagesOverlap

`ImagesOverlap(image1%, x1%, y1%, image2%, x2%, y2%)` → integer

Returns 1 if the bounding rectangles of image1 at x1,y1 and image2 at x2,y2 overlap (fast, not pixel-accurate).

```basic
; Quick check if the boxes of two images overlap
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
If ImagesOverlap(ship, 100, 100, coin, 110, 110) Then Print "Ship is near the coin"
```

### ImagesCollide

`ImagesCollide(image1%, x1%, y1%, frame1%, image2%, x2%, y2%, frame2%)` → integer

Returns 1 if frame1 of image1 at x1,y1 and frame2 of image2 at x2,y2 overlap, ignoring transparent pixels.

```basic
; Pixel accurate check, ignoring transparent parts
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
; image, x, y, frame for each of the two images
If ImagesCollide(ship, 100, 100, 0, coin, 110, 110, 0) Then Print "Ship touched the coin"
```

### ImageRectOverlap

`ImageRectOverlap(image%, x%, y%, rect_x%, rect_y%, rect_width%, rect_height%)` → integer

Returns 1 if the bounding rectangle of image at x,y overlaps the rectangle at rect_x,rect_y of size rect_width by rect_height.

```basic
; Check if the box of the ship overlaps a rectangle
Graphics 640, 480
ship = LoadImage("ship.png")
If ImageRectOverlap(ship, 100, 100, 90, 90, 40, 40) Then Print "Ship is in the zone"
```

### LoadImageAsync

`LoadImageAsync(file$, [width%=0], [height%=0], [first%=0], [count%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Starts loading an image file in the background and returns its handle at once (a single transparent pixel until it arrives); with width and height it is cut into frames like LoadAnimImage (first and count as there, count 0 = all). Use ImageLoaded or PendingLoads to know when it is ready.

```basic
; Start loading an image in the background, and wait for it
Graphics 640, 480
background = LoadImageAsync("checker.png")
While ImageLoaded(background) = 0
    Cls
    Text 10, 10, "Loading..."
    Flip
Wend
DrawImage background, 0, 0
Flip
```

### ImageLoaded

`ImageLoaded(image%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 when image has loaded (always for other images), 0 while it is loading in the background, -1 if loading failed.

```basic
; 1 = ready, 0 = still loading, -1 = failed
Graphics 640, 480
picture = LoadImageAsync("tree.png")
state = ImageLoaded(picture)
If state = 1 Then Print "Ready"
If state = 0 Then Print "Still loading"
If state = -1 Then Print "Failed"
```

### ImageName

`ImageName(image%)` → string

**(ext)** Returns the file image was loaded from, or "" for an image made in the program.

```basic
; Print the file an image came from
Graphics 640, 480
coin = LoadImage("coin.png")
Print ImageName(coin)
```

## Buffers and pixels

### GetColor

`GetColor x%, y%`

Sets the current drawing colour to the colour of the pixel at x, y in the current buffer. Pixels are read from image and texture buffers, not the screen.

```basic
; Read a pixel of an image into the drawing colour
Graphics 640, 480
picture = CreateImage(16, 16)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(picture)
Color 10, 200, 30
Plot 5, 5
GetColor 5, 5   ; the drawing colour is now the colour of that pixel
Print ColorRed() + ", " + ColorGreen() + ", " + ColorBlue()
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
```

### CopyPixel

`CopyPixel src_x%, src_y%, src_buffer%, dest_x%, dest_y%, [dest_buffer%=0]`

Copies the pixel at src_x, src_y in src_buffer to dest_x, dest_y in dest_buffer (default the current buffer). The source cannot be the screen.

```basic
; Copy one pixel from an image onto the screen
Graphics 640, 480
picture = CreateImage(16, 16)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(picture)
Color 255, 0, 0
Plot 3, 3
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
CopyPixel 3, 3, ImageBuffer(picture), 100, 100   ; source x, y, source buffer, then destination x, y
Flip
```

### CopyPixelFast

`CopyPixelFast src_x%, src_y%, src_buffer%, dest_x%, dest_y%, [dest_buffer%=0]`

Same as CopyPixel; copies one pixel from src_buffer to dest_buffer.

```basic
; Copy one pixel from one image to another
Graphics 640, 480
source = CreateImage(16, 16)
target = CreateImage(16, 16)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(source)
Color 0, 0, 255
Plot 2, 2
CopyPixelFast 2, 2, ImageBuffer(source), 8, 8, ImageBuffer(target)
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
```

### BufferWidth

`BufferWidth(buffer%)` → integer

Returns the width of buffer in pixels.

```basic
; Print the width of the back buffer
Graphics 640, 480
Print "Width: " + BufferWidth(BackBuffer())
```

### BufferHeight

`BufferHeight(buffer%)` → integer

Returns the height of buffer in pixels.

```basic
; Print the height of an image buffer
Graphics 640, 480
picture = CreateImage(64, 32)
Print "Height: " + BufferHeight(ImageBuffer(picture))
```

### SaveBuffer

`SaveBuffer(buffer%, file$)` → integer

Saves the contents of buffer (the screen too) to an image file; returns 1 on success, 0 on failure.

```basic
; Take a screenshot
Graphics 640, 480
Text 10, 10, "Screenshot test"
If SaveBuffer(BackBuffer(), "screenshot.png") Then Print "Saved" Else Print "Failed"
```

### LoadBuffer

`LoadBuffer(buffer%, file$)` → integer

Loads an image file into buffer, drawn from the top-left corner (stretched over the whole screen when buffer is the screen); returns 1 on success, 0 on failure.

```basic
; Load a picture onto the back buffer
Graphics 640, 480
If LoadBuffer(BackBuffer(), "checker.png") = 0 Then Print "Could not load"
Flip
```

### BackBuffer

`BackBuffer()` → integer

Returns the back buffer, the hidden screen that is drawn to and shown by Flip.

```basic
; Draw on the hidden screen, then show it with Flip
Graphics 640, 480
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
Text 10, 10, "Drawn on the back buffer"
Flip
```

### FrontBuffer

`FrontBuffer()` → integer

Returns the front buffer, the screen that is currently visible.

```basic
; Draw straight onto the visible screen
Graphics 640, 480
SetBuffer FrontBuffer()
Text 10, 10, "Drawn on the front buffer"
```

### GraphicsBuffer

`GraphicsBuffer()` → integer

Returns the buffer that drawing commands currently draw to.

```basic
; Remember the current buffer and restore it later
Graphics 640, 480
oldBuffer = GraphicsBuffer()
picture = CreateImage(32, 32)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(picture)
Rect 0, 0, 32, 32
SetBuffer oldBuffer
```

### ImageBuffer

`ImageBuffer(image%, [frame%=0])` → integer

Returns the buffer of frame of image, so it can be drawn to or read.

```basic
; Draw on an image, then draw the image on the screen
Graphics 640, 480
badge = CreateImage(64, 64)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(badge)
Color 255, 200, 0
Oval 0, 0, 64, 64
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
DrawImage badge, 100, 100
Flip
```

### TextureBuffer

`TextureBuffer(texture%, [frame%=0])` → integer

Returns the buffer of frame of texture, so it can be drawn to or read.

```basic
; Paint a red square onto a texture
Graphics3D 800, 600
tex = CreateTexture(64, 64)
SetBuffer TextureBuffer(tex)
Color 255, 0, 0
Rect 0, 0, 64, 64
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
```

### SetBuffer

`SetBuffer buffer%`

Makes buffer the one 2D drawing commands draw to.

```basic
; Draw into an image, then go back to the screen
Graphics 640, 480
picture = CreateImage(32, 32)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(picture)
Cls
SetBuffer BackBuffer()   ; drawing goes to the screen again
```

### LockBuffer

`LockBuffer [buffer%=0]`

Locks buffer (default the current one) for fast pixel access with ReadPixelFast and WritePixelFast.

```basic
; Lock a buffer while writing many pixels
Graphics 640, 480
LockBuffer BackBuffer()
For x = 0 To 99
    WritePixelFast x, 50, $FFFF0000
Next
UnlockBuffer BackBuffer()
Flip
```

### UnlockBuffer

`UnlockBuffer [buffer%=0]`

Unlocks a buffer locked with LockBuffer.

```basic
; Unlock the buffer when you have finished with fast pixels
Graphics 640, 480
LockBuffer
WritePixelFast 10, 10, $FF00FF00
UnlockBuffer
Flip
```

### ReadPixel

`ReadPixel(x%, y%, [buffer%=0])` → integer

Returns the colour of the pixel at x, y of buffer as an ARGB value.

```basic
; Read the colour of a pixel and split it into red, green and blue
Graphics 640, 480
picture = CreateImage(8, 8)
SetBuffer ImageBuffer(picture)
WritePixel 1, 1, $FF3366CC
argb = ReadPixel(1, 1)
red = (argb Shr 16) And 255
green = (argb Shr 8) And 255
blue = argb And 255
Print red + ", " + green + ", " + blue
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
```

### ReadPixelFast

`ReadPixelFast(x%, y%, [buffer%=0])` → integer

Returns the colour of the pixel at x, y of a locked buffer as an ARGB value.

```basic
; Read pixels of a locked buffer
Graphics 640, 480
picture = CreateImage(8, 8)
buffer = ImageBuffer(picture)
LockBuffer buffer
WritePixelFast 2, 2, $FFFFFFFF, buffer
argb = ReadPixelFast(2, 2, buffer)
UnlockBuffer buffer
Print "Pixel: " + Hex(argb)
```

### WritePixel

`WritePixel x%, y%, argb%, [buffer%=0]`

Sets the pixel at x, y of buffer to the ARGB colour argb.

```basic
; Set a pixel with an ARGB value (alpha, red, green, blue)
Graphics 640, 480
WritePixel 100, 100, $FFFF8000   ; solid orange
Flip
```

### WritePixelFast

`WritePixelFast x%, y%, argb%, [buffer%=0]`

Sets the pixel at x, y of a locked buffer to the ARGB colour argb.

```basic
; Fill a row of pixels quickly in a locked buffer
Graphics 640, 480
buffer = BackBuffer()
LockBuffer buffer
For x = 0 To 639
    WritePixelFast x, 240, $FF00FFFF, buffer
Next
UnlockBuffer buffer
Flip
```

### CopyRect

`CopyRect src_x%, src_y%, width%, height%, dest_x%, dest_y%, [src_buffer%=0], [dest_buffer%=0]`

Copies a width by height rectangle at src_x, src_y of src_buffer to dest_x, dest_y of dest_buffer (both default to the current buffer).

```basic
; Copy a 50 by 50 area from one place on the screen to another
Graphics 640, 480
Color 255, 0, 0
Rect 0, 0, 50, 50
CopyRect 0, 0, 50, 50, 200, 100   ; source x, y, width, height, then destination x, y
Flip
```

## Tile maps

### CreateTileMap

`CreateTileMap(image%, width%, height%)` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an empty tile map of width by height tiles, whose tiles are frames of the animation image image, and returns it.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
; a map 20 tiles wide and 15 tall, drawn with the frames of tiles
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
Print "Map size: " + TileMapWidth(map) + " x " + TileMapHeight(map)
```

### LoadTileMap

`LoadTileMap(file$, image%)` → integer

**(ext)** Loads a tile map saved with SaveTileMap from file and draws it with the animation image image (one frame per tile). Returns the tile map handle, or 0 if the file cannot be read.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
; load a map saved earlier; it returns 0 if the file cannot be read
map = LoadTileMap("level1.map", tiles)
If map = 0 Then Print "Could not load level1.map"
```

### SaveTileMap

`SaveTileMap(map%, file$)` → integer

**(ext)** Saves tile map map (its tiles, terrains and autotile rules) to a text file. Returns 1 on success, 0 on failure.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
SetTile map, 3, 4, 1
; returns 1 when the file was written, 0 on failure
If SaveTileMap(map, "level1.map") Then Print "Saved" Else Print "Save failed"
```

### FreeTileMap

`FreeTileMap map%`

**(ext)** Frees tile map map.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
; ... use the map ...
FreeTileMap map                ; finished with it
```

### SetTile

`SetTile map%, x%, y%, tile%`

**(ext)** Sets the cell at column x, row y of tile map map to frame number tile.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
; put frame 5 in the cell at column 3, row 4 (counting from 0)
SetTile map, 3, 4, 5
```

### GetTile

`GetTile(map%, x%, y%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the frame number in the cell at column x, row y of tile map map.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
SetTile map, 3, 4, 5
frame = GetTile(map, 3, 4)     ; the frame number stored in that cell
Print "Tile at 3,4 is frame " + frame
```

### TileMapWidth

`TileMapWidth(map%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the width of tile map map in cells.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
```
