```basic
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
columns = TileMapWidth(map)    ; width in cells
Print "The map has " + columns + " columns"
```

### TileMapHeight

`TileMapHeight(map%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the height of tile map map in cells.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
rows = TileMapHeight(map)      ; height in cells
Print "The map has " + rows + " rows"
```

### TileMapImage

`TileMapImage map%, image%`

**(ext)** Changes the animation image whose frames tile map map draws.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
; switch the whole map to a different tile set (for example a winter theme)
snowTiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles_snow.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
TileMapImage map, snowTiles
```

### TileMapTerrain

`TileMapTerrain map%, terrain%, first_frame%, [mode%=47]`

**(ext)** Sets up autotiling for terrain number terrain: its tiles start at frame first_frame of the map's image. mode is 16 (four neighbours, 16 frames) or 47 (eight neighbours, 47 "blob" frames, the default).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
; terrain 1 uses 16 frames starting at frame 0 (mode 16 = four neighbours)
TileMapTerrain map, 1, 0, 16
```

### AutoTile

`AutoTile map%, x%, y%, terrain%`

**(ext)** Paints the cell at column x, row y with terrain number terrain, choosing the frame that matches its neighbours and updating the cells around it.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
TileMapTerrain map, 1, 0, 16
; paint terrain 1 along a row of cells; the frames join up with their neighbours
For column = 2 To 8
    AutoTile map, column, 5, 1
Next
```

### GetTileTerrain

`GetTileTerrain(map%, x%, y%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the terrain number painted with AutoTile in the cell at column x, row y of tile map map.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
TileMapTerrain map, 1, 0, 16
AutoTile map, 4, 4, 1
terrain = GetTileTerrain(map, 4, 4)   ; 1 here; an unpainted cell gives 0
Print "Terrain at 4,4: " + terrain
```

### DrawTileMap

`DrawTileMap map%, [x%=0], [y%=0]`

**(ext)** Draws tile map map with its top-left corner at x,y (pixels, or world coordinates with Camera2D on). Only tiles on screen are drawn.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
Cls
DrawTileMap map, 0, 0          ; top-left corner of the map at 0,0
Flip
WaitKey
```

### TileMapCellX

`TileMapCellX(map%, x#)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the column of tile map map under x, given in pixels from the map's left edge.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
; the map is drawn at 0,0, so the mouse position is also the position on the map
column = TileMapCellX(map, MouseX())
Print "The mouse is over column " + column
```

### TileMapCellY

`TileMapCellY(map%, y#)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the row of tile map map under y, given in pixels from the map's top edge.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
row = TileMapCellY(map, MouseY())   ; the row under the mouse
Print "The mouse is over row " + row
```

### TileAt

`TileAt(map%, x#, y#)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the frame number of the tile under the point x,y, given in pixels from the map's top-left corner.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
; the frame of the tile under pixel 100,70 of the map
frame = TileAt(map, 100, 70)
If frame <> 0 Then Print "Something solid is here"
```

## 2D camera

### Camera2D

`Camera2D x#, y#, [zoom#=1], [rotation#=0]`

**(ext)** Turns on the 2D camera: 2D drawing then uses world coordinates, with world point x,y at the centre of the screen, zoom scaling the view (2 = twice as big) and rotation turning it clockwise in degrees.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
player = LoadImage("ship.png")
Cls
; world point 500,300 in the middle of the screen, zoomed in 2x, not rotated
Camera2D 500, 300, 2, 0
DrawImage player, 500, 300
Flip
WaitKey
```

### Camera2DEnable

`Camera2DEnable enable%`

**(ext)** Turns the 2D camera on (1) or off (0), for example to draw a HUD in screen coordinates on top.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Camera2D 500, 300, 2
Cls
Text 500, 300, "World text"      ; drawn in world coordinates
Camera2DEnable 0                 ; off: back to screen pixels for the HUD
Text 10, 10, "Score: 100"
Camera2DEnable 1                 ; on again for the next frame
Flip
WaitKey
```

### ScreenToWorld2DX

`ScreenToWorld2DX(x#, y#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the world X coordinate under screen point x,y for the 2D camera.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Camera2D 500, 300, 2
; where in the world is the mouse pointing?
worldX# = ScreenToWorld2DX(MouseX(), MouseY())
Print "Mouse is at world X " + worldX#
```

### ScreenToWorld2DY

`ScreenToWorld2DY(x#, y#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the world Y coordinate under screen point x,y for the 2D camera.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Camera2D 500, 300, 2
worldY# = ScreenToWorld2DY(MouseX(), MouseY())
Print "Mouse is at world Y " + worldY#
```

### WorldToScreen2DX

`WorldToScreen2DX(x#, y#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the screen X coordinate where world point x,y appears with the 2D camera.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Camera2D 500, 300, 2
; the screen X where the world point 520,300 appears
screenX# = WorldToScreen2DX(520, 300)
Print "Draw the label at screen X " + screenX#
```

### WorldToScreen2DY

`WorldToScreen2DY(x#, y#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the screen Y coordinate where world point x,y appears with the 2D camera.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Camera2D 500, 300, 2
screenY# = WorldToScreen2DY(520, 300)
Print "Draw the label at screen Y " + screenY#
```

## 2D animation

### CreateAnim2D

`CreateAnim2D(image%, [first%=0], [last%=-1], [fps#=10], [mode%=1])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an animation that plays frames first to last (-1 = last frame) of the animation image image at fps frames a second. mode is 1 loop, 2 ping-pong, 3 play once. Returns the animation handle.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
frames = LoadAnimImage("hero.png", 32, 32, 0, 8)
; frames 0 to 3 at 10 frames a second; mode 1 = loop (2 ping-pong, 3 play once)
walk = CreateAnim2D(frames, 0, 3, 10, 1)
```

### Anim2DPlay

`Anim2DPlay anim%, first%, last%, [fps#=10], [mode%=1]`

**(ext)** Starts anim playing frames first to last at fps frames a second, with mode 1 loop, 2 ping-pong or 3 play once.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
frames = LoadAnimImage("hero.png", 32, 32, 0, 8)
anim = CreateAnim2D(frames, 0, 3, 10, 1)
; switch to frames 4 to 7 at 12 frames a second, playing once (mode 3)
Anim2DPlay anim, 4, 7, 12, 3
```

### Anim2DPause

`Anim2DPause anim%, [pause%=1]`

**(ext)** Pauses anim (pause 1) or resumes it (pause 0).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
frames = LoadAnimImage("hero.png", 32, 32, 0, 8)
anim = CreateAnim2D(frames, 0, 3, 10)
Anim2DPause anim, 1            ; 1 = pause
Anim2DPause anim, 0            ; 0 = resume
```

### Anim2DFrame

`Anim2DFrame(anim%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the frame number anim is showing now.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
frames = LoadAnimImage("hero.png", 32, 32, 0, 8)
anim = CreateAnim2D(frames, 0, 3, 10)
current = Anim2DFrame(anim)    ; the frame number showing right now
Print "Showing frame " + current
```

### Anim2DPlaying

`Anim2DPlaying(anim%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 while anim is playing, 0 when paused or finished.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
frames = LoadAnimImage("hero.png", 32, 32, 0, 8)
anim = CreateAnim2D(frames, 4, 7, 12, 3)   ; plays once
If Anim2DPlaying(anim) = 0 Then Print "Finished" Else Print "Still playing"
```

### DrawAnim2D

`DrawAnim2D anim%, x#, y#`

**(ext)** Draws the current frame of anim at x,y like DrawImage.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
frames = LoadAnimImage("hero.png", 32, 32, 0, 8)
anim = CreateAnim2D(frames, 0, 3, 10)
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    DrawAnim2D anim, 320, 240      ; draws the current frame, like DrawImage
    Flip
Wend
```

### FreeAnim2D

`FreeAnim2D anim%`

**(ext)** Frees anim (the image is not freed).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
frames = LoadAnimImage("hero.png", 32, 32, 0, 8)
anim = CreateAnim2D(frames, 0, 3, 10)
FreeAnim2D anim                ; frees the animation, not the image
FreeImage frames
```

## 2D physics

### Physics2DGravity

`Physics2DGravity x#, y#`

**(ext)** Sets 2D physics gravity to x,y in pixels per second squared (Y down) and starts the 2D physics world.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
; gravity of 600 pixels per second squared, pulling down (Y down)
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
```

### Physics2DScale

`Physics2DScale pixels_per_metre#`

**(ext)** Sets how many pixels make one physics metre (default 50). Set it before creating bodies.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DScale 100             ; 100 pixels = 1 physics metre (set before making bodies)
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
```

### CreateBody2D

`CreateBody2D([type%=0], [x#=0], [y#=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a 2D physics body at x,y pixels. type is 0 dynamic, 1 static or 2 kinematic. Returns the body handle; give it shapes with Body2DBox and similar commands.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
; type 0 = dynamic, 1 = static, 2 = kinematic; starts at 320,100
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16
```

### FreeBody2D

`FreeBody2D body%`

**(ext)** Frees 2D body body and its shapes.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
crate = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DBox crate, 32, 32
FreeBody2D crate               ; the body and its shapes are gone
```

### Body2DBox

`Body2DBox body%, width#, height#, [offset_x#=0], [offset_y#=0]`

**(ext)** Adds a box shape width by height pixels to body, centred at offset_x,offset_y from the body.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ground = CreateBody2D(1, 320, 460)    ; static
Body2DBox ground, 640, 40             ; 640 wide, 40 tall
crate = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DBox crate, 32, 32, 0, 0         ; last two: offset from the body centre
```

### Body2DCircle

`Body2DCircle body%, radius#, [offset_x#=0], [offset_y#=0]`

**(ext)** Adds a circle shape of radius pixels to body, centred at offset_x,offset_y from the body.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16          ; radius 16 pixels
```

### Body2DCapsule

`Body2DCapsule body%, width#, height#, [offset_x#=0], [offset_y#=0]`

**(ext)** Adds an upright capsule (pill) shape width by height pixels to body, centred at offset_x,offset_y; good for characters.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40     ; 20 wide, 40 tall pill shape
Body2DFixedRotation hero, 1    ; stay upright
```

### Body2DPolygon

`Body2DPolygon body%, points$`

**(ext)** Adds a polygon shape to body from points, a string "x1,y1,x2,y2,..." of at least three points in pixels relative to the body.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ramp = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 400)
; three corners (x,y pairs) relative to the body: a triangle
Body2DPolygon ramp, "-60,20,60,20,60,-20"
```

### Body2DSegment

`Body2DSegment body%, x1#, y1#, x2#, y2#`

**(ext)** Adds a line segment shape from x1,y1 to x2,y2 (pixels, relative to the body) to body.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wall = CreateBody2D(1, 0, 0)
; a thin line from 0,450 to 640,450 acting as the ground
Body2DSegment wall, 0, 450, 640, 450
```

### TileMapBody2D

`TileMapBody2D(map%, [x#=0], [y#=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a static body with boxes for the solid (non-empty) tiles of tile map map, with the map's top-left corner at x,y. Returns the body handle.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
tiles = LoadAnimImage("tiles.png", 32, 32, 0, 16)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
For column = 0 To 19
    SetTile map, column, 14, 1       ; a floor of solid tiles
Next
; one static body with a box for every non-empty tile, map corner at 0,0
level = TileMapBody2D(map, 0, 0)
```

### Body2DFriction

`Body2DFriction body%, friction#`

**(ext)** Sets the friction of body's shapes (0 = slippery).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
crate = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DBox crate, 32, 32
Body2DFriction crate, 0.1      ; low friction: slippery like ice
```

### Body2DBounce

`Body2DBounce body%, bounce#`

**(ext)** Sets the bounciness (restitution) of body's shapes (0 = no bounce, 1 = full bounce).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16
Body2DBounce ball, 0.8         ; 0 = no bounce, 1 = full bounce
```

### Body2DDensity

`Body2DDensity body%, density#`

**(ext)** Sets the density of body's shapes, which sets its mass.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
crate = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DBox crate, 32, 32
Body2DDensity crate, 5         ; heavier than the default
```

### Body2DSensor

`Body2DSensor body%, enable%`

**(ext)** Makes body's shapes sensors (enable 1) that detect overlaps with Body2DTouching but do not collide.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
coin = CreateBody2D(1, 400, 300)
Body2DCircle coin, 12
Body2DSensor coin, 1           ; detects overlaps but does not block
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
If Body2DTouching(hero, coin) Then Print "Picked up the coin"
```

### Body2DFixedRotation

`Body2DFixedRotation body%, enable%`

**(ext)** Stops body from rotating (enable 1) or lets it rotate again (0).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
Body2DFixedRotation hero, 1    ; 1 = never rotates, 0 = rotates again
```

### Body2DGravityScale

`Body2DGravityScale body%, scale#`

**(ext)** Multiplies the gravity on body by scale (0 = no gravity).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
balloon = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 400)
Body2DCircle balloon, 20
Body2DGravityScale balloon, -0.2   ; negative: floats upwards
```

### Body2DDamping

`Body2DDamping body%, linear#, [angular#=0]`

**(ext)** Sets the linear and angular damping of body, which slow its movement and spin.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16
Body2DDamping ball, 0.5, 1.0   ; slows movement (0.5) and spin (1.0)
```

### PositionBody2D

`PositionBody2D body%, x#, y#`

**(ext)** Moves body to x,y in pixels.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
PositionBody2D hero, 100, 300  ; teleport back to the start (pixels)
```

### RotateBody2D

`RotateBody2D body%, angle#`

**(ext)** Sets body's angle to angle degrees clockwise.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
crate = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DBox crate, 32, 32
RotateBody2D crate, 45         ; tilted 45 degrees clockwise
```

### Body2DX

`Body2DX(body%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X position of body in pixels.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16
x# = Body2DX(ball)             ; position in pixels
Print "Ball X: " + x#
```

### Body2DY

`Body2DY(body%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y position of body in pixels.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16
y# = Body2DY(ball)
Print "Ball Y: " + y#
```

### Body2DAngle

`Body2DAngle(body%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the angle of body in degrees clockwise.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
crate = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DBox crate, 32, 32
angle# = Body2DAngle(crate)    ; degrees clockwise
Print "Crate angle: " + angle#
```

### SetVelocity2D

`SetVelocity2D body%, x#, y#`

**(ext)** Sets the velocity of body to x,y pixels per second.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
; run right at 200 pixels per second, keeping the current fall speed
SetVelocity2D hero, 200, Body2DVY(hero)
```

### Body2DVX

`Body2DVX(body%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X velocity of body in pixels per second.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
SetVelocity2D hero, 200, 0
Print "Speed sideways: " + Body2DVX(hero)    ; pixels per second
```

### Body2DVY

`Body2DVY(body%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y velocity of body in pixels per second.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
; positive = falling, negative = rising (Y is down)
If Body2DVY(hero) > 0 Then Print "Falling"
```

### SetSpin2D

`SetSpin2D body%, degrees_per_second#`

**(ext)** Sets the spin of body in degrees per second (clockwise).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wheel = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 240)
Body2DCircle wheel, 30
SetSpin2D wheel, 90            ; a quarter turn per second, clockwise
```

### Body2DSpin

`Body2DSpin(body%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the spin of body in degrees per second.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wheel = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 240)
Body2DCircle wheel, 30
SetSpin2D wheel, 90
Print "Spinning at " + Body2DSpin(wheel) + " degrees per second"
```

### ApplyForce2D

`ApplyForce2D body%, x#, y#`

**(ext)** Pushes body with force x,y (pixel units) for this step.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
rocket = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 400)
Body2DBox rocket, 20, 40
; call every frame while the engine is on: pushes up for this step
ApplyForce2D rocket, 0, -2000
```

### ApplyImpulse2D

`ApplyImpulse2D body%, x#, y#`

**(ext)** Gives body an instant kick x,y (pixel units), like a jump.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
; an instant upward kick, like a jump (space bar)
If KeyHit(57) Then ApplyImpulse2D hero, 0, -300
```

### ApplyTorque2D

`ApplyTorque2D body%, torque#`

**(ext)** Applies a turning force torque to body for this step.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wheel = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 240)
Body2DCircle wheel, 30
ApplyTorque2D wheel, 5000      ; turning force for this step
```

### Body2DTouching

`Body2DTouching(body%, [other%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if body is touching or overlapping body other, or any body when other is 0.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
ground = CreateBody2D(1, 320, 460)
Body2DBox ground, 640, 40
If Body2DTouching(hero, ground) Then Print "On the ground"
If Body2DTouching(hero) Then Print "Touching something"   ; other = 0 means any body
```

### CountContacts2D

`CountContacts2D(body%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many bodies are touching body.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
Print "Touching " + CountContacts2D(hero) + " bodies"
```

### ContactBody2D

`ContactBody2D(body%, index%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the body touching body with number index (1 to CountContacts2D), or 0.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
hero = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 300)
Body2DCapsule hero, 20, 40
; go through every body the hero is touching (numbered from 1)
For index = 1 To CountContacts2D(hero)
    other = ContactBody2D(hero, index)
    Print "Touching body " + other
Next
```

### RayCast2D

`RayCast2D(x1#, y1#, x2#, y2#)` → integer

**(ext)** Casts a ray from x1,y1 to x2,y2 and returns the first body it hits, or 0. Read the hit point and normal with RayCast2DX/Y/NX/NY.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wall = CreateBody2D(1, 400, 300)
Body2DBox wall, 20, 200
; fire a ray from 100,300 to 600,300 and see what it hits first
hit = RayCast2D(100, 300, 600, 300)
If hit <> 0 Then Print "Hit body " + hit Else Print "Nothing in the way"
```

### RayCast2DX

`RayCast2DX()` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X coordinate in pixels of the point hit by the last RayCast2D.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wall = CreateBody2D(1, 400, 300)
Body2DBox wall, 20, 200
If RayCast2D(100, 300, 600, 300) <> 0 Then Print "Hit at X " + RayCast2DX()
```

### RayCast2DY

`RayCast2DY()` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y coordinate in pixels of the point hit by the last RayCast2D.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wall = CreateBody2D(1, 400, 300)
Body2DBox wall, 20, 200
If RayCast2D(100, 300, 600, 300) <> 0 Then Print "Hit at Y " + RayCast2DY()
```

### RayCast2DNX

`RayCast2DNX()` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X component of the surface normal at the point hit by the last RayCast2D.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wall = CreateBody2D(1, 400, 300)
Body2DBox wall, 20, 200
; the normal points away from the surface that was hit
If RayCast2D(100, 300, 600, 300) <> 0 Then Print "Normal X " + RayCast2DNX()
```

### RayCast2DNY

`RayCast2DNY()` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y component of the surface normal at the point hit by the last RayCast2D.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
wall = CreateBody2D(1, 400, 300)
Body2DBox wall, 20, 200
If RayCast2D(100, 300, 600, 300) <> 0 Then Print "Normal Y " + RayCast2DNY()
```

### DrawBodies2D

`DrawBodies2D`

**(ext)** Draws the outlines of all 2D bodies' shapes in the current colour, for debugging.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16
Color 0, 255, 0
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    DrawBodies2D                ; outlines of every shape, for debugging
    Flip
Wend
```

### DrawBody2D

`DrawBody2D body%, image%, [frame%=0]`

**(ext)** Draws frame of image at body's position and angle.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Physics2DGravity 0, 600
ball = CreateBody2D(0, 320, 100)
Body2DCircle ball, 16
coin = LoadAnimImage("coin.png", 32, 32, 0, 1)
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    DrawBody2D ball, coin, 0    ; frame 0 drawn at the body's position and angle
    Flip
Wend
```

## UI widgets

### UIButton

`UIButton(x#, y#, width#, height#, label$)` → integer

**(ext)** Draws a button at x,y of size width by height with label. Call it every frame; returns 1 in the frame it is clicked.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    ; call every frame; returns 1 in the frame the button is clicked
    If UIButton(20, 20, 120, 30, "Start game") Then Print "Starting..."
    Flip
Wend
```

### UICheckBox

`UICheckBox(x#, y#, label$, checked%)` → integer

**(ext)** Draws a check box at x,y with label, ticked when checked is 1. Call it every frame; returns the new checked state (toggled when clicked).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
fullscreen = 0
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    ; pass the current state in, get the new state back
    fullscreen = UICheckBox(20, 20, "Fullscreen", fullscreen)
    Flip
Wend
```

### UISlider

`UISlider(x#, y#, width#, value#, [min#=0], [max#=1])` → float

**(ext)** Draws a horizontal slider at x,y, width pixels wide, showing value between min and max. Call it every frame; returns the new value, changed while the user drags it.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
volume# = 0.5
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    volume# = UISlider(20, 20, 200, volume#, 0, 1)   ; 200 wide, range 0 to 1
    Text 240, 20, "Volume: " + volume#
    Flip
Wend
```

### UITextBox

`UITextBox(x#, y#, width#, text$)` → string

**(ext)** Draws an editable text box at x,y, width pixels wide, containing text. Click it to give it keyboard focus; returns the edited text.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
playerName = "Player"
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    ; click the box to type; the edited text is returned
    playerName = UITextBox(20, 20, 200, playerName)
    Flip
Wend
```

### UIPanel

`UIPanel x#, y#, width#, height#, [title$=""]`

**(ext)** Draws a panel (background box) at x,y of size width by height, with an optional title.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Cls
UIPanel 20, 20, 240, 120, "Settings"   ; the title is optional
UILabel 36, 56, "Sound and graphics"
Flip
WaitKey
```

### UILabel

`UILabel x#, y#, text$`

**(ext)** Draws text at x,y in the UI style.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
Cls
UILabel 20, 20, "Press the button to begin"
Flip
WaitKey
```

### UIHover

`UIHover()` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if the mouse was over a UI widget (or one is being dragged), so the game can ignore that click.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    clicked = UIButton(20, 20, 120, 30, "Menu")
    ; only treat the click as a shot when the mouse is not over the UI
    If MouseHit(1) And UIHover() = 0 Then Print "Fire!"
    Flip
Wend
```

### UITheme

`UITheme red%, green%, blue%`

**(ext)** Sets the UI accent colour (red, green, blue 0-255).

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
UITheme 255, 128, 0              ; orange accent colour (red, green, blue)
Cls
clicked = UIButton(20, 20, 120, 30, "Orange")
Flip
WaitKey
```

## Movies

### OpenMovie

`OpenMovie(file$)` → integer

Opens a movie for playback and starts playing it once in real time. Plays animated GIF, Motion-JPEG AVI or a folder of numbered images (30 frames a second); sound tracks are not played. Returns the movie handle, or 0 on failure.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
movie = OpenMovie("intro.gif")    ; starts playing straight away
If movie = 0 Then Print "Could not open the movie"
```

### CloseMovie

`CloseMovie movie%`

Closes movie movie and frees it.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
movie = OpenMovie("intro.gif")
; ... play it ...
CloseMovie movie
```

### DrawMovie

`DrawMovie(movie%, [x%=0], [y%=0], [width%=-1], [height%=-1])` → integer

Draws the current frame of movie at x,y in pixels, scaled to width by height (-1 keeps the movie's own size). Returns 1 while the movie is still playing.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
movie = OpenMovie("intro.gif")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    Cls
    ; draw at 0,0 scaled to 640 x 480; returns 0 once it has finished
    If DrawMovie(movie, 0, 0, 640, 480) = 0 Then Exit
    Flip
Wend
CloseMovie movie
```

### MovieWidth

`MovieWidth(movie%)` → integer

Returns the width of movie in pixels.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
movie = OpenMovie("intro.gif")
Print "The movie is " + MovieWidth(movie) + " pixels wide"
```

### MovieHeight

`MovieHeight(movie%)` → integer

Returns the height of movie in pixels.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
movie = OpenMovie("intro.gif")
Print "The movie is " + MovieHeight(movie) + " pixels tall"
```

### MoviePlaying

`MoviePlaying(movie%)` → integer

Returns 1 while movie is still playing, 0 when it has finished.

```basic
Graphics 640, 480
movie = OpenMovie("intro.gif")
While MoviePlaying(movie)
    Cls
    DrawMovie movie, 0, 0
    Flip
Wend
CloseMovie movie
```

## Keyboard

### WaitKey

`WaitKey()` → integer

Waits until a key is pressed and returns its key code.

```basic
Print "Press any key to start..."
code = WaitKey()          ; pauses here until a key is pressed
Print "You pressed key code " + code
```

### KeyDown

`KeyDown(key%)` → integer

Returns 1 while key (a scan code) is held down.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
x = 0
While Not KeyDown(KEY_ESCAPE)     ; keep going until Escape is held
    If KeyDown(KEY_RIGHT) Then x = x + 1   ; true every frame the key is held
    If KeyDown(KEY_LEFT) Then x = x - 1
    Flip
Wend
```

### KeyHit

`KeyHit(key%)` → integer

Returns how many times key (a scan code) was pressed since the last call.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
jumps = 0
While Not KeyDown(KEY_ESCAPE)
    ; KeyHit counts presses since the last call, so holding the key counts once
    jumps = jumps + KeyHit(KEY_SPACE)
    Flip
Wend
Print "You jumped " + jumps + " times"
```

### GetKey

`GetKey()` → integer

Returns the character code of the next key typed, or 0 if none.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
typed = ""
While Not KeyDown(KEY_ENTER)
    letter = GetKey()                       ; 0 when nothing was typed
    If letter >= 32 Then typed = typed + Chr(letter)   ; keep printable characters only
    Flip
Wend
Print "You typed: " + typed
```

### FlushKeys

`FlushKeys`

Clears all keyboard presses and typed characters.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
Print "Press a key to continue"
FlushKeys                  ; ignore keys pressed before this point
code = WaitKey()
```

## Mouse

### HidePointer

`HidePointer`

Hides the mouse pointer over the window.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
HidePointer                ; the mouse arrow is no longer drawn over the window
Flip
```

### ShowPointer

`ShowPointer`

Shows the mouse pointer again after HidePointer.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
HidePointer
Flip
ShowPointer                ; bring the arrow back, for example for a menu
Flip
```

### MoveMouse

`MoveMouse x%, y%`

Moves the mouse pointer to x, y in pixels within the window.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
MoveMouse 320, 240         ; put the pointer in the middle of the window
Flip
```

### CaptureMouse

`CaptureMouse enable%`

**(ext)** Locks the mouse pointer to the window and hides it when enable is 1, for mouse-look; 0 releases and shows it again. MouseXSpeed and MouseYSpeed keep working while captured.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
CaptureMouse 1             ; lock and hide the pointer for mouse-look
Flip
turn = MouseXSpeed()       ; still reports movement while captured
CaptureMouse 0             ; release the pointer again
```

### MouseZSpeed

`MouseZSpeed()` → integer

Returns how far the mouse wheel has moved since the last call to MouseZSpeed.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
wheel = MouseZSpeed()      ; positive when the wheel is rolled forward
MoveEntity camera, 0, 0, wheel   ; zoom in or out with the wheel
Flip
```

### GetMouse

`GetMouse()` → integer

Returns the number of the mouse button clicked since the last check (1 left, 2 right, 3 middle), or 0 if none.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
button = GetMouse()        ; 0 when no button was clicked
If button = 1 Then Print "Left click"
If button = 2 Then Print "Right click"
If button = 3 Then Print "Middle click"
Flip
```

### WaitMouse

`WaitMouse()` → integer

Waits until a mouse button is clicked and returns its number (1 left, 2 right, 3 middle).

```basic
Print "Click a mouse button..."
button = WaitMouse()       ; 1 left, 2 right, 3 middle
Print "You clicked button " + button
```

### MouseX

`MouseX()` → integer

Returns the mouse X position in pixels.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
cursorX = MouseX()         ; pixels from the left edge of the window
cursorY = MouseY()
Print "Pointer at " + cursorX + ", " + cursorY
Flip
```

### MouseY

`MouseY()` → integer

Returns the mouse Y position in pixels.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
If MouseY() < 50 Then Print "The pointer is near the top of the window"
Flip
```

### MouseZ

`MouseZ()` → integer

Returns the mouse wheel position.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
zoom = MouseZ()            ; total wheel position, starts at 0
Print "Wheel position: " + zoom
Flip
```

### MouseDown

`MouseDown(button%)` → integer

Returns 1 while mouse button (1 left, 2 right, 3 middle) is held down.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
While Not KeyDown(KEY_ESCAPE)
    If MouseDown(1) Then Print "Left button is held"    ; 1 left, 2 right, 3 middle
    Flip
Wend
```

### MouseHit

`MouseHit(button%)` → integer

Returns how many times mouse button (1 left, 2 right, 3 middle) was clicked since the last call.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
shots = 0
While Not KeyDown(KEY_ESCAPE)
    shots = shots + MouseHit(1)    ; one count per click, however long it is held
    Flip
Wend
Print "You fired " + shots + " shots"
```

### MouseXSpeed

`MouseXSpeed()` → integer

Returns how far the mouse moved horizontally since the last call, in pixels.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
turn = MouseXSpeed()               ; pixels moved sideways since the last call
TurnEntity camera, 0, -turn * 0.2, 0   ; turn the camera with the mouse
Flip
```

### MouseYSpeed

`MouseYSpeed()` → integer

Returns how far the mouse moved vertically since the last call, in pixels.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
look = MouseYSpeed()               ; pixels moved up or down since the last call
TurnEntity camera, look * 0.2, 0, 0
Flip
```

### FlushMouse

`FlushMouse`

Clears all mouse button presses.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
FlushMouse                 ; forget clicks made while a menu was open
clicks = MouseHit(1)       ; now 0
Flip
```

## Joysticks and gamepads

### EnableDirectInput

`EnableDirectInput enable%`

Accepted for compatibility; enable (1 or 0) is remembered but input always comes straight from the operating system.

```basic
EnableDirectInput 1        ; remembered, but input always comes from the system
Print DirectInputEnabled()
```

### DirectInputEnabled

`DirectInputEnabled()` → integer

Returns the last value set with EnableDirectInput (1 or 0).

```basic
EnableDirectInput 0
If DirectInputEnabled() = 0 Then Print "DirectInput is off"
```

### JoyType

`JoyType([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the type of controller on port: 0 if none is connected, 2 (analog) for a connected gamepad. Port 0 is the first controller.

```basic
; 0 = no controller, 2 = gamepad
If JoyType(0) = 0 Then Print "No controller on port 0" Else Print "Gamepad found"
```

### CountJoys

`CountJoys()` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the number of connected joysticks and gamepads.

```basic
pads = CountJoys()         ; how many controllers are connected
Print "Controllers connected: " + pads
```

### JoyDown

`JoyDown(button%, [port%=0])` → integer

Returns 1 if button is held down on the controller on port, otherwise 0.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
While Not KeyDown(KEY_ESCAPE)
    If JoyDown(1, 0) Then Print "Button 1 held on the first controller"
    Flip
Wend
```

### JoyHit

`JoyHit(button%, [port%=0])` → integer

Returns how many times button has been pressed on the controller on port since the last check.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
jumps = 0
While Not KeyDown(KEY_ESCAPE)
    jumps = jumps + JoyHit(1)      ; port is optional and defaults to 0
    Flip
Wend
```

### GetJoy

`GetJoy([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the number of a button pressed on the controller on port since the last check, or 0 if none.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
button = GetJoy(0)         ; 0 when no button was pressed
If button > 0 Then Print "Button " + button + " was pressed"
Flip
```

### WaitJoy

`WaitJoy([port%=0])` → integer

Waits until a button is pressed on the controller on port and returns its number.

```basic
Print "Press a button on the gamepad..."
button = WaitJoy(0)        ; waits for a button on the first controller
Print "You pressed button " + button
```

### FlushJoy

`FlushJoy`

Forgets all pending joystick button presses.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
FlushJoy                   ; forget button presses from before this point
presses = JoyHit(1)        ; now 0
Flip
```

### JoyX

`JoyX([port%=0])` → float

Returns the left stick's horizontal position from -1 (left) to 1 (right) on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
player = CreateCube()
camera = CreateCamera()
MoveEntity camera, 0, 0, -5
MoveEntity player, JoyX(0) * 0.1, 0, 0   ; -1 (left) to 1 (right)
Flip
```

### JoyY

`JoyY([port%=0])` → float

Returns the left stick's vertical position from -1 to 1 on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
player = CreateCube()
camera = CreateCamera()
MoveEntity camera, 0, 0, -5
MoveEntity player, 0, 0, -JoyY(0) * 0.1  ; pushing the stick forward gives -1
Flip
```

### JoyZ

`JoyZ([port%=0])` → float

Returns the trigger axis from -1 to 1 (right trigger minus left trigger) on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
throttle = JoyZ(0)         ; right trigger minus left trigger, -1 to 1
Print "Throttle: " + throttle
Flip
```

### JoyU

`JoyU([port%=0])` → float

Returns the right stick's horizontal position from -1 to 1 on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
lookSide = JoyU(0)         ; right stick, left (-1) to right (1)
Print "Right stick sideways: " + lookSide
Flip
```

### JoyV

`JoyV([port%=0])` → float

Returns the right stick's vertical position from -1 to 1 on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
lookUp = JoyV(0)           ; right stick, vertical position -1 to 1
Print "Right stick vertical: " + lookUp
Flip
```

### JoyPitch

`JoyPitch([port%=0])` → float

Returns the pitch axis in degrees from -180 to 180 (taken from the right stick's vertical position) on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
RotateEntity camera, JoyPitch(0) * 0.5, 0, 0   ; -180 to 180 degrees
Flip
```

### JoyYaw

`JoyYaw([port%=0])` → float

Returns the yaw axis in degrees from -180 to 180 (taken from the right stick's horizontal position) on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
RotateEntity camera, 0, JoyYaw(0) * 0.5, 0     ; -180 to 180 degrees
Flip
```

### JoyRoll

`JoyRoll([port%=0])` → float

Returns the roll axis in degrees from -180 to 180 (taken from the triggers) on the controller on port.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
RotateEntity camera, 0, 0, JoyRoll(0) * 0.5    ; -180 to 180 degrees
Flip
```

### JoyHat

`JoyHat([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the direction of the d-pad (hat) in degrees, 0 for up going clockwise in steps of 45, or -1 if it is not pressed.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
hat = JoyHat(0)            ; 0 = up, 90 = right, 180 = down, 270 = left, -1 = not pressed
If hat = 0 Then Print "D-pad up"
If hat = 90 Then Print "D-pad right"
If hat = -1 Then Print "D-pad released"
Flip
```

### JoyXDir

`JoyXDir([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the direction of the joystick's X axis as -1, 0 or 1.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
If JoyXDir(0) = -1 Then Print "Pushing left"    ; -1, 0 or 1
If JoyXDir(0) = 1 Then Print "Pushing right"
Flip
```

### JoyYDir

`JoyYDir([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the direction of the joystick's Y axis as -1, 0 or 1.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
If JoyYDir(0) = -1 Then Print "Pushing the stick forward"   ; -1, 0 or 1
If JoyYDir(0) = 1 Then Print "Pulling the stick back"
Flip
```

### JoyZDir

`JoyZDir([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the direction of the joystick's Z axis as -1, 0 or 1.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
If JoyZDir(0) = 1 Then Print "Right trigger is pulled more than the left"
Flip
```

### JoyUDir

`JoyUDir([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the direction of the joystick's U axis as -1, 0 or 1.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
If JoyUDir(0) <> 0 Then Print "Right stick is pushed sideways"
Flip
```

### JoyVDir

`JoyVDir([port%=0])` → integer

Returns the direction of the joystick's V axis as -1, 0 or 1.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
If JoyVDir(0) <> 0 Then Print "Right stick is pushed up or down"
Flip
```

## Sound

### LoadSound

`LoadSound(file$)` → integer

Loads a sound from file (WAV, OGG, MP3 or FLAC) and returns it, or 0 if it cannot be loaded.

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
If blip = 0 Then RuntimeError "Could not load the sound"   ; 0 means it failed
PlaySound blip
```

### Load3DSound

`Load3DSound(file$)` → integer

Loads a sound from file for 3D positional playback with EmitSound and returns it.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
engine = Load3DSound("examples/media/hum.wav")   ; to be played with EmitSound
If engine = 0 Then RuntimeError "Could not load the sound"
```

### FreeSound

`FreeSound sound%`

Frees sound.

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
PlaySound blip
FreeSound blip             ; the handle must not be used after this
```

### LoopSound

`LoopSound sound%`

Makes sound loop when it is played.

```basic
hum = LoadSound("examples/media/hum.wav")
LoopSound hum              ; PlaySound now repeats the sound
channel = PlaySound(hum)
```

### SoundVolume

`SoundVolume sound%, volume#`

Sets sound's volume, from 0 (silent) to 1 (full).

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
SoundVolume blip, 0.5      ; 0 = silent, 1 = full volume
PlaySound blip
```

### SoundPan

`SoundPan sound%, pan#`

Sets sound's pan, from -1 (left) to 1 (right).

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
SoundPan blip, -1          ; -1 = left speaker, 0 = centre, 1 = right
PlaySound blip
```

### SoundPitch

`SoundPitch sound%, pitch%`

Sets the playback rate of sound, in Hz (samples per second).

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
SoundPitch blip, 22050     ; playback rate in Hz: lower is deeper and slower
PlaySound blip
```

### PlaySound

`PlaySound(sound%)` → integer

Plays sound and returns the channel it plays on.

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
channel = PlaySound(blip)  ; the channel lets you control this playing copy
If ChannelPlaying(channel) Then Print "Playing"
```

### PlayCDTrack

`PlayCDTrack(track%, [mode%=1])` → integer

Kept so older programs still run: CD audio is not supported, so it does nothing and returns 0.

```basic
channel = PlayCDTrack(2)   ; CD audio is not supported: does nothing, returns 0
Print channel
```

### PlayMusic

`PlayMusic(file$, [mode%=0])` → integer

Plays a music file (streamed from disk) once, or looping when mode is 1, and returns its channel, or 0 if it cannot be loaded.

```basic
music = PlayMusic("theme.ogg", 1)   ; 1 = loop, 0 = play once
If music = 0 Then Print "Could not load the music"
```

### StopChannel

`StopChannel channel%`

Stops the sound playing on channel.

```basic
hum = LoadSound("examples/media/hum.wav")
LoopSound hum
channel = PlaySound(hum)
StopChannel channel        ; silence it
```

### PauseChannel

`PauseChannel channel%`

Pauses the sound playing on channel.

```basic
music = PlayMusic("theme.ogg", 1)
PauseChannel music         ; for example when the game is paused
```

### ResumeChannel

`ResumeChannel channel%`

Resumes a channel paused with PauseChannel.

```basic
music = PlayMusic("theme.ogg", 1)
PauseChannel music
ResumeChannel music        ; carries on from where it stopped
```

### ChannelVolume

`ChannelVolume channel%, volume#`

Sets the volume of channel, from 0 (silent) to 1 (full).

```basic
hum = LoadSound("examples/media/hum.wav")
channel = PlaySound(hum)
ChannelVolume channel, 0.25   ; 0 = silent, 1 = full; only this playing copy
```

### CreateAudioBus

`CreateAudioBus([name$=""], [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an audio bus (a mixer channel that sounds play into) feeding parent (0, the default, is the master bus everything ends in) and returns its handle; name is optional. Use one bus each for music, effects and voices to set their volumes apart, or to give a group of sounds a reverb or filter.

```basic
; one bus each for music and effects, so their volumes can be set apart
music = CreateAudioBus("music")
effects = CreateAudioBus("effects")
BusVolume music, 0.5
SoundBus LoadSound("examples/media/hum.wav"), music
```

### BusVolume

`BusVolume bus%, volume#`

**(ext)** Sets the volume of bus (0 is the master bus), from 0 (silent) to 1 (full); above 1 is louder.

```basic
; the options menu's volume sliders set the buses
effects = CreateAudioBus("effects")
BusVolume effects, 0.8     ; 0 = silent, 1 = full
BusVolume 0, 1             ; bus 0 is the master bus everything ends in
```

### BusReverb

`BusReverb bus%, decay_seconds#, [dry#=1], [brightness#=11000]`

**(ext)** Gives bus a reverb, the echoes of a room, hall or cave: decay_seconds is how long the echoes last (0 removes the reverb), dry how much of the original sound is kept (0-1), brightness the highest frequency the echoes keep in Hz (lower is duller).

```basic
; sounds in a cave echo for three seconds
cave = CreateAudioBus("cave")
BusReverb cave, 3.0, 0.6, 6000   ; decay seconds, dry 0-1, brightness in Hz
SoundBus LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav"), cave
BusReverb cave, 0                ; 0 removes the reverb again
```

### BusFilter

`BusFilter bus%, kind%, [frequency#=1000], [quality#=0.7], [gain#=1]`

**(ext)** Gives bus a filter of kind FILTER_LOWPASS (muffles: underwater, behind a wall), FILTER_HIGHPASS (thins: a radio, a telephone), FILTER_BANDPASS, FILTER_LOWSHELF, FILTER_HIGHSHELF or FILTER_ALLPASS at frequency in Hz, with quality (the width around it) and gain; FILTER_NONE removes it. The filter comes before the reverb.

```basic
; muffle everything while the player is under water
world = CreateAudioBus("world")
BusFilter world, FILTER_LOWPASS, 800     ; only sounds below about 800 Hz pass
BusFilter world, FILTER_NONE             ; back to normal
```

### SoundBus

`SoundBus sound%, bus%`

**(ext)** Makes the channels that sound plays from now on go into bus.

```basic
; every later PlaySound of this sound goes into the voices bus
voices = CreateAudioBus("voices")
hello = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
SoundBus hello, voices
PlaySound hello
```

### ChannelBus

`ChannelBus channel%, bus%`

**(ext)** Moves a playing channel into bus.

```basic
; move a sound that is already playing into another bus
radio = CreateAudioBus("radio")
BusFilter radio, FILTER_HIGHPASS, 1500   ; thin, like a small speaker
channel = PlaySound(LoadSound("examples/media/hum.wav"))
ChannelBus channel, radio
```

### ChannelPan

`ChannelPan channel%, pan#`

Sets the pan of channel, from -1 (left) to 1 (right).

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
channel = PlaySound(blip)
ChannelPan channel, 1      ; -1 = left, 1 = right
```

### ChannelPitch

`ChannelPitch channel%, pitch%`

Sets the playback rate of channel, in Hz.

```basic
hum = LoadSound("examples/media/hum.wav")
channel = PlaySound(hum)
ChannelPitch channel, 44100   ; playback rate in Hz
```

### ChannelPlaying

`ChannelPlaying(channel%)` → integer

Returns 1 if channel is playing, otherwise 0.

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
channel = PlaySound(blip)
While ChannelPlaying(channel)   ; 1 while it plays, 0 once it has finished
    Delay 10
Wend
Print "Sound finished"
```

### CreateListener

`CreateListener(parent%, [rolloff_factor#=1], [doppler_scale#=1], [distance_scale#=1])` → integer

Creates the 3D sound listener (the player's ears) as a child of the entity parent, usually the camera, and returns it; rolloff_factor and distance_scale control how sound fades with distance, doppler_scale how much moving sounds change pitch.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
listener = CreateListener(camera)   ; the ears follow the camera
engine = Load3DSound("examples/media/hum.wav")
```

### EmitSound

`EmitSound(sound%, entity%)` → integer

Plays a 3D sound from the position of entity and returns its channel.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
camera = CreateCamera()
listener = CreateListener(camera)
engine = Load3DSound("examples/media/hum.wav")
car = CreateCube()
MoveEntity car, 5, 0, 10            ; to the right and ahead of the camera
channel = EmitSound(engine, car)    ; the sound comes from the car's position
```

### LoadSoundAsync

`LoadSoundAsync(file$, [spatial%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Starts loading a sound file in the background and returns its handle at once (silent until it arrives); spatial 1 makes it a 3D sound like Load3DSound. Use SoundLoaded or PendingLoads to know when it is ready.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
boom = LoadSoundAsync("explosion.wav")   ; returns at once; the game keeps running
While SoundLoaded(boom) = 0
    Flip                                 ; show a loading screen meanwhile
Wend
```

### SoundLoaded

`SoundLoaded(sound%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 when sound has loaded (always 1 for sounds loaded with LoadSound or Load3DSound), 0 while it is loading in the background, -1 if loading failed.

```basic
Graphics3D 640, 480
boom = LoadSoundAsync("explosion.wav")
state = SoundLoaded(boom)   ; 1 = ready, 0 = still loading, -1 = failed
If state = 1 Then PlaySound boom
If state = -1 Then Print "Could not load explosion.wav"
```

### SoundName

`SoundName(sound%)` → string

**(ext)** Returns the file sound was loaded from.

```basic
blip = LoadSound("examples/media/blip.wav")
Print SoundName(blip)      ; the file the sound came from
```

## Entities

### EntityClass

`EntityClass(entity%)` → string

Returns the kind of entity as a string, such as "Pivot", "Camera", "Light" or "Mesh".

```basic
; Ask an entity what kind of thing it is
camera = CreateCamera()
box = CreateCube()
Print EntityClass(camera)   ; Camera
Print EntityClass(box)      ; Mesh
```

### EntityAutoFade

`EntityAutoFade entity%, near#, far#`

Makes entity fade out as the camera gets between near and far units away; far 0 or less turns fading off.

```basic
; The crate fades out between 20 and 50 units away from the camera
camera = CreateCamera()
crate = CreateCube()
EntityAutoFade crate, 20, 50
; EntityAutoFade crate, 0, 0 would turn fading off again
```

### ClearWorld

`ClearWorld [entities%=1], [brushes%=1], [textures%=1]`

Frees all entities, brushes and/or textures, as chosen by entities, brushes and textures (all default 1).

```basic
; Throw away the whole level before loading the next one
level = CreateSphere()
tex = CreateTexture(64, 64)
ClearWorld                ; frees entities, brushes and textures
ClearWorld 1, 0, 0        ; or free only the entities
```

### CreatePivot

`CreatePivot([parent%=0])` → integer

Creates an invisible pivot entity, useful as a parent or marker, and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; A pivot is an invisible point; things parented to it move and turn with it
hinge = CreatePivot()
door = CreateCube(hinge)
PositionEntity door, 1, 0, 0
TurnEntity hinge, 0, 90, 0     ; the door swings around the hinge
```

### CreateCube

`CreateCube([parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a cube from -1 to 1 on each axis and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; Make a cube and move it up
box = CreateCube()
PositionEntity box, 0, 1, 5
```

### CreateSphere

`CreateSphere([segments%=8], [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a sphere of radius 1 with segments detail (default 8) and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; A smoother ball: more segments means more detail
ball = CreateSphere(16)
PositionEntity ball, 0, 1, 5
```

### CreateCylinder

`CreateCylinder([segments%=8], [solid%=1], [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a cylinder of radius 1 and height 2 with segments detail and returns its handle; solid 0 leaves the ends open. parent is optional.

```basic
; A pillar with 12 sides, and a pipe with open ends
pillar = CreateCylinder(12)
pipe = CreateCylinder(12, 0)     ; solid = 0 leaves the ends open
PositionEntity pillar, -3, 0, 8
PositionEntity pipe, 3, 0, 8
```

### CreateCone

`CreateCone([segments%=8], [solid%=1], [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a cone of radius 1 and height 2 with segments detail and returns its handle; solid 0 leaves the base open. parent is optional.

```basic
; A tree-top cone with 16 sides
treetop = CreateCone(16)
PositionEntity treetop, 0, 2, 6
ScaleEntity treetop, 1, 1.5, 1
```

### CreatePlane

`CreatePlane([segments%=1], [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates an infinite flat plane at Y 0 facing up, with segments detail, and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; A flat floor that goes on forever
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityColor floor, 80, 160, 80
```

### EntityScaleX

`EntityScaleX(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X scale of entity; global 1 gives the world scale including its parents.

```basic
; Read back the width of a stretched box
box = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity box, 3, 1, 1
Print EntityScaleX(box)       ; 3.0
```

### EntityScaleY

`EntityScaleY(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y scale of entity; global 1 gives the world scale including its parents.

```basic
; Read back the height of a stretched box
box = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity box, 1, 2, 1
Print EntityScaleY(box)       ; 2.0
```

### EntityScaleZ

`EntityScaleZ(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Z scale of entity; global 1 gives the world scale including its parents.

```basic
; With global = 1 the parent's scale is included
parent = CreatePivot()
box = CreateCube(parent)
ScaleEntity parent, 1, 1, 2
ScaleEntity box, 1, 1, 3
Print EntityScaleZ(box)       ; 3.0 (its own scale)
Print EntityScaleZ(box, 1)    ; 6.0 (world scale)
```

### CopyEntity

`CopyEntity(entity%, [parent%=0])` → integer

Makes a copy of entity (and its children), optionally attached to parent, and returns the new entity.

```basic
; Make a forest from one tree
tree = CreateCone()
HideEntity tree
For i = 1 To 5
    copy = CopyEntity(tree)
    ShowEntity copy
    PositionEntity copy, i * 3, 0, 10
Next
```

### FreeEntity

`FreeEntity entity%`

Deletes entity and all its children.

```basic
; Remove a bullet once it has travelled far enough
bullet = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity bullet, 0, 0, 200
If EntityZ(bullet) > 100 Then FreeEntity bullet : bullet = 0
```

### EntityParent

`EntityParent entity%, parent%, [global%=1]`

Attaches entity to parent (0 detaches it). With global 1 (default) the entity keeps its world position and rotation; with 0 it keeps its local ones.

```basic
; Put a hat on a head and keep it where it is
head = CreateSphere()
hat = CreateCone()
PositionEntity hat, 0, 1, 0
EntityParent hat, head          ; hat keeps its world position
EntityParent hat, 0             ; detach it again
```

### GetParent

`GetParent(entity%)` → integer

Returns the parent of entity, or 0 if it has none.

```basic
; Find out what a part is attached to
car = CreatePivot()
wheel = CreateCylinder(8, 1, car)
If GetParent(wheel) = car Then Print "The wheel belongs to the car"
```

### CountChildren

`CountChildren(entity%)` → integer

Returns the number of direct children of entity.

```basic
; Count the wheels on a car
car = CreatePivot()
For i = 1 To 4
    wheel = CreateCylinder(8, 1, car)
Next
Print CountChildren(car)        ; 4
```

### GetChild

`GetChild(entity%, index%)` → integer

Returns child number index (counting from 1) of entity, or 0 if there is none.

```basic
; Go through every child of an entity (numbered from 1)
car = CreatePivot()
For i = 1 To 4
    wheel = CreateCylinder(8, 1, car)
Next
For i = 1 To CountChildren(car)
    wheel = GetChild(car, i)
    TurnEntity wheel, 0, 0, 10
Next
```

### FindChild

`FindChild(entity%, name$)` → integer

Searches entity's children and their descendants for an entity called name and returns it, or 0 if not found.

```basic
; Look for a named part anywhere inside a model
robot = CreatePivot()
arm = CreateCube(robot)
NameEntity arm, "left_arm"
found = FindChild(robot, "left_arm")
If found <> 0 Then Print "Found the arm"
```

### NameEntity

`NameEntity entity%, name$`

Sets the name of entity.

```basic
; Give an entity a name so you can find it later
door = CreateCube()
NameEntity door, "front_door"
Print EntityName(door)
```

### EntityName

`EntityName(entity%)` → string

Returns the name of entity.

```basic
; Print the name of an entity
door = CreateCube()
NameEntity door, "front_door"
Print "This is the " + EntityName(door)
```

### HideEntity

`HideEntity entity%`

Hides entity and its children so they are not drawn and take no part in collisions or picking.

```basic
; Hide an enemy while it is dead
enemy = CreateCube()
HideEntity enemy
```

### ShowEntity

`ShowEntity entity%`

Shows an entity hidden with HideEntity.

```basic
; Bring a hidden enemy back
enemy = CreateCube()
HideEntity enemy
ShowEntity enemy
```

### EntityHidden

`EntityHidden(entity%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if entity has been hidden with HideEntity, otherwise 0.

```basic
; Only update an enemy while it is visible
enemy = CreateCube()
HideEntity enemy
If EntityHidden(enemy) = 1 Then Print "The enemy is hidden"
```

## Transforms

### PositionEntity

`PositionEntity entity%, x#, y#, z#, [global%=0]`

Places entity at x, y, z, relative to its parent unless global is 1.

```basic
; Place a ball 5 units up and 10 units in front of the origin
ball = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity ball, 0, 5, 10
```

### MoveEntity

`MoveEntity entity%, x#, y#, z#`

Moves entity by x, y, z along its own axes (so z moves it forward the way it faces).

```basic
; Fly a ship forward the way it faces
ship = CreateCube()
RotateEntity ship, 0, 90, 0
MoveEntity ship, 0, 0, 0.5       ; z is forward
```

### TranslateEntity

`TranslateEntity entity%, x#, y#, z#, [global%=0]`

Moves entity by x, y, z along the world axes (or its parent's axes when global is 0), ignoring its own rotation.

```basic
; Push a crate along the world X axis, whichever way it faces
crate = CreateCube()
RotateEntity crate, 0, 45, 0
TranslateEntity crate, 1, 0, 0
```

### RotateEntity

`RotateEntity entity%, pitch#, yaw#, roll#, [global%=0]`

Sets entity's rotation to pitch, yaw, roll in degrees, relative to its parent unless global is 1.

```basic
; Face the entity 90 degrees to the side
statue = CreateCone()
RotateEntity statue, 0, 90, 0     ; pitch, yaw, roll
```

### TurnEntity

`TurnEntity entity%, pitch#, yaw#, roll#, [global%=0]`

Turns entity by pitch, yaw, roll degrees from its current rotation.

```basic
; Spin a coin a little each frame
coin = CreateCylinder()
TurnEntity coin, 0, 3, 0
```

### ScaleEntity

`ScaleEntity entity%, x_scale#, y_scale#, z_scale#, [global%=0]`

Sets entity's scale on each axis to x_scale, y_scale, z_scale (1 is normal size).

```basic
; Make a flat wide platform
platform = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity platform, 4, 0.25, 4
```

### PointEntity

`PointEntity entity%, target%, [roll#=0]`

Rotates entity to face target entity, with roll degrees of roll.

```basic
; Make a turret track the player
turret = CreateCone()
player = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity player, 10, 0, 10
PointEntity turret, player
```

### LookAt

`LookAt entity%, x#, y#, z#, [roll#=0]`

**(ext)** Rotates entity to face the world position x, y, z, with roll degrees of roll.

```basic
; Aim a spotlight at a spot on the floor
lamp = CreateLight(3)
PositionEntity lamp, 0, 10, 0
LookAt lamp, 5, 0, 5
```

### CameraLookAt

`CameraLookAt camera%, x#, y#, z#, [roll#=0]`

**(ext)** Rotates camera to face the world position x, y, z, with roll degrees of roll; the same as LookAt.

```basic
; Point the camera at the middle of the scene
camera = CreateCamera()
PositionEntity camera, 0, 5, -10
CameraLookAt camera, 0, 0, 0
```

### EntityX

`EntityX(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

Returns entity's X position, relative to its parent or in world space when global is 1.

```basic
; Check where a ball is along X
ball = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity ball, 4, 0, 0
Print EntityX(ball)
```

### EntityY

`EntityY(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

Returns entity's Y position, relative to its parent or in world space when global is 1.

```basic
; Stop a falling ball at the ground
ball = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity ball, 0, -3, 0
If EntityY(ball) < 0 Then PositionEntity ball, EntityX(ball), 0, EntityZ(ball)
Print EntityY(ball)
```

### EntityZ

`EntityZ(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

Returns entity's Z position, relative to its parent or in world space when global is 1.

```basic
; Child positions are relative to the parent unless global is 1
parent = CreatePivot()
PositionEntity parent, 0, 0, 10
child = CreateCube(parent)
PositionEntity child, 0, 0, 2
Print EntityZ(child)        ; 2.0 (local)
Print EntityZ(child, 1)     ; 12.0 (world)
```

### EntityPitch

`EntityPitch(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

Returns entity's pitch in degrees, relative to its parent or in world space when global is 1.

```basic
; Read how far a cannon is tilted
cannon = CreateCylinder()
RotateEntity cannon, 30, 0, 0
Print EntityPitch(cannon)
```

### EntityYaw

`EntityYaw(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

Returns entity's yaw in degrees, relative to its parent or in world space when global is 1.

```basic
; Read which way a ship is turned
ship = CreateCube()
RotateEntity ship, 0, 45, 0
Print EntityYaw(ship)
```

### EntityRoll

`EntityRoll(entity%, [global%=0])` → float

Returns entity's roll in degrees, relative to its parent or in world space when global is 1.

```basic
; Read how far a plane is banked
plane = CreateCube()
RotateEntity plane, 0, 0, 20
Print EntityRoll(plane)
```

## Vectors and maths on entities

### TFormPoint

`TFormPoint x#, y#, z#, source_entity%, dest_entity%`

Transforms the point x, y, z from the space of source_entity to the space of dest_entity (0 means world space); read the result with TFormedX, TFormedY, TFormedZ.

```basic
; Where is the point 1 unit in front of the ship, in world space?
ship = CreateCube()
PositionEntity ship, 10, 0, 0
RotateEntity ship, 0, 90, 0
TFormPoint 0, 0, 1, ship, 0       ; from ship space to world space (0)
Print TFormedX() + ", " + TFormedY() + ", " + TFormedZ()
```

### TFormVector

`TFormVector x#, y#, z#, source_entity%, dest_entity%`

Transforms the direction x, y, z from the space of source_entity to the space of dest_entity (0 means world space), ignoring position; read the result with TFormedX, TFormedY, TFormedZ.

```basic
; Which world direction is "forward" for the ship?
ship = CreateCube()
RotateEntity ship, 0, 90, 0
TFormVector 0, 0, 1, ship, 0
Print TFormedX() + ", " + TFormedY() + ", " + TFormedZ()
```

### TFormNormal

`TFormNormal x#, y#, z#, source_entity%, dest_entity%`

Like TFormVector but the result is normalised to length 1; read it with TFormedX, TFormedY, TFormedZ.

```basic
; Same as TFormVector, but the result has length 1
ship = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity ship, 3, 3, 3
RotateEntity ship, 0, 90, 0
TFormNormal 0, 0, 1, ship, 0
Print TFormedX() + ", " + TFormedY() + ", " + TFormedZ()
```

### TFormedX

`TFormedX()` → float

Returns the X of the result of the last TFormPoint, TFormVector or TFormNormal.

```basic
; Read the X of the last TForm result
ship = CreateCube()
PositionEntity ship, 5, 0, 0
TFormPoint 0, 0, 0, ship, 0
Print TFormedX()          ; 5.0
```

### TFormedY

`TFormedY()` → float

Returns the Y of the result of the last TFormPoint, TFormVector or TFormNormal.

```basic
; Read the Y of the last TForm result
ship = CreateCube()
PositionEntity ship, 0, 7, 0
TFormPoint 0, 0, 0, ship, 0
Print TFormedY()          ; 7.0
```

### TFormedZ

`TFormedZ()` → float

Returns the Z of the result of the last TFormPoint, TFormVector or TFormNormal.

```basic
; Read the Z of the last TForm result
ship = CreateCube()
PositionEntity ship, 0, 0, 9
TFormPoint 0, 0, 0, ship, 0
Print TFormedZ()          ; 9.0
```

### VectorYaw

`VectorYaw(x#, y#, z#)` → float

Returns the yaw in degrees of the direction x, y, z.

```basic
; Which way (in degrees) is the direction 1, 0, 1 turned?
Print VectorYaw(1, 0, 1)
```

### VectorPitch

`VectorPitch(x#, y#, z#)` → float

Returns the pitch in degrees of the direction x, y, z.

```basic
; How steeply does the direction 0, -1, 1 slope?
Print VectorPitch(0, -1, 1)
```

### DeltaYaw

`DeltaYaw(src_entity%, dest_entity%)` → float

Returns the yaw in degrees src_entity would need to turn to face dest_entity.

```basic
; How far must a guard turn to face the player?
guard = CreateCube()
player = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity player, 10, 0, 10
amount = DeltaYaw(guard, player)
TurnEntity guard, 0, amount, 0
```

### DeltaPitch

`DeltaPitch(src_entity%, dest_entity%)` → float

Returns the pitch in degrees src_entity would need to turn to face dest_entity.

```basic
; How far must a turret tilt to aim at a plane?
turret = CreateCone()
plane = CreateCube()
PositionEntity plane, 0, 10, 10
TurnEntity turret, DeltaPitch(turret, plane), 0, 0
```

### AlignToVector

`AlignToVector entity%, vector_x#, vector_y#, vector_z#, axis%, [rate#=1]`

Turns entity so its axis (1 X, 2 Y, 3 Z) points along vector_x, vector_y, vector_z; rate from 0 to 1 sets how far it turns (1 aligns fully).

```basic
; Tilt a tree so its up axis (2 = Y) follows the slope of the ground
tree = CreateCone()
AlignToVector tree, 0.3, 1, 0, 2        ; axis 1 = X, 2 = Y, 3 = Z
AlignToVector tree, 0, 1, 0.3, 2, 0.1   ; rate 0.1 turns it only a tenth of the way
```

### GetMatElement

`GetMatElement(entity%, row%, column%)` → float

Returns the element at row and column (0 to 2) of entity's world rotation and scale matrix.

```basic
; Read the "forward" direction straight from the rotation matrix
ship = CreateCube()
RotateEntity ship, 0, 90, 0
forwardx = GetMatElement(ship, 2, 0)    ; row 2, column 0
Print forwardx
```

### EntityDistance

`EntityDistance(source_entity%, destination_entity%)` → float

Returns the distance in world units between source_entity and destination_entity.

```basic
; Wake a guard when the player comes within 20 units
guard = CreateCube()
player = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity player, 5, 0, 5
If EntityDistance(guard, player) < 20 Then Print "Guard wakes up"
```

## Cameras

### CameraClsMode

`CameraClsMode camera%, cls_color%, cls_zbuffer%`

Sets whether camera clears the colour buffer (cls_color) and depth buffer (cls_zbuffer) before rendering; 1 clears, 0 keeps what was there, so the camera's picture is laid over the other cameras (also inside its viewport). Cameras in scenes after the first always work like cls_color 0.

```basic
; Draw a second camera on top without erasing the first
main = CreateCamera()
minimap = CreateCamera()
CameraViewport minimap, 10, 10, 200, 200
CameraClsMode minimap, 0, 1       ; keep the colours, clear only depth
```

### CreateCamera

`CreateCamera([parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a camera and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; A camera 5 units back from the origin
camera = CreateCamera()
PositionEntity camera, 0, 2, -5
```

### CameraClsColor

`CameraClsColor camera%, red#, green#, blue#`

Sets the colour camera clears the background to, with red, green, blue from 0 to 255.

```basic
; A dark blue night sky behind everything
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraClsColor camera, 10, 10, 60
```

### CameraRange

`CameraRange camera%, near#, far#`

Sets the nearest and farthest distances camera can see (defaults 1 and 1000).

```basic
; See from 0.5 units out to 500 units away
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraRange camera, 0.5, 500
```

### CameraZoom

`CameraZoom camera%, zoom#`

Sets camera's zoom factor; 1 is the default, higher values zoom in.

```basic
; Zoom in like a sniper scope
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraZoom camera, 3       ; 1 is normal
```

### CameraViewport

`CameraViewport camera%, x%, y%, width%, height%`

Sets the area of the screen camera draws into, as x, y, width, height in pixels.

```basic
; Draw only into the left half of an 800 x 600 screen
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraViewport camera, 0, 0, 400, 600    ; x, y, width, height
```

### CameraProjMode

`CameraProjMode camera%, mode%`

Sets camera's projection: 0 off (draws nothing), 1 perspective (default), 2 orthographic.

```basic
; Flat view with no perspective
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraProjMode camera, 2       ; 0 off, 1 perspective, 2 orthographic
```

### CameraFogMode

`CameraFogMode camera%, mode%`

Sets camera's fog: 0 off, 1 linear fog.

```basic
; Turn fog on for a misty scene
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraFogMode camera, 1
```

### CameraFogColor

`CameraFogColor camera%, red#, green#, blue#`

Sets camera's fog colour, with red, green, blue from 0 to 255.

```basic
; Grey fog
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraFogMode camera, 1
CameraFogColor camera, 128, 128, 128
```

### CameraFogRange

`CameraFogRange camera%, near#, far#`

Sets the distances where camera's fog starts (near) and becomes solid (far).

```basic
; Fog starts at 10 units and is solid at 60
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraFogMode camera, 1
CameraFogRange camera, 10, 60
```

## Lights

### AmbientLight

`AmbientLight red#, green#, blue#`

Sets the colour of the ambient light that lights every surface equally, using red, green and blue values 0-255.

```basic
; A dim blue-ish ambient light that lights everything a little
AmbientLight 40, 40, 70
```

### CreateLight

`CreateLight([type%=1], [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a light and returns its handle; type is 1 directional (default), 2 point or 3 spot. parent is an optional parent entity.

```basic
; A sun, a lamp and a torch
sun = CreateLight()          ; type 1 directional
lamp = CreateLight(2)        ; type 2 point
torch = CreateLight(3)       ; type 3 spot
PositionEntity lamp, 0, 5, 0
```

### LightMesh

`LightMesh mesh%, red#, green#, blue#, [range#=0], [light_x#=0], [light_y#=0], [light_z#=0]`

Lights a mesh by adding colour to its vertex colours, using red, green, blue 0-255; with range above 0 the light is a point at light_x, light_y, light_z that fades with distance, otherwise the whole mesh is lit evenly.

```basic
; Light a wall with a red point light at 0, 2, 0 that reaches 10 units
wall = CreateCube()
LightMesh wall, 255, 0, 0, 10, 0, 2, 0
```

### LightColor

`LightColor light%, red#, green#, blue#`

Sets light's colour, with red, green, blue from 0 to 255 (higher values make it brighter).

```basic
; An orange campfire light
fire = CreateLight(2)
LightColor fire, 255, 140, 40
```

### LightRange

`LightRange light%, range#`

Sets how far light reaches, in world units.

```basic
; The lamp reaches 15 units
lamp = CreateLight(2)
LightRange lamp, 15
```

### LightConeAngles

`LightConeAngles light%, inner_angle#, outer_angle#`

Sets a spot light's inner (full brightness) and outer cone angles, in degrees.

```basic
; A torch with a bright centre of 20 degrees and a soft edge out to 45
torch = CreateLight(3)
LightConeAngles torch, 20, 45
```

### SetLightDirection

`SetLightDirection light%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Turns light so it shines along the direction x, y, z.

```basic
; Make the sun shine from above and to one side
sun = CreateLight()
SetLightDirection sun, 1, -1, 0.5
```

### SetEntityDirection

`SetEntityDirection entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Turns entity so it faces along the direction x, y, z.

```basic
; Make a ship face along the world X axis
ship = CreateCube()
SetEntityDirection ship, 1, 0, 0
```

### LightShadows

`LightShadows light%, enable%`

**(ext)** Turns real-time shadows on (enable 1) or off (0) for light.

```basic
fill = CreateLight(LIGHT_POINT)
LightShadows fill, False
```

### LightIntensity

`LightIntensity light%, intensity#`

**(ext)** Sets how bright light is, multiplying its colour: 1 is normal, above 1 brighter than white (a lamp that lights up a room, the sun at noon), 0 off.

```basic
; a lamp three times brighter than white, for a dazzling room
lamp = CreateLight(LIGHT_POINT)
LightColor lamp, 255, 230, 200
LightIntensity lamp, 3
```

### LightScatter

`LightScatter light%, amount#, [red#=255], [green#=255], [blue#=255]`

**(ext)** Sets the glow a point or spot light makes in the air around it (light shafts in dusty or foggy air): amount is a multiple of the normal strength (0 turns it off, 3 is thick haze), red, green, blue tint it. Needs EFFECT_LIGHT_SCATTER, on at high and ultra quality.

```basic
; a lantern in a misty cellar: thick orange haze around it
PostEffect EFFECT_LIGHT_SCATTER, True
lantern = CreateLight(LIGHT_POINT)
LightColor lantern, 255, 150, 60
LightScatter lantern, 3, 255, 170, 90
```

### LightCookie

`LightCookie light%, texture%`

**(ext)** Projects texture through a spot light like a slide, multiplying its light: dark parts block light (window bars, leaves), coloured parts tint it (stained glass); 0 removes it. Affects surfaces with the standard (PBR) material.

```basic
; moonlight through window bars: draw the bars into a texture
bars = CreateTexture(128, 128)
SetBuffer TextureBuffer(bars)
ClsColor 255, 255, 255
Cls
Color 0, 0, 0
Rect 60, 0, 8, 128
Rect 0, 60, 128, 8
SetBuffer BackBuffer()
moon = CreateLight(LIGHT_SPOT)
LightCookie moon, bars
```

### LightShadowBias

`LightShadowBias light%, bias#`

**(ext)** Sets the shadow bias of one light: raise it a little if lit surfaces show stripes or speckles of shadow ("shadow acne"); too high and shadows come loose from their objects.

```basic
; the floor shows stripes of shadow in the sun: push the shadow back a little
sun = CreateLight()
LightShadowBias sun, 0.002
```

## Materials and textures

### LoadAnimTexture

`LoadAnimTexture(file$, flags%, frame_width%, frame_height%, first_frame%, frame_count%)` → integer

Loads an image file holding several animation frames of frame_width by frame_height pixels as one texture, using frame_count frames starting at first_frame; returns its handle or 0 on failure.

```basic
; A sprite sheet of 4 frames, each 32 by 32 pixels
explosion = LoadAnimTexture("explosion.png", 1 + 2, 32, 32, 0, 4)
If explosion = 0 Then Print "Could not load the sheet"
```

### TextureFilter

`TextureFilter match_text$, flags%`

Applies texture flags automatically to every texture loaded later whose file name contains match_text.

```basic
; Every texture loaded later with "grass" in its name is mipmapped
TextureFilter "grass", 1 + 8
grass = LoadTexture("grass_01.png")
```

### ClearTextureFilters

`ClearTextureFilters`

Removes all texture filters set with TextureFilter.

```basic
; Stop applying the automatic flags
TextureFilter "grass", 1 + 8
ClearTextureFilters
```

### EntityColor

`EntityColor entity%, red#, green#, blue#`

Sets entity's colour, with red, green, blue from 0 to 255.

```basic
; Paint a cube red
box = CreateCube()
EntityColor box, 255, 0, 0
```

### EntityAlpha

`EntityAlpha entity%, alpha#`

Sets entity's transparency, from 0 (invisible) to 1 (solid).

```basic
; Make a window half see-through
window = CreateCube()
EntityAlpha window, 0.5
```

### LoadTextureAsync

`LoadTextureAsync(file$, [flags%=1])` → integer

**(ext)** Starts loading a texture from file in the background and returns it at once; it is plain white until the image has loaded. Check progress with TextureLoaded.

```basic
; Start loading a texture without freezing the game
tex = LoadTextureAsync("checker.png")
box = CreateCube()
EntityTexture box, tex          ; white until it has loaded
```

### TextureLoaded

`TextureLoaded(texture%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 when texture has finished loading, 0 while it is still loading and -1 if loading failed.

```basic
; Check whether a texture is ready
tex = LoadTextureAsync("checker.png")
If TextureLoaded(tex) = 1 Then Print "Ready"
If TextureLoaded(tex) = -1 Then Print "Loading failed"
```

### LoadTexture

`LoadTexture(file$, [flags%=1])` → integer

Loads a texture from file with the given flags (1 colour, 2 alpha, 4 masked, 8 mipmapped, 16/32 clamp U/V, 64 sphere map, 128 cube map...) and returns it, or 0 if the file cannot be loaded.

```basic
; Load a picture and put it on a cube
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png", 1 + 8)    ; colour + mipmapped
If tex = 0 Then RuntimeError "checker.png is missing"
box = CreateCube()
EntityTexture box, tex
```

### CreateTexture

`CreateTexture(width%, height%, [flags%=1], [frames%=1])` → integer

Creates a blank texture of width by height pixels with the given flags and number of animation frames, and returns it.

```basic
; A blank 256 x 256 texture
tex = CreateTexture(256, 256)
Print TextureWidth(tex)
```

### FreeTexture

`FreeTexture texture%`

Frees texture; entities that use it keep it until they no longer need it.

```basic
; Free a texture you no longer need
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
FreeTexture tex
```

### TextureWidth

`TextureWidth(texture%)` → integer

Returns the width of texture in pixels.

```basic
; Print the size of a texture
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
Print TextureWidth(tex) + " pixels wide"
```

### SetCubeFace

`SetCubeFace texture%, face%`

Chooses which face of a cube map texture (0 left, 1 front, 2 right, 3 back, 4 up, 5 down) its texture buffer draws to.

```basic
; Choose the front face (1) of a cube map to draw into
cube = CreateTexture(128, 128, 1 + 128)    ; flag 128 = cube map
SetCubeFace cube, 1
```

### SetCubeMode

`SetCubeMode texture%, mode%`

Sets how a cube map texture is used: 1 reflection (default), 2 diffuse lighting, 3 specular lighting.

```basic
; Use a cube map for reflections (1), diffuse (2) or specular (3) light
cube = CreateTexture(128, 128, 1 + 128)
SetCubeMode cube, 1
```

### TextureHeight

`TextureHeight(texture%)` → integer

Returns the height of texture in pixels.

```basic
; Print the height of a texture
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
Print TextureHeight(tex) + " pixels high"
```

### TextureName

`TextureName(texture%)` → string

Returns the file name texture was loaded from.

```basic
; Which file did this texture come from?
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
Print TextureName(tex)
```

### ScaleTexture

`ScaleTexture texture%, u_scale#, v_scale#`

Scales texture's coordinates by u_scale and v_scale (values above 1 make the texture smaller and repeat more).

```basic
; Tile a floor texture 8 times across and 8 times up
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
ScaleTexture tex, 8, 8
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityTexture floor, tex
```

### PositionTexture

`PositionTexture texture%, u_position#, v_position#`

Offsets texture's coordinates by u_position and v_position.

```basic
; Slide a texture along, for example moving water
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
PositionTexture tex, 0.25, 0
```

### RotateTexture

`RotateTexture texture%, angle#`

Rotates texture by angle degrees.

```basic
; Turn a texture by 45 degrees
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
RotateTexture tex, 45
```

### TextureBlend

`TextureBlend texture%, blend%`

Sets how texture combines with the layers below it: 0 none, 1 alpha, 2 multiply (default), 3 add, 4 dot3, 5 multiply by 2.

```basic
; Add a glow texture on top of the layer below it
glow = LoadTexture("ring.png", 1 + 2)
TextureBlend glow, 3      ; 3 = add
```

### TextureCoords

`TextureCoords texture%, coords%`

Chooses which UV set texture uses: 0 the first (default) or 1 the second.

```basic
; Use the second UV set of the mesh (for example for lightmaps)
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
TextureCoords tex, 1
```

### EntityTexture

`EntityTexture entity%, texture%, [frame%=0], [index%=0]`

Applies texture to entity, using animation frame frame, in texture layer index.

```basic
; Put a texture on a cube, and a second one on layer 1
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
box = CreateCube()
EntityTexture box, tex
detail = LoadTexture("ring.png", 1 + 2)
EntityTexture box, detail, 0, 1   ; frame 0, texture layer 1
```

### EntityNormalMap

`EntityNormalMap entity%, texture%`

**(ext)** Gives entity a normal map texture for per-pixel lighting detail; 0 removes it.

```basic
; Add bumps with a normal map
box = CreateCube()
bumps = LoadTexture("checker.png")
EntityNormalMap box, bumps
EntityNormalMap box, 0        ; remove it again
```

### EntityShininess

`EntityShininess entity%, shininess#`

Sets entity's specular shininess, from 0 (none) to 1 (very shiny).

```basic
; Make a ball shiny
ball = CreateSphere(16)
EntityShininess ball, 0.8
```

### EntityBlend

`EntityBlend entity%, blend%`

Sets how entity is blended with what is behind it: 1 alpha, 2 multiply, 3 add.

```basic
; Make a glowing beam that adds light to what is behind it
beam = CreateCylinder()
EntityBlend beam, 3         ; 1 alpha, 2 multiply, 3 add
```

### EntityFX

`EntityFX entity%, fx%`

Sets special effects flags for entity: 1 full bright, 2 use vertex colours, 4 flat shading, 8 no fog, 16 no back-face culling, 32 force alpha blending.

```basic
; A full-bright cube that ignores lighting and is drawn on both sides
box = CreateCube()
EntityFX box, 1 + 16
```

### EntityOrder

`EntityOrder entity%, order%`

Sets the drawing order of entity: non-zero values are drawn after the main scene with depth buffering off, lower values in front.

```basic
; Draw a gun in front of everything else
gun = CreateCube()
EntityOrder gun, -1
```

## PBR materials
