### EntityMetallic

`EntityMetallic entity%, metallic#`

**(ext)** Sets how metallic entity's material is, from 0 (not metal) to 1 (metal).

```basic
; A fully metal ball
ball = CreateSphere(16)
EntityMetallic ball, 1
```

### EntityRoughness

`EntityRoughness entity%, roughness#`

**(ext)** Sets the roughness of entity's material, from 0 (smooth and glossy) to 1 (rough and matte).

```basic
; A smooth, glossy ball
ball = CreateSphere(16)
EntityRoughness ball, 0.1
```

### EntityEmissive

`EntityEmissive entity%, red#, green#, blue#, [intensity#=1]`

**(ext)** Makes entity glow with the colour red, green, blue (0-255) times intensity; all zero turns the glow off. Glow shows strongly with the bloom post effect.

```basic
; A lamp that glows orange
lamp = CreateSphere()
EntityEmissive lamp, 255, 120, 20, 2     ; red, green, blue, intensity
EntityEmissive lamp, 0, 0, 0             ; switch the glow off
```

### EntityMetallicMap

`EntityMetallicMap entity%, texture%`

**(ext)** Gives entity a texture controlling how metallic each part of it is; 0 removes it.

```basic
; Metal in some places and not in others
box = CreateCube()
mask = LoadTexture("checker.png")
EntityMetallicMap box, mask
```

### EntityRoughnessMap

`EntityRoughnessMap entity%, texture%`

**(ext)** Gives entity a texture controlling the roughness of each part of it; 0 removes it.

```basic
; Rough in some places and smooth in others
box = CreateCube()
mask = LoadTexture("checker.png")
EntityRoughnessMap box, mask
```

### EntityEmissiveMap

`EntityEmissiveMap entity%, texture%`

**(ext)** Gives entity a texture controlling which parts of it glow; 0 removes it.

```basic
; Only the bright parts of this picture glow
sign = CreateCube()
glowmap = LoadTexture("ring.png")
EntityEmissiveMap sign, glowmap
```

### EntityAOMap

`EntityAOMap entity%, texture%`

**(ext)** Gives entity an ambient occlusion texture that darkens creases and corners; 0 removes it.

```basic
; Darken the creases with a baked occlusion texture
wall = CreateCube()
occlusion = LoadTexture("checker.png")
EntityAOMap wall, occlusion
```

### EntityLightmap

`EntityLightmap entity%, texture%`

**(ext)** Gives entity a lightmap texture, read with the mesh's second UV set; 0 removes it.

```basic
; A baked lightmap, read with the second UV set
room = CreateCube()
baked = LoadTexture("checker.png")
EntityLightmap room, baked
```

### BakeLightmaps

`BakeLightmaps([texels_per_unit#=4], [quality%=1], [direct%=0], [entity%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Works out soft lighting for still scenery and stores it in lightmap textures, then returns how many surfaces were baked: sky light (the AmbientLight colour) dimmed in corners and under things, and light bounced off nearby surfaces in their colours. texels_per_unit sets the detail (default 4 per unit), quality 0 fast, 1 normal, 2 best, direct 1 also bakes the lights' direct light with shadows (then turn those lights off), entity limits it to one entity and its children. Only meshes with the standard (PBR) material are baked; it may take seconds. Turn AmbientLight down afterwards so the baked sky light shows.

```basic
; bake soft sky light, corner shadows and bounced light into a still room
Graphics3D 1280, 720
room = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity room, 5, 3, 5
FlipMesh room                         ; seen from inside
lamp = CreateLight(LIGHT_POINT)
AmbientLight 120, 120, 130            ; the sky light that gets baked
count = BakeLightmaps(4, 1, True)     ; 4 texels a unit, normal quality, lamp light too
HideEntity lamp                       ; its light is baked in now
AmbientLight 20, 20, 25               ; turn down, so the baked light shows
Print count + " surfaces baked"
```

### ClearLightmaps

`ClearLightmaps [entity%=0]`

**(ext)** Removes baked light from entity and its children, or from everything (0, the default).

```basic
; remove the baked light, for example before baking again after a change
Graphics3D 1280, 720
room = CreateCube()
BakeLightmaps
ClearLightmaps room        ; just this entity and its children
ClearLightmaps             ; or everything
```

### EntityHeightMap

`EntityHeightMap entity%, texture%, [scale#=0.08]`

**(ext)** Gives entity a height map texture (white is high) for parallax mapping, with depth scale (default 0.08); 0 removes it.

```basic
; Make a brick wall look deeper with parallax mapping
wall = CreateCube()
heights = LoadTexture("checker.png")
EntityHeightMap wall, heights, 0.1      ; depth scale
```

### CreatePBRMaterial

`CreatePBRMaterial()` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a physically based material (metallic/roughness, normal maps, parallax, emission, environment reflections, shadows) and returns it. Draw entities with it using EntityMaterial.

```basic
steel = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAlbedo steel, 160, 160, 165
SetMetallic steel, 1
SetRoughness steel, 0.45
pipe = CreateCylinder(24)
EntityMaterial pipe, steel
```

### SetAlbedo

`SetAlbedo material%, red#, green#, blue#, [alpha#=255]`

**(ext)** Sets the base colour of material, red, green, blue, alpha 0-255.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAlbedo m, 200, 30, 30
```

### SetAlbedoMap

`SetAlbedoMap material%, texture%`

**(ext)** Sets the base colour texture of material.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAlbedoMap m, LoadTexture("examples/media/checker.png")
```

### SetMetallic

`SetMetallic material%, metallic#`

**(ext)** Sets how metallic material is, 0 (plastic, stone) to 1 (metal).

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetMetallic m, 1
```

### SetRoughness

`SetRoughness material%, roughness#`

**(ext)** Sets how rough material is, 0 (mirror-smooth) to 1 (matte).

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetRoughness m, 0.2
```

### SetEmissive

`SetEmissive material%, red#, green#, blue#, [strength#=1]`

**(ext)** Makes material glow with red, green, blue 0-255 times strength (glows more with bloom).

```basic
screen = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAlbedo screen, 10, 10, 10
SetEmissive screen, 80, 200, 255, 3
```

### SetNormalMap

`SetNormalMap material%, texture%, [strength#=1]`

**(ext)** Sets the normal map of material, with strength to soften or exaggerate the bumps.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetNormalMap m, LoadTexture("brick_normal.png"), 1.5
```

### SetMetallicMap

`SetMetallicMap material%, texture%`

**(ext)** Sets a texture whose red channel scales the metallic value of material across its surface.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetMetallic m, 1
SetMetallicMap m, LoadTexture("armour_metal.png")
```

### SetRoughnessMap

`SetRoughnessMap material%, texture%`

**(ext)** Sets a texture whose red channel scales the roughness of material across its surface.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetRoughness m, 1
SetRoughnessMap m, LoadTexture("floor_rough.png")
```

### SetAOMap

`SetAOMap material%, texture%`

**(ext)** Sets the ambient occlusion texture of material (dark where ambient light cannot reach).

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAOMap m, LoadTexture("brick_ao.png")
```

### SetEmissiveMap

`SetEmissiveMap material%, texture%`

**(ext)** Sets the texture that colours the glow of material.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetEmissive m, 255, 255, 255, 2
SetEmissiveMap m, LoadTexture("windows_lit.png")
```

### SetOpacityMap

`SetOpacityMap material%, texture%, [mode%=1]`

**(ext)** Sets a texture whose red channel is the opacity of material; mode OPACITY_CUTOUT (default) cuts holes and keeps shadows, OPACITY_BLEND blends, OPACITY_OPAQUE ignores it.

```basic
leaves = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAlbedoMap leaves, LoadTexture("leaf.png")
SetOpacityMap leaves, LoadTexture("leaf_mask.png")
```

### SetOpacity

`SetOpacity material%, opacity#, [mode%=2]`

**(ext)** Sets the opacity of material, 0-1; mode OPACITY_BLEND (default) blends, OPACITY_CUTOUT cuts below 0.5.

```basic
glassy = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAlbedo glassy, 180, 220, 255
SetOpacity glassy, 0.3
```

### SetHeightMap

`SetHeightMap material%, texture%, [scale#=0.05]`

**(ext)** Sets the height map of material (white = high) for parallax mapping, with its depth scale; texture 0 turns it off.

```basic
cobbles = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetAlbedoMap cobbles, LoadTexture("cobbles.png")
SetHeightMap cobbles, LoadTexture("cobbles_height.png"), 0.04
```

### SetEnvironmentMap

`SetEnvironmentMap material%, cubemap%, [intensity#=1]`

**(ext)** Sets the cube map material reflects, with intensity.

```basic
chrome = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetMetallic chrome, 1
SetRoughness chrome, 0.05
SetEnvironmentMap chrome, LoadTexture("sky.png", 128)
```

### SetReflectionIntensity

`SetReflectionIntensity material%, intensity#`

**(ext)** Sets how strongly material reflects its environment map.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetEnvironmentMap m, LoadTexture("sky.png", 128)
SetReflectionIntensity m, 0.4
```

### SetFresnel

`SetFresnel material%, amount#`

**(ext)** Sets how much stronger reflections of material get at glancing angles, 0 (not at all) to 1 (fully).

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetFresnel m, 0.5
```

### SetParallaxScale

`SetParallaxScale material%, scale#`

**(ext)** Sets the depth of material's parallax mapping.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetHeightMap m, LoadTexture("cobbles_height.png")
SetParallaxScale m, 0.08
```

### SetMaterialWorkflow

`SetMaterialWorkflow material%, workflow%, [red#=10], [green#=10], [blue#=10]`

**(ext)** Sets the workflow of material: WORKFLOW_METALLIC, or WORKFLOW_SPECULAR with the specular colour red, green, blue 0-255.

```basic
m = CreatePBRMaterial()
SetMaterialWorkflow m, WORKFLOW_SPECULAR, 40, 40, 40
```

## Brushes

### CreateBrush

`CreateBrush([red#=255], [green#=255], [blue#=255])` → integer

Creates a brush with colour red, green, blue (0-255, default white) and returns its handle.

```basic
; A reusable red brush
red = CreateBrush(255, 0, 0)
box = CreateCube()
PaintEntity box, red
```

### LoadBrush

`LoadBrush(file$, [flags%=1], [u_scale#=1], [v_scale#=1])` → integer

Creates a brush with the texture in file and returns its handle; flags are texture flags and u_scale, v_scale scale the texture.

```basic
; A brush that already has a texture, tiled 4 times
brick = LoadBrush("checker.png", 1, 4, 4)
wall = CreateCube()
PaintEntity wall, brick
```

### FreeBrush

`FreeBrush brush%`

Frees brush. Things already painted with it keep their look.

```basic
; Free a brush after painting; the paint stays
brush = CreateBrush(0, 200, 0)
box = CreateCube()
PaintEntity box, brush
FreeBrush brush
```

### BrushColor

`BrushColor brush%, red#, green#, blue#`

Sets the colour of brush to red, green, blue (0-255).

```basic
; Change the colour of a brush
brush = CreateBrush()
BrushColor brush, 255, 128, 0
```

### BrushAlpha

`BrushAlpha brush%, alpha#`

Sets the alpha of brush from 0 (invisible) to 1 (solid).

```basic
; A half transparent brush
brush = CreateBrush()
BrushAlpha brush, 0.5
```

### BrushShininess

`BrushShininess brush%, shininess#`

Sets the specular shininess of brush from 0 to 1.

```basic
; A shiny brush
brush = CreateBrush()
BrushShininess brush, 0.7
```

### BrushTexture

`BrushTexture brush%, texture%, [frame%=0], [index%=0]`

Puts frame of texture on brush in texture layer index (default 0).

```basic
; Put a texture on a brush
brush = CreateBrush()
tex = LoadTexture("checker.png")
BrushTexture brush, tex            ; frame 0, layer 0
```

### BrushBlend

`BrushBlend brush%, blend%`

Sets the blend mode of brush: 1 alpha, 2 multiply, 3 add.

```basic
; A brush that adds its colour to the background
brush = CreateBrush()
BrushBlend brush, 3
```

### BrushFX

`BrushFX brush%, fx%`

Sets the effect flags of brush (1 full bright, 2 vertex colours, 4 flat shaded, 8 no fog, 16 no back-face culling, 32 force alpha blending).

```basic
; A brush for full-bright, two-sided surfaces
brush = CreateBrush()
BrushFX brush, 1 + 16
```

### PaintSurface

`PaintSurface surface%, brush%`

Paints surface with brush.

```basic
; Paint just one surface of a mesh
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
a = AddVertex(surf, 0, 0, 0)
b = AddVertex(surf, 1, 0, 0)
c = AddVertex(surf, 0, 1, 0)
AddTriangle surf, a, b, c
brush = CreateBrush(255, 0, 0)
PaintSurface surf, brush
```

### PaintEntity

`PaintEntity entity%, brush%`

Paints a whole entity with brush.

```basic
; Paint a whole cube blue
brush = CreateBrush(0, 0, 255)
box = CreateCube()
PaintEntity box, brush
```

### PaintMesh

`PaintMesh mesh%, brush%`

Paints every surface of mesh with brush.

```basic
; Paint every surface of a mesh green
brush = CreateBrush(0, 255, 0)
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
PaintMesh mesh, brush
```

### GetSurfaceBrush

`GetSurfaceBrush(surface%)` → integer

Returns a new brush with the look of surface. Free it with FreeBrush when done.

```basic
; Copy the look of a surface, then free the copy
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
copy = GetSurfaceBrush(surf)
FreeBrush copy
```

### GetEntityBrush

`GetEntityBrush(entity%)` → integer

Returns a new brush with the look of entity. Free it with FreeBrush when done.

```basic
; Give a second cube the same look as the first
original = CreateCube()
EntityColor original, 255, 0, 0
look = GetEntityBrush(original)
twin = CreateCube()
PaintEntity twin, look
FreeBrush look
```

### GetBrushTexture

`GetBrushTexture(brush%, [index%=0])` → integer

Returns the texture in layer index (default 0) of brush, or 0 if there is none.

```basic
; Find the texture on a brush
brush = LoadBrush("checker.png")
tex = GetBrushTexture(brush)
If tex <> 0 Then Print TextureName(tex)
```

## Custom meshes

### CreateMesh

`CreateMesh([brush%=0], [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates an empty mesh and returns its handle; brush and parent are optional.

```basic
; An empty mesh; add a surface and vertices to give it a shape
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
Print "Surfaces: " + CountSurfaces(mesh)
```

### CreateSurface

`CreateSurface(mesh%, [brush%=0])` → integer

Adds a new empty surface to mesh, optionally painted with brush, and returns its handle.

```basic
; Surfaces hold the triangles; here the surface is painted red
Graphics3D 800, 600
red = CreateBrush(255, 0, 0)
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh, red)
Print "Created surface " + surf
```

### CountSurfaces

`CountSurfaces(mesh%)` → integer

Returns the number of surfaces in mesh.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateMesh()
a = CreateSurface(mesh)
b = CreateSurface(mesh)
Print "Surfaces: " + CountSurfaces(mesh)   ; 2
```

### GetSurface

`GetSurface(mesh%, index%)` → integer

Returns the handle of surface number index (starting at 1) of mesh.

```basic
; Surfaces are numbered from 1
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
For i = 1 To CountSurfaces(mesh)
    surf = GetSurface(mesh, i)
    Print "Surface " + i + " has " + CountVertices(surf) + " vertices"
Next
```

### AddVertex

`AddVertex(surface%, x#, y#, z#, [u#=0], [v#=0], [w#=1])` → integer

Adds a vertex at x, y, z with texture coordinates u, v, w to surface and returns its index (starting at 0).

```basic
; Three corners of a triangle; the returned numbers start at 0
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
v0 = AddVertex(surf, -1, 0, 0, 0, 1)   ; x, y, z, then texture u, v
v1 = AddVertex(surf, 0, 2, 0, 0.5, 0)
v2 = AddVertex(surf, 1, 0, 0, 1, 1)
AddTriangle surf, v0, v1, v2
```

### AddTriangle

`AddTriangle(surface%, v0%, v1%, v2%)` → integer

Adds a triangle joining vertices v0, v1 and v2 to surface and returns its index.

```basic
; Join three vertices into a triangle and print its number
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
a = AddVertex(surf, 0, 0, 0)
b = AddVertex(surf, 0, 1, 0)
c = AddVertex(surf, 1, 0, 0)
tri = AddTriangle(surf, a, b, c)
Print "Triangle number " + tri
```

### ClearSurface

`ClearSurface surface%, [clear_vertices%=1], [clear_triangles%=1]`

Removes the vertices and/or triangles from surface, as chosen by clear_vertices and clear_triangles (both default 1).

```basic
; Remove all triangles but keep the vertices (second value 1 = clear triangles)
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
ClearSurface surf, 0, 1
Print CountVertices(surf) + " vertices, " + CountTriangles(surf) + " triangles"
```

### CountVertices

`CountVertices(surface%)` → integer

Returns the number of vertices in surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Vertices: " + CountVertices(surf)
```

### CountTriangles

`CountTriangles(surface%)` → integer

Returns the number of triangles in surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Triangles: " + CountTriangles(surf)
```

### TriangleVertex

`TriangleVertex(surface%, triangle%, corner%)` → integer

Returns the index of the vertex at corner (0, 1 or 2) of triangle in surface.

```basic
; Print the three vertex numbers of the first triangle (triangles start at 0)
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
For corner = 0 To 2
    Print "Corner " + corner + " uses vertex " + TriangleVertex(surf, 0, corner)
Next
```

### VertexCoords

`VertexCoords surface%, index%, x#, y#, z#`

Moves vertex index of surface to x, y, z.

```basic
; Move the first vertex up to make a spike
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexCoords surf, 0, 0, 3, 0   ; vertex 0 goes to x=0, y=3, z=0
```

### VertexNormal

`VertexNormal surface%, index%, nx#, ny#, nz#`

Sets the normal of vertex index of surface to nx, ny, nz.

```basic
; Make vertex 0 point straight up for lighting
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexNormal surf, 0, 0, 1, 0
```

### VertexColor

`VertexColor surface%, index%, red#, green#, blue#, [alpha#=1]`

Sets the colour of vertex index of surface to red, green, blue (0-255) and alpha (0 to 1, default 1). Shown when the entity uses vertex colours (EntityFX 2).

```basic
; Colour a vertex orange; use EntityFX 2 so the colours show
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexColor surf, 0, 255, 128, 0   ; red, green, blue (alpha defaults to 1)
EntityFX mesh, 2
```

### VertexData

`VertexData surface%, index%, slot%, x#, [y#=0], [z#=0], [w#=0]`

**(ext)** Stores up to four extra values x, y, z, w in data slot (0 to 3) of vertex index of surface, for use by custom shaders.

```basic
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
v = AddVertex(surf, 0, 0, 0)
VertexData surf, v, 0, Rnd(0, 6.28), 1      ; a random phase and a height
```

### VertexDataValue

`VertexDataValue(surface%, index%, slot%, component%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns one component (0 to 3) of data slot (0 to 3) of vertex index of surface, as set with VertexData.

```basic
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
v = AddVertex(surf, 0, 0, 0)
VertexData surf, v, 0, 2.5
DebugLog VertexDataValue(surf, v, 0, 0)     ; 2.5
```

### VertexTexCoords

`VertexTexCoords surface%, index%, u#, v#, [w#=1], [coord_set%=0]`

Sets the texture coordinates u, v, w of vertex index of surface in coordinate set coord_set (0 or 1).

```basic
; Stretch the texture over the first vertex's corner
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexTexCoords surf, 0, 0.5, 0.5   ; u, v in the first coordinate set
```

### VertexX

`VertexX(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the X position of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Vertex 0 x = " + VertexX(surf, 0)
```

### VertexY

`VertexY(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the Y position of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Vertex 0 y = " + VertexY(surf, 0)
```

### VertexZ

`VertexZ(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the Z position of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Vertex 0 z = " + VertexZ(surf, 0)
```

### VertexNX

`VertexNX(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the X part of the normal of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Normal x = " + VertexNX(surf, 0)
```

### VertexNY

`VertexNY(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the Y part of the normal of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Normal y = " + VertexNY(surf, 0)
```

### VertexNZ

`VertexNZ(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the Z part of the normal of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "Normal z = " + VertexNZ(surf, 0)
```

### VertexRed

`VertexRed(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the red component (0-255) of the colour of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexColor surf, 0, 200, 100, 50
Print "Red = " + VertexRed(surf, 0)   ; 0 to 255
```

### VertexGreen

`VertexGreen(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the green component (0-255) of the colour of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexColor surf, 0, 200, 100, 50
Print "Green = " + VertexGreen(surf, 0)
```

### VertexBlue

`VertexBlue(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the blue component (0-255) of the colour of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexColor surf, 0, 200, 100, 50
Print "Blue = " + VertexBlue(surf, 0)
```

### VertexAlpha

`VertexAlpha(surface%, index%)` → float

Returns the alpha (0 to 1) of vertex index of surface.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
VertexColor surf, 0, 255, 255, 255, 0.5
Print "Alpha = " + VertexAlpha(surf, 0)   ; 0 to 1
```

### VertexU

`VertexU(surface%, index%, [coord_set%=0])` → float

Returns the U texture coordinate of vertex index of surface in coordinate set coord_set.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "U = " + VertexU(surf, 0)   ; first coordinate set
```

### VertexV

`VertexV(surface%, index%, [coord_set%=0])` → float

Returns the V texture coordinate of vertex index of surface in coordinate set coord_set.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "V = " + VertexV(surf, 0)
```

### VertexW

`VertexW(surface%, index%, [coord_set%=0])` → float

Returns the W texture coordinate of vertex index of surface in coordinate set coord_set.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
surf = GetSurface(mesh, 1)
Print "W = " + VertexW(surf, 0)
```

### UpdateNormals

`UpdateNormals mesh%`

Recalculates the normals of every vertex in mesh for smooth lighting.

```basic
; Build a triangle by hand, then let the engine work out smooth lighting
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh)
a = AddVertex(surf, -1, 0, 0)
b = AddVertex(surf, 0, 2, 0)
c = AddVertex(surf, 1, 0, 0)
AddTriangle surf, a, b, c
UpdateNormals mesh
```

### FlipMesh

`FlipMesh mesh%`

Reverses the triangles of mesh so it faces the other way.

```basic
; Turn a mesh inside out, for example a room you stand inside
Graphics3D 800, 600
room = CreateCube()
ScaleMesh room, 10, 5, 10
FlipMesh room
```

### ScaleMesh

`ScaleMesh mesh%, x_scale#, y_scale#, z_scale#`

Scales the vertices of mesh by x_scale, y_scale, z_scale, changing the geometry itself.

```basic
; Change the shape itself (not just the entity's scale): a long flat plank
Graphics3D 800, 600
plank = CreateCube()
ScaleMesh plank, 4, 0.2, 1
```

### PositionMesh

`PositionMesh mesh%, x#, y#, z#`

Moves the vertices of mesh by x, y, z, changing the geometry itself.

```basic
; Shift the vertices so the entity turns around its bottom edge
Graphics3D 800, 600
door = CreateCube()
ScaleMesh door, 1, 2, 0.1
PositionMesh door, 1, 2, 0
```

### RotateMesh

`RotateMesh mesh%, pitch#, yaw#, roll#`

Rotates the vertices of mesh by pitch, yaw and roll in degrees, changing the geometry itself.

```basic
; Lay a cylinder on its side (pitch, yaw, roll in degrees)
Graphics3D 800, 600
log = CreateCylinder()
RotateMesh log, 0, 0, 90
```

### FitMesh

`FitMesh mesh%, x#, y#, z#, width#, height#, depth#, [uniform%=0]`

Scales and moves the vertices of mesh to fit the box at x, y, z of size width, height, depth; uniform 1 keeps the mesh's proportions.

```basic
; Squeeze the mesh into a box at x, y, z of size width, height, depth; 1 keeps proportions
Graphics3D 800, 600
statue = CreateSphere()
FitMesh statue, -1, 0, -1, 2, 4, 2, 1
```

### AddMesh

`AddMesh source_mesh%, dest_mesh%`

Copies the surfaces of source_mesh into dest_mesh.

```basic
; Combine two meshes into one: the first is copied into the second
Graphics3D 800, 600
base = CreateCube()
ScaleMesh base, 2, 0.2, 2
tower = CreateCylinder()
PositionMesh tower, 0, 1, 0
AddMesh tower, base
FreeEntity tower
```

### CopyMesh

`CopyMesh(mesh%, [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a new mesh with a copy of the geometry of mesh and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; A separate copy whose geometry can be changed without touching the original
Graphics3D 800, 600
original = CreateCube()
copy = CopyMesh(original)
ScaleMesh copy, 2, 2, 2
```

### MeshCullBox

`MeshCullBox mesh%, x#, y#, z#, width#, height#, depth#`

Accepted for compatibility; meshes are culled by their real bounds.

```basic
; Kept so old programs still run; it has no effect
Graphics3D 800, 600
mesh = CreateCube()
MeshCullBox mesh, -10, -10, -10, 20, 20, 20
```

### FindSurface

`FindSurface(mesh%, brush%)` → integer

Returns the surface of mesh painted with brush, or 0 if there is none.

```basic
; Find the surface painted with a given brush
Graphics3D 800, 600
red = CreateBrush(255, 0, 0)
mesh = CreateMesh()
surf = CreateSurface(mesh, red)
found = FindSurface(mesh, red)
If found = surf Then Print "Found the red surface" Else Print "No red surface"
```

### MeshesIntersect

`MeshesIntersect(mesh_a%, mesh_b%)` → integer

Returns 1 if any triangle of mesh_a intersects a triangle of mesh_b, otherwise 0.

```basic
; Check whether two models touch
Graphics3D 800, 600
ship = CreateCube()
rock = CreateSphere()
PositionMesh rock, 0.5, 0, 0
If MeshesIntersect(ship, rock) Then Print "Crash!" Else Print "Clear"
```

### MeshWidth

`MeshWidth(mesh%)` → float

Returns the width (size along X) of mesh, in world units.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
crate = CreateCube()
ScaleMesh crate, 3, 1, 2
Print "Width: " + MeshWidth(crate)
```

### MeshHeight

`MeshHeight(mesh%)` → float

Returns the height (size along Y) of mesh, in world units.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
crate = CreateCube()
ScaleMesh crate, 3, 1, 2
Print "Height: " + MeshHeight(crate)
```

### MeshDepth

`MeshDepth(mesh%)` → float

Returns the depth (size along Z) of mesh, in world units.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
crate = CreateCube()
ScaleMesh crate, 3, 1, 2
Print "Depth: " + MeshDepth(crate)
```

### CountMeshTriangles

`CountMeshTriangles(mesh%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the total number of triangles in all surfaces of mesh.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
ball = CreateSphere(16)
Print "The sphere has " + CountMeshTriangles(ball) + " triangles"
```

## Models and animation

### CaptureWorld

`CaptureWorld`

Records the position, rotation and scale of every entity, for RenderWorld tweening.

```basic
; Record positions so RenderWorld can smooth movement between two fixed updates
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
cube = CreateCube()
CaptureWorld
UpdateWorld
RenderWorld 0.5   ; halfway between the captured and the new positions
Flip
```

### AddAnimSeq

`AddAnimSeq(entity%, length%)` → integer

Adds an empty keyframe animation sequence of length frames to entity and returns its number; fill it with SetAnimKey.

```basic
; Make a simple 30-frame animation: the cube moves 5 units to the right
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
seq = AddAnimSeq(cube, 30)
PositionEntity cube, 0, 0, 0
SetAnimKey cube, 0
PositionEntity cube, 5, 0, 0
SetAnimKey cube, 30
Animate cube, 1, 1, seq
```

### SetAnimKey

`SetAnimKey entity%, frame%, [pos_key%=1], [rot_key%=1], [scale_key%=1]`

Stores entity's current position, rotation and/or scale (chosen by pos_key, rot_key, scale_key) as a key at frame of its last added sequence.

```basic
; Store the cube's current position as keys at frames 0 and 20
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
seq = AddAnimSeq(cube, 20)
SetAnimKey cube, 0
TurnEntity cube, 0, 180, 0
SetAnimKey cube, 20, 0, 1, 0   ; only the rotation key this time
Animate cube, 3, 1, seq
```

### LoaderMatrix

`LoaderMatrix file_extension$, xx#, xy#, xz#, yx#, yy#, yz#, zx#, zy#, zz#`

Sets the matrix applied to models loaded from files with file_extension; the three rows xx,xy,xz / yx,yy,yz / zx,zy,zz are the new X, Y and Z axes.

```basic
; Models from .glb files are turned to face the other way when loaded
Graphics3D 800, 600
LoaderMatrix "glb", -1, 0, 0,  0, 1, 0,  0, 0, -1
model = LoadMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
```

### LoadAnimSeq

`LoadAnimSeq(entity%, file$)` → integer

Loads the animations from another model file and adds them to the animated model entity (retargeted to its skeleton); returns the number of the first new sequence, or -1 on failure.

```basic
; Add the animations from a second file to a character
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
new_seq = LoadAnimSeq(hero, "examples/media/Fox.glb")
If new_seq = -1 Then Print "Could not load animations" Else Print "New sequences start at " + new_seq
```

### LoadMesh

`LoadMesh(file$, [parent%=0])` → integer

Loads a glTF (.gltf/.glb), FBX or saved scene file as a single entity and returns its handle, or 0 on failure; parent is optional. Older formats (.b3d, .x, .3ds) are not supported.

```basic
; Load a model as one entity; 0 means it could not be loaded
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
If fox = 0 Then RuntimeError "Fox.glb not found"
PositionEntity fox, 0, 0, 5
```

### LoadMeshAsync

`LoadMeshAsync(file$, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Starts loading a model file in the background and returns its entity at once; the model appears in it when loaded. parent is optional.

```basic
; Start loading and carry on; the model appears when it is ready
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadMeshAsync("examples/media/Fox.glb")
PositionEntity fox, 0, 0, 5   ; usable straight away
```

### EntityLoaded

`EntityLoaded(entity%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if the model of entity has finished loading (always for other entities), 0 while it is still loading, -1 if loading failed.

```basic
; Wait until a background load has finished (-1 means it failed)
Graphics3D 800, 600
model = LoadMeshAsync("examples/media/Fox.glb")
While EntityLoaded(model) = 0
    Delay 10
Wend
If EntityLoaded(model) = -1 Then Print "Load failed" Else Print "Ready"
```

### PendingLoads

`PendingLoads()` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the number of background model and texture loads still in progress.

```basic
; Show a loading message while background loads are running
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadMeshAsync("examples/media/Fox.glb")
man = LoadMeshAsync("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
While PendingLoads() > 0
    Print "Still loading: " + PendingLoads()
    Delay 100
Wend
```

### LoadAnimMesh

`LoadAnimMesh(file$, [parent%=0])` → integer

Loads an animated model (with its bone hierarchy and animations) from file and returns it as an entity, optionally attached to parent. Returns 0 if the file cannot be loaded.

```basic
; Load an animated character and play its first animation in a loop
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Animate fox, 1
```

### Animate

`Animate entity%, [mode%=1], [speed#=1], [sequence%=0], [transition#=0]`

Starts animating entity: mode 0 stops, 1 loops, 2 ping-pongs, 3 plays once; speed scales playback, sequence picks the animation sequence and transition is the number of frames to blend from the current pose.

```basic
; mode 1 = loop; speed 1.5 = faster than normal; sequence 2; blend over 10 frames
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Animate fox, 1, 1.5, 2, 10
```

### SetAnimTime

`SetAnimTime entity%, time#, [sequence%=0]`

Jumps entity's animation to time (in frames) in the given sequence.

```basic
; Jump to frame 12 of sequence 0 without playing
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
SetAnimTime fox, 12, 0
```

### Animating

`Animating(entity%)` → integer

Returns 1 if entity is currently animating, otherwise 0.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Animate fox, 3   ; play once
If Animating(fox) Then Print "Still playing" Else Print "Finished"
```

### AnimTime

`AnimTime(entity%)` → float

Returns the current time (frame) of entity's animation.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Animate fox, 1
UpdateWorld
Print "Frame: " + AnimTime(fox)
```

### AnimSeq

`AnimSeq(entity%)` → integer

Returns the animation sequence entity is currently playing.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Animate fox, 1, 1, 1
Print "Playing sequence " + AnimSeq(fox)
```

### AnimLength

`AnimLength(entity%)` → integer

Returns the length in frames of entity's current animation sequence.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Print "Current animation is " + AnimLength(fox) + " frames long"
```

### ExtractAnimSeq

`ExtractAnimSeq(entity%, first_frame%, last_frame%, [anim_seq%=0])` → integer

Creates a new animation sequence from frames first_frame to last_frame of sequence anim_seq and returns the new sequence's number.

```basic
; Cut frames 1 to 20 out of the main sequence into a new one
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
walk = ExtractAnimSeq(fox, 1, 20, 0)
Animate fox, 1, 1, walk
```

### CountAnimSeqs

`CountAnimSeqs(entity%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many animation sequences entity has (0 if it is not animated).

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Print "The fox has " + CountAnimSeqs(fox) + " animations"
```

### AnimLayer

`AnimLayer entity%, layer%, sequence%, [mode%=1], [speed#=1], [weight#=1], [bone$=""]`

**(ext)** Plays sequence on animation layer (1-8) of entity on top of its main animation, blended in by weight (0-1); mode and speed work like Animate. bone limits the layer to that bone and everything below it ("" = the whole model), e.g. the upper body shooting while the legs run; a whole-body layer at weight 0.3 mixes the two animations 70/30.

```basic
; Play sequence 2 on layer 1 over the whole body at half strength
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
Animate fox, 1, 1, 1
AnimLayer fox, 1, 2, 1, 1, 0.5, ""   ; layer, sequence, mode, speed, weight, bone
```

### AnimLayerWeight

`AnimLayerWeight entity%, layer%, weight#`

**(ext)** Changes how strongly animation layer of entity is blended in, from 0 (not at all) to 1 (fully); change it a little each frame to fade a layer in or out.

```basic
; Fade a layer in a little each frame
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
AnimLayer fox, 1, 2, 1, 1, 0
weight = 0.0
While weight < 1
    weight = weight + 0.05
    AnimLayerWeight fox, 1, weight
    UpdateWorld
Wend
```

### AnimLayerStop

`AnimLayerStop entity%, layer%`

**(ext)** Removes animation layer from entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
AnimLayer fox, 1, 2
AnimLayerStop fox, 1
```

### CreateBlendSpace

`CreateBlendSpace()` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an empty blend space and returns its handle; add sequences to it with BlendSpacePoint and play it with AnimBlend or AddBlendState.

```basic
; A blend space mixes animations by speed
Graphics3D 800, 600
space = CreateBlendSpace()
BlendSpacePoint space, 0, 0   ; sequence 0 at speed 0
BlendSpacePoint space, 1, 3   ; sequence 1 at speed 3
```

### BlendSpacePoint

`BlendSpacePoint space%, sequence%, x#, [y#=0]`

**(ext)** Places animation sequence at point x (and y) of blend space, e.g. idle at 0, walk at 1 and run at 3 for speed. Points that all share one y form a line; otherwise they form a plane (speed and direction).

```basic
; idle (sequence 0) at 0, walk (1) at 1, run (2) at 3
Graphics3D 800, 600
space = CreateBlendSpace()
BlendSpacePoint space, 0, 0
BlendSpacePoint space, 1, 1
BlendSpacePoint space, 2, 3
```

### AnimBlend

`AnimBlend entity%, space%, x#, [y#=0], [speed#=1], [transition#=0]`

**(ext)** Plays blend space on entity, mixing its sequences for the point x,y (call it again each frame with new values; the mix changes smoothly). All sequences stay at the same point of their cycle so steps stay in step. speed scales playback; transition crossfades from the previous animation over that many frames.

```basic
; Call every frame with the character's current speed
Graphics3D 800, 600
fox = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
space = CreateBlendSpace()
BlendSpacePoint space, 0, 0
BlendSpacePoint space, 1, 3
speed = 1.5
AnimBlend fox, space, speed
```

### AddAnimState

`AddAnimState(entity%, name$, sequence%, [mode%=1], [speed#=1])` → integer

**(ext)** Adds a state called name to entity's animation state machine that plays sequence with mode (1 loop, 2 ping-pong, 3 once) and speed; returns the state's number.

```basic
; States of an animation state machine: idle loops, jump plays once
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
idle = AddAnimState(hero, "idle", 0, 1)   ; loop sequence 0
jump = AddAnimState(hero, "jump", 1, 3)   ; play sequence 1 once
```

### AddBlendState

`AddBlendState(entity%, name$, space%, param_x$, [param_y$=""], [speed#=1])` → integer

**(ext)** Adds a state called name to entity's animation state machine that plays blend space, its position taken each frame from the parameters param_x (and param_y) set with SetAnimParam; returns the state's number.

```basic
; A state that mixes animations using the "speed" parameter
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
space = CreateBlendSpace()
BlendSpacePoint space, 0, 0
BlendSpacePoint space, 1, 3
move = AddBlendState(hero, "move", space, "speed")
SetAnimParam hero, "speed", 2
```

### AddAnimTransition

`AddAnimTransition entity%, from$, to$, [condition$=""], [duration#=10]`

**(ext)** Makes entity's state machine switch from state from ("*" or "" for any state) to state to when condition holds, crossfading over duration frames. condition is "" (at once), "end" (the state's one-shot animation has finished) or a comparison of a parameter such as "speed > 0.5" (operators > < >= <= = <>).

```basic
; Go from idle to run when speed rises above 0.5, taking 8 frames to blend
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
idle = AddAnimState(hero, "idle", 0)
run = AddAnimState(hero, "run", 1)
AddAnimTransition hero, "idle", "run", "speed > 0.5", 8
AddAnimTransition hero, "run", "idle", "speed <= 0.5", 8
```

### SetAnimParam

`SetAnimParam entity%, name$, value#`

**(ext)** Sets parameter name of entity's animation state machine to value; transitions and blend states read it.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
idle = AddAnimState(hero, "idle", 0)
StartAnimStates hero, "idle"
SetAnimParam hero, "speed", 1.0   ; transitions read this value
```

### AnimParam

`AnimParam(entity%, name$)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the value of parameter name of entity's animation state machine (0 if never set).

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
SetAnimParam hero, "speed", 2.5
Print "Speed parameter: " + AnimParam(hero, "speed")
```

### StartAnimStates

`StartAnimStates entity%, state$`

**(ext)** Starts entity's animation state machine in state; from then on UpdateWorld takes its transitions by itself.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
idle = AddAnimState(hero, "idle", 0)
StartAnimStates hero, "idle"   ; UpdateWorld now runs the state machine
```

### AnimStateName

`AnimStateName(entity%)` → string

**(ext)** Returns the name of the state entity's animation state machine is in, or "" if it has none.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/Fox.glb")
idle = AddAnimState(hero, "idle", 0)
StartAnimStates hero, "idle"
Print "State: " + AnimStateName(hero)
```

### AnimRootMotion

`AnimRootMotion entity%, [bone$=""], [enable%=1], [keep_y%=1]`

**(ext)** Root motion: the movement of the root bone in entity's animation moves the entity instead, so its feet do not slide (for animations made moving forward rather than in place). bone picks the root bone ("" = the model's topmost moving bone); enable 0 turns it off; keep_y 1 leaves up and down movement in the animation.

```basic
; Let the animation's own forward movement move the entity (bone "" = automatic)
Graphics3D 800, 600
hero = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
AnimRootMotion hero, "", 1, 0   ; on; keep_y 0 ignores up and down movement
Animate hero, 1
```

### CreateTrack

`CreateTrack(entity%, property$)` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a property track that animates property of entity and returns its handle. property is position, rotation, scale, color, alpha, shininess, emissive, lightcolor, lightrange, camerazoom, fogrange, texture (offset of the entity's first texture) or texturerotation; add keys with TrackKey.

```basic
; Animate a cube's position over time
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
track = CreateTrack(cube, "position")
TrackKey track, 0, 0, 0, 0
TrackKey track, 60, 0, 5, 0   ; at frame 60 (one second) it is 5 units up
```

### TrackKey

`TrackKey track%, frame#, value1#, [value2#=0], [value3#=0]`

**(ext)** Sets the value of track at frame (1/60 s each): value1 to value3 as the property needs (x,y,z; red,green,blue; near,far; u,v; or one value). Values between keys change in a straight line.

```basic
; Fade a cube out: alpha goes from 1 to 0 over two seconds
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
track = CreateTrack(cube, "alpha")
TrackKey track, 0, 1
TrackKey track, 120, 0
```

### PlayTrack

`PlayTrack track%, [mode%=1], [speed#=1]`

**(ext)** Plays track from its start: mode 1 loops, 2 goes back and forth, 3 plays once, 0 stops; speed scales it (negative plays backwards). Tracks advance in UpdateWorld.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
track = CreateTrack(cube, "position")
TrackKey track, 0, 0, 0, 0
TrackKey track, 60, 0, 5, 0
PlayTrack track, 2, 1   ; mode 2 = back and forth, normal speed
```

### SetTrackTime

`SetTrackTime track%, frame#`

**(ext)** Stops track and sets its property to the value at frame.

```basic
; Jump a track to frame 30
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
track = CreateTrack(cube, "position")
TrackKey track, 0, 0, 0, 0
TrackKey track, 60, 0, 5, 0
SetTrackTime track, 30   ; the cube is now at y = 2.5
```

### TrackTime

`TrackTime(track%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the current frame of track.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
track = CreateTrack(cube, "position")
TrackKey track, 0, 0, 0, 0
TrackKey track, 60, 0, 5, 0
PlayTrack track, 1
UpdateWorld
Print "Track is at frame " + TrackTime(track)
```

### TrackPlaying

`TrackPlaying(track%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 while track is playing, 0 when stopped or finished.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
track = CreateTrack(cube, "position")
TrackKey track, 0, 0, 0, 0
TrackKey track, 60, 0, 5, 0
PlayTrack track, 3   ; play once
If TrackPlaying(track) Then Print "Moving" Else Print "Finished"
```

### FreeTrack

`FreeTrack track%`

**(ext)** Deletes track; the property keeps its last value.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
cube = CreateCube()
track = CreateTrack(cube, "position")
TrackKey track, 0, 0, 0, 0
TrackKey track, 60, 0, 5, 0
FreeTrack track   ; the cube stays where it is
```

## Sprites

### CreateSprite

`CreateSprite([parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a flat sprite that faces the camera and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; A flat picture that always faces the camera
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
coin = CreateSprite()
tex = LoadTexture("examples/media/coin.png")
EntityTexture coin, tex
PositionEntity coin, 0, 0, 5
```

### LoadSprite

`LoadSprite(file$, [texture_flags%=1], [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a sprite with the texture in file (texture_flags default 1) and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; Create a sprite and load its picture in one step
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
coin = LoadSprite("examples/media/coin.png")
PositionEntity coin, 0, 0, 5
```

### SpriteViewMode

`SpriteViewMode sprite%, view_mode%`

Sets how sprite faces the camera: 1 always faces it, 2 fixed (does not turn), 3 turns only around its Y axis, 4 upright but turns to face the camera.

```basic
; 3 = turns only around its Y axis, good for trees
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
tree = LoadSprite("examples/media/tree.png")
SpriteViewMode tree, 3
PositionEntity tree, 0, 1, 8
```

### RotateSprite

`RotateSprite sprite%, angle#`

Rotates sprite by angle degrees around the view direction.

```basic
; Spin a sprite 45 degrees around the view direction
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
ring = LoadSprite("examples/media/ring.png")
PositionEntity ring, 0, 0, 5
RotateSprite ring, 45
```

### ScaleSprite

`ScaleSprite sprite%, x_scale#, y_scale#`

Sets the size of sprite to x_scale by y_scale (a scale of 1 is 2 units wide).

```basic
; A scale of 1 is 2 units wide, so this sprite is 4 wide and 2 tall
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
ship = LoadSprite("examples/media/ship.png")
PositionEntity ship, 0, 0, 8
ScaleSprite ship, 2, 1
```

### HandleSprite

`HandleSprite sprite%, x_handle#, y_handle#`

Sets the handle of sprite, the point it is centred on, as x_handle, y_handle from -1 to 1 (0, 0 is the middle).

```basic
; Put the handle at the bottom middle so the sprite stands on the ground
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
tree = LoadSprite("examples/media/tree.png")
HandleSprite tree, 0, -1
PositionEntity tree, 0, 0, 8
```

## Terrain

### CreateTerrain

`CreateTerrain(grid_size%, [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a flat terrain of grid_size by grid_size squares (a power of 2) and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; A 128 by 128 terrain (the size must be a power of 2), stretched out
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
ScaleEntity land, 4, 20, 4
PositionEntity land, -256, 0, -256
```

### LoadTerrain

`LoadTerrain(file$, [parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a terrain from a heightmap image file (brightness is height) and returns its handle; parent is optional.

```basic
; Brightness of the heightmap picture becomes height
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = LoadTerrain("heightmap.png")
ScaleEntity land, 2, 30, 2
```

### TerrainSize

`TerrainSize(terrain%)` → integer

Returns the grid size of terrain.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(256)
Print "Grid size: " + TerrainSize(land)   ; 256
```

### TerrainHeight

`TerrainHeight(terrain%, grid_x%, grid_z%)` → float

Returns the height of terrain at grid point grid_x, grid_z, from 0 to 1.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
ModifyTerrain land, 10, 10, 0.5
Print "Height at 10,10: " + TerrainHeight(land, 10, 10)   ; 0 to 1
```

### ModifyTerrain

`ModifyTerrain terrain%, grid_x%, grid_z%, height#, [realtime%=0]`

Sets the height of terrain at grid point grid_x, grid_z to height (0 to 1); realtime is accepted for compatibility.

```basic
; Raise a small hill (heights are 0 to 1)
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
For x = 60 To 68
    For z = 60 To 68
        ModifyTerrain land, x, z, 0.8
    Next
Next
```

### TerrainY

`TerrainY(terrain%, x#, y#, z#)` → float

Returns the world Y height of terrain at world position x, y, z.

```basic
; Stand a player on the ground at x=10, z=20
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
ScaleEntity land, 4, 20, 4
player = CreateCube()
ground = TerrainY(land, 10, 0, 20)
PositionEntity player, 10, ground + 1, 20
```

### TerrainX

`TerrainX(terrain%, x#, y#, z#)` → float

Returns the world X of the terrain point under world position x, y, z.

```basic
; The world X of the terrain point under a position
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
ScaleEntity land, 4, 20, 4
Print "Terrain X: " + TerrainX(land, 10, 0, 20)
```

### TerrainZ

`TerrainZ(terrain%, x#, y#, z#)` → float

Returns the world Z of the terrain point under world position x, y, z.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
ScaleEntity land, 4, 20, 4
Print "Terrain Z: " + TerrainZ(land, 10, 0, 20)
```

### TerrainDetail

`TerrainDetail terrain%, detail_level%, [vertex_morph%=0]`

Sets terrain level of detail: about detail_level triangles are drawn, with full detail near the camera and fewer, larger squares further away (chosen every render). 0 draws the full grid (the default). vertex_morph is accepted. Collisions and picks always use the full grid.

```basic
; Draw about 3000 triangles, fewer further from the camera
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(256)
TerrainDetail land, 3000
```

### TerrainShading

`TerrainShading terrain%, enable%`

Accepted for compatibility; terrain shading is handled by the GPU.

```basic
; Kept so old programs still run; it has no effect
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
TerrainShading land, 1
```

### TerrainLayer

`TerrainLayer terrain%, texture%, [layer%=0]`

**(ext)** Gives terrain a texture for layer 0 (the base, the same as EntityTexture) to 3. Layers 1 to 3 are painted over the base with PaintTerrain or LoadTerrainLayers (grass, rock, sand, snow...); scale them with ScaleTexture.

```basic
; Base grass with a rock layer painted on top
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
grass = LoadTexture("grass.png")
rock = LoadTexture("rock.png")
TerrainLayer land, grass, 0
TerrainLayer land, rock, 1
ScaleTexture rock, 0.1, 0.1
```

### PaintTerrain

`PaintTerrain terrain%, layer%, x#, z#, radius#, [strength#=1]`

**(ext)** Paints layer (0-3) of terrain with a soft round brush at world position x, z: radius in world units, strength 0-1 (how much a single stroke adds; negative removes). Painting a layer also uncovers it from the layers above it; painting layer 0 brings the base back.

```basic
; Paint a round patch of layer 1 at world position 50, 50
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
rock = LoadTexture("rock.png")
TerrainLayer land, rock, 1
PaintTerrain land, 1, 50, 50, 10, 1   ; layer, x, z, radius, strength
```

### ErodeTerrain

`ErodeTerrain terrain%, [droplets%=50000], [seed%=0]`

**(ext)** Erodes terrain with simulated rain: droplets raindrops each run downhill, taking soil from the slopes and leaving it where they slow down, which carves gullies and ridges into mountains and fills valleys. More droplets erode more (try 50000 to 300000 for a 256 terrain); the same seed gives the same result. Set the terrain's ScaleEntity first: steepness depends on its proportions.

```basic
; Carve gullies into a hilly terrain; the same seed gives the same result
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(256)
ScaleEntity land, 2, 40, 2
ErodeTerrain land, 100000, 42
```

### ThermalErodeTerrain

`ThermalErodeTerrain terrain%, [iterations%=30], [max_angle#=40]`

**(ext)** Lets slopes of terrain steeper than max_angle degrees crumble down until they are no steeper, repeated iterations times: softens sharp peaks and builds scree at the foot of cliffs. Set the terrain's ScaleEntity first.

```basic
; Crumble every slope steeper than 35 degrees, 20 times over
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(256)
ScaleEntity land, 2, 40, 2
ThermalErodeTerrain land, 20, 35
```

### ModifyTerrainLayer

`ModifyTerrainLayer terrain%, grid_x%, grid_z%, layer%, weight#`

**(ext)** Sets how much painted layer (1-3) covers terrain at grid point grid_x, grid_z, from 0 to 1, without changing the other layers (like ModifyTerrain for heights; layers above still cover it). Useful for painting by height or slope in a loop.

```basic
; Cover a grid point fully with layer 1
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
rock = LoadTexture("rock.png")
TerrainLayer land, rock, 1
ModifyTerrainLayer land, 20, 20, 1, 1.0
```

### TerrainLayerWeight

`TerrainLayerWeight(terrain%, layer%, x#, z#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how much of layer (0-3) shows on terrain at world position x, z, from 0 to 1 (for footstep sounds, placing trees on grass, slowing down in sand).

```basic
; React to the ground type under a position
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
rock = LoadTexture("rock.png")
TerrainLayer land, rock, 1
If TerrainLayerWeight(land, 1, 10, 10) > 0.5 Then Print "Stepping on rock"
```

### LoadTerrainLayers

`LoadTerrainLayers terrain%, file$`

**(ext)** Paints terrain's layers 1, 2 and 3 from the red, green and blue of an image file (a splat map, as painted in another program), stretched over the whole terrain; the top of the image is the far (+Z) edge.

```basic
; Red, green and blue of the splat map paint layers 1, 2 and 3
Graphics3D 800, 600
land = CreateTerrain(128)
LoadTerrainLayers land, "splatmap.png"
```

## Mirrors and sky

### SkyBox

`SkyBox texture%`

**(ext)** Shows a cube map texture (loaded with flag 128, six square faces side by side) as the sky; 0 removes the sky.

```basic
; Show a cube map as the sky
sky = LoadTexture("sky_cube.png", 1 + 128)
SkyBox sky
SkyBox 0      ; remove the sky
```

### CreateMirror

`CreateMirror([parent%=0])` → integer

Creates a mirror entity that reflects the world below a flat plane and returns its handle; parent is an optional parent entity.

```basic
; A mirror floor that reflects the world above it
mirror = CreateMirror()
PositionEntity mirror, 0, -1, 0
ball = CreateSphere()
```

## Collisions

### CollisionSurface

`CollisionSurface(entity%, index%)` → integer

Returns 1 if collision number index (from 1 to CountCollisions) of entity hit a mesh triangle, otherwise 0.

```basic
; After UpdateWorld, ask whether the player hit a mesh surface
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
level = CreateCube()
EntityType level, 2
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then
    If CollisionSurface(player, 1) Then Print "Hit a surface"
EndIf
```

### CollisionTriangle

`CollisionTriangle(entity%, index%)` → integer

Returns the index of the triangle hit in collision number index (from 1 to CountCollisions) of entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
level = CreateCube()
EntityType level, 2
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then Print "Triangle: " + CollisionTriangle(player, 1)
```

### EntityType

`EntityType entity%, collision_type%, [recursive%=0]`

Sets the collision type number of entity, and of its children too when recursive is 1. Type 0 means no collisions.

```basic
; Give collision types: 1 = player, 2 = walls
Const TYPE_PLAYER = 1
Const TYPE_WALL = 2
Graphics3D 800, 600
player = CreateSphere()
wall = CreateCube()
EntityType player, TYPE_PLAYER
EntityType wall, TYPE_WALL
```

### GetEntityType

`GetEntityType(entity%)` → integer

Returns the collision type number of entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
Print "Collision type: " + GetEntityType(player)   ; 1
```

### EntityRadius

`EntityRadius entity%, x_radius#, [y_radius#=0]`

Sets the collision radius of entity; give y_radius for an ellipsoid with a different vertical radius.

```basic
; An ellipsoid: 1 wide but 2 tall
Graphics3D 800, 600
player = CreateCube()
EntityRadius player, 1, 2
```

### EntityBox

`EntityBox entity%, x#, y#, z#, width#, height#, depth#`

Sets the collision box of entity: corner x,y,z and size width,height,depth, for box collisions.

```basic
; A collision box starting at -1,-1,-1 and 2 units in every direction
Graphics3D 800, 600
crate = CreateCube()
EntityType crate, 2
EntityBox crate, -1, -1, -1, 2, 2, 2
```

### Collisions

`Collisions src_type%, dest_type%, method%, response%`

Enables collisions from entities of type src_type to type dest_type. method is 1 ellipsoid-ellipsoid, 2 ellipsoid-polygon, 3 ellipsoid-box; response is 1 stop, 2 slide, 3 slide without sliding down slopes.

```basic
; Players (1) hit walls (2) using ellipsoid-polygon (2) and slide (2)
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
```

### ClearCollisions

`ClearCollisions`

Removes all collision rules set with Collisions.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
ClearCollisions   ; no entities collide any more
```

### ResetEntity

`ResetEntity entity%`

Forgets entity's previous position so the next UpdateWorld does not collide it along the way, for teleporting.

```basic
; Teleport the player without it colliding along the way
Graphics3D 800, 600
player = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity player, 50, 0, 50
ResetEntity player
```

### EntityCollided

`EntityCollided(entity%, type%)` → integer

Returns the entity of collision type type that entity collided with in the last UpdateWorld, or 0.

```basic
; Did the player touch a coin (type 3) during the last UpdateWorld?
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 3, 1, 1
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
coin = CreateSphere()
EntityType coin, 3
EntityRadius coin, 1
UpdateWorld
hit = EntityCollided(player, 3)
If hit <> 0 Then FreeEntity hit
```

### CountCollisions

`CountCollisions(entity%)` → integer

Returns how many collisions entity had in the last UpdateWorld.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
Print "Collisions: " + CountCollisions(player)
```

### CollisionX

`CollisionX(entity%, index%)` → float

Returns the world X coordinate of collision index (1 to CountCollisions) of entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then Print "Hit at x = " + CollisionX(player, 1)
```

### CollisionY

`CollisionY(entity%, index%)` → float

Returns the world Y coordinate of collision index of entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then Print "Hit at y = " + CollisionY(player, 1)
```

### CollisionZ

`CollisionZ(entity%, index%)` → float

Returns the world Z coordinate of collision index of entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then Print "Hit at z = " + CollisionZ(player, 1)
```

### CollisionNX

`CollisionNX(entity%, index%)` → float

Returns the X component of the surface normal of collision index of entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then Print "Surface normal x = " + CollisionNX(player, 1)
```

### CollisionNY

`CollisionNY(entity%, index%)` → float

Returns the Y component of the surface normal of collision index of entity.

```basic
; A normal with y near 1 means the player is standing on a floor
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then
    If CollisionNY(player, 1) > 0.7 Then Print "On the ground"
EndIf
```

### CollisionNZ

`CollisionNZ(entity%, index%)` → float

Returns the Z component of the surface normal of collision index of entity.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then Print "Surface normal z = " + CollisionNZ(player, 1)
```

### CollisionTime

`CollisionTime(entity%, index%)` → float

Returns the time (0 to 1 along the movement) at which collision index of entity happened.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
If CountCollisions(player) > 0 Then Print "Hit " + CollisionTime(player, 1) + " of the way along the move"
```

### CollisionEntity

`CollisionEntity(entity%, index%)` → integer

Returns the entity hit in collision index of entity.

```basic
; Find out what the player ran into
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
For i = 1 To CountCollisions(player)
    Print "Hit entity " + CollisionEntity(player, i)
Next
```

## Picking

### EntityPickMode

`EntityPickMode entity%, pick_geometry%, [obscurer%=1]`

Makes entity pickable: pick_geometry 0 none, 1 sphere, 2 polygon, 3 box. With obscurer 1 it also blocks EntityVisible checks.

```basic
; Make the crate pickable by its polygons (2) and let it block sight lines
Graphics3D 800, 600
crate = CreateCube()
EntityPickMode crate, 2, 1
```

### LinePick

`LinePick(x#, y#, z#, dx#, dy#, dz#, [radius#=0])` → integer

Picks along a line from x,y,z in direction and length dx,dy,dz, optionally with a thickness of radius. Returns the picked entity, or 0.

```basic
; Cast a ray from the origin straight along +Z for 100 units
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
hit = LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100)
If hit <> 0 Then Print "Hit at z = " + PickedZ()
```

### EntityPick

`EntityPick(entity%, range#)` → integer

Picks from entity straight ahead up to range units. Returns the picked entity, or 0.

```basic
; Shoot straight ahead from the player, up to 50 units
Graphics3D 800, 600
player = CreateCube()
target = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity target, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode target, 1
hit = EntityPick(player, 50)
If hit = target Then Print "Target in the sights"
```

### CameraPick

`CameraPick(camera%, viewport_x#, viewport_y#)` → integer

Picks the entity under screen point viewport_x,viewport_y of camera. Returns the picked entity, or 0.

```basic
; Which object is under the mouse?
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
cube = CreateCube()
PositionEntity cube, 0, 0, 5
EntityPickMode cube, 2
picked = CameraPick(camera, MouseX(), MouseY())
If picked <> 0 Then Print "Mouse is over entity " + picked
```

### PickedX

`PickedX()` → float

Returns the world X coordinate of the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Hit at x = " + PickedX()
```

### PickedY

`PickedY()` → float

Returns the world Y coordinate of the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Hit at y = " + PickedY()
```

### PickedZ

`PickedZ()` → float

Returns the world Z coordinate of the last pick.

```basic
; Place a marker where the ray hit
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
marker = CreateSphere()
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then PositionEntity marker, PickedX(), PickedY(), PickedZ()
```

### PickedNX

`PickedNX()` → float

Returns the X component of the surface normal at the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Normal x = " + PickedNX()
```

### PickedNY

`PickedNY()` → float

Returns the Y component of the surface normal at the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Normal y = " + PickedNY()
```

### PickedNZ

`PickedNZ()` → float

Returns the Z component of the surface normal at the last pick.

```basic
; A wall facing us has a normal pointing back along -Z
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Normal z = " + PickedNZ()
```

### PickedTime

`PickedTime()` → float

Returns the time (0 to 1 along the line) of the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Hit " + PickedTime() + " of the way along the line"   ; 0 to 1
```

### PickedEntity

`PickedEntity()` → integer

Returns the entity hit by the last pick, or 0.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
LinePick 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100
If PickedEntity() = wall Then Print "Hit the wall"
```

### PickedTriangle

`PickedTriangle()` → integer

Returns the index of the triangle hit by the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Triangle " + PickedTriangle()
```

### PickedSurface

`PickedSurface()` → integer

Returns the surface hit by the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then
    surf = PickedSurface()
    Print "Surface " + surf + " has " + CountTriangles(surf) + " triangles"
EndIf
```

### CameraProject

`CameraProject camera%, x#, y#, z#`

Projects world point x,y,z through camera to the screen; read the result with ProjectedX/Y/Z.

```basic
; Find where a 3D point lands on the screen, e.g. for a health bar above an enemy
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
enemy = CreateCube()
PositionEntity enemy, 2, 1, 10
CameraProject camera, EntityX(enemy), EntityY(enemy) + 2, EntityZ(enemy)
Print "Screen position: " + ProjectedX() + ", " + ProjectedY()
```

### ProjectedX

`ProjectedX()` → float

Returns the screen X coordinate from the last CameraProject.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraProject camera, 0, 0, 10
Print "Screen x = " + ProjectedX()
```

### ProjectedY

`ProjectedY()` → float

Returns the screen Y coordinate from the last CameraProject.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraProject camera, 0, 0, 10
Print "Screen y = " + ProjectedY()
```

### ProjectedZ

`ProjectedZ()` → float

Returns the depth from the last CameraProject; 0 means the point is behind the camera.

```basic
; 0 means the point is behind the camera
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraProject camera, 0, 0, -10
If ProjectedZ() = 0 Then Print "Behind the camera" Else Print "In front"
```

### EntityInView

`EntityInView(entity%, camera%)` → integer

Returns 1 if entity is inside camera's view.

```basic
; Only draw an enemy's health bar when it is on screen
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
enemy = CreateCube()
PositionEntity enemy, 0, 0, 10
If EntityInView(enemy, camera) Then Print "Enemy visible"
```

### EntityVisible

`EntityVisible(src_entity%, dest_entity%)` → integer

Returns 1 if nothing pickable as an obscurer blocks the line between src_entity and dest_entity.

```basic
; Can the guard see the player, or is a wall in the way?
Graphics3D 800, 600
guard = CreateCube()
player = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity player, 0, 0, 20
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2, 1   ; obscurer = 1
If EntityVisible(guard, player) Then Print "Spotted!" Else Print "Hidden"
```

## 3D physics

### EntityPhysics

`EntityPhysics entity%, [body_type%=1], [shape%=0], [mass#=1]`

**(ext)** Makes entity a rigid body: body_type 1 dynamic, 2 static, 3 kinematic; shape 0 automatic, 1 box, 2 sphere, 3 capsule, 4 cylinder, 5 mesh, 6 convex hull; mass in kilograms.

```basic
; A dynamic box that falls, and a static floor it lands on
floor = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity floor, 10, 0.5, 10
EntityPhysics floor, 2, 1            ; body_type 2 = static, shape 1 = box
crate = CreateCube()
PositionEntity crate, 0, 5, 0
EntityPhysics crate, 1, 1, 2.5       ; dynamic, box shape, 2.5 kg
```

### PhysicsGravity

`PhysicsGravity x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sets the world's gravity vector x, y, z, in units per second squared.

```basic
; Moon gravity: weaker than the default pull downwards
PhysicsGravity 0, -1.6, 0
```

### EntityMass

`EntityMass entity%, mass#`

**(ext)** Sets the mass of entity's physics body.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
EntityMass ball, 10                  ; now weighs 10 kilograms
```

### EntityFriction

`EntityFriction entity%, friction#`

**(ext)** Sets the friction of entity's physics body (0 is slippery).

```basic
floor = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics floor, 2, 1
EntityFriction floor, 0.02           ; almost no friction: like ice
```

### EntityBounce

`EntityBounce entity%, bounce#`

**(ext)** Sets how bouncy entity's physics body is, from 0 (no bounce) to 1.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
EntityBounce ball, 0.8               ; 0 = no bounce, 1 = bounces back fully
```

### EntityDamping

`EntityDamping entity%, linear#, angular#`

**(ext)** Sets the linear and angular damping of entity's physics body, which slows its movement and spin.

```basic
; Make a ball slow down quickly as it rolls and spins
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
EntityDamping ball, 0.5, 0.8         ; linear damping, angular damping
```

### EntityGravityScale

`EntityGravityScale entity%, scale#`

**(ext)** Scales how much gravity affects entity's physics body (0 none, 1 normal).

```basic
balloon = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics balloon, 1, 2
EntityGravityScale balloon, 0.1      ; falls slowly: 0 = none, 1 = normal
```

### LockRotation

`LockRotation entity%, x%, y%, z%`

**(ext)** Stops entity's physics body rotating about each axis whose flag x, y, z is 1.

```basic
; A character body that can turn around Y but must not tip over
player = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics player, 1, 3
LockRotation player, 1, 0, 1         ; 1 = locked about X and Z, 0 = free about Y
```

### ApplyForce

`ApplyForce entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Applies a continuous force x, y, z to entity's physics body.

```basic
; A rocket engine: push upwards a little every frame
rocket = CreateCone()
EntityPhysics rocket, 1, 4, 2
For frame = 1 To 60
    ApplyForce rocket, 0, 40, 0
    UpdateWorld
Next
```

### ApplyImpulse

`ApplyImpulse entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Gives entity's physics body an instant push x, y, z.

```basic
; Kick the ball forward once
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
ApplyImpulse ball, 0, 0, 5
```

### ApplyImpulseAt

`ApplyImpulseAt entity%, x#, y#, z#, point_x#, point_y#, point_z#`

**(ext)** Gives entity's physics body an instant push x, y, z at the world point point_x, point_y, point_z, which can also make it spin.

```basic
; Hit the crate near its top edge, so it tips over as well as moving
crate = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics crate, 1, 1
ApplyImpulseAt crate, 3, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0   ; push x,y,z at the world point 0,1,0
```

### ApplyTorque

`ApplyTorque entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Applies a continuous turning force x, y, z to entity's physics body.

```basic
; Keep turning a wheel about its Z axis
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
For frame = 1 To 60
    ApplyTorque wheel, 0, 0, 5
    UpdateWorld
Next
```

### ApplyTorqueImpulse

`ApplyTorqueImpulse entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Gives entity's physics body an instant spin x, y, z.

```basic
; Give a coin a quick flip
coin = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics coin, 1, 4
ApplyTorqueImpulse coin, 2, 0, 0
```

### SetVelocity

`SetVelocity entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sets the velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
; Shoot a bullet straight ahead at 30 units per second
bullet = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics bullet, 1, 2
SetVelocity bullet, 0, 0, 30
```

### SetAngularVelocity

`SetAngularVelocity entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sets the angular velocity of entity's physics body.

```basic
; Start a wheel spinning
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
SetAngularVelocity wheel, 0, 0, 10
```

### VelocityX

`VelocityX(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
SetVelocity ball, 4, 0, 0
Print "Sideways speed: " + VelocityX(ball)
```

### VelocityY

`VelocityY(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
If VelocityY(ball) < 0 Then Print "The ball is falling"
```

### VelocityZ

`VelocityZ(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Z velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
car = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics car, 1, 1
SetVelocity car, 0, 0, 12
Print "Forward speed: " + VelocityZ(car)
```

### AngularVelocityX

`AngularVelocityX(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the angular velocity of entity's physics body about the X axis.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
SetAngularVelocity ball, 3, 0, 0
Print "Spin about X: " + AngularVelocityX(ball)
```

### AngularVelocityY

`AngularVelocityY(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the angular velocity of entity's physics body about the Y axis.

```basic
top = CreateCone()
EntityPhysics top, 1, 4
SetAngularVelocity top, 0, 8, 0
Print "Spin about Y: " + AngularVelocityY(top)
```

### AngularVelocityZ

`AngularVelocityZ(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the angular velocity of entity's physics body about the Z axis.

```basic
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
SetAngularVelocity wheel, 0, 0, 5
Print "Spin about Z: " + AngularVelocityZ(wheel)
```

### EntityTouching

`EntityTouching(entity%, other%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if entity's physics body is touching other, otherwise 0.

```basic
floor = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity floor, 10, 0.5, 10
EntityPhysics floor, 2, 1
ball = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity ball, 0, 2, 0
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
If EntityTouching(ball, floor) Then Print "On the ground" Else Print "In the air"
```

### CountContacts

`CountContacts(entity%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many physics bodies entity is touching.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
Print "The ball touches " + CountContacts(ball) + " bodies"
```

### ContactEntity

`ContactEntity(entity%, index%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns contact number index (counting from 1) of entity, or 0 if there is none.

```basic
; List everything the ball is touching (numbering starts at 1)
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
For index = 1 To CountContacts(ball)
    other = ContactEntity(ball, index)
    Print "Touching entity " + other
Next
```

### PhysicsRayCast

`PhysicsRayCast(x#, y#, z#, dx#, dy#, dz#)` → integer

**(ext)** Casts a ray from x, y, z along dx, dy, dz against physics bodies and returns the first entity hit, or 0; PickedX and the other picked commands give the hit details.

```basic
; Look straight down from above the world and see what is below
hit = PhysicsRayCast(0, 10, 0, 0, -1, 0)   ; start x,y,z then direction dx,dy,dz
If hit <> 0 Then Print "Ground is at height " + PickedY()
```

## Joints and ragdolls

### CreateBallJoint

`CreateBallJoint(entity1%, entity2%, x#, y#, z#)` → integer

**(ext)** Joins the physics bodies of entity1 and entity2 with a ball joint at world point x, y, z and returns the joint.

```basic
; Hang a weight from a fixed anchor, like a pendulum
anchor = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity anchor, 0, 5, 0
EntityPhysics anchor, 2, 2
weight = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity weight, 2, 5, 0
EntityPhysics weight, 1, 2
joint = CreateBallJoint(anchor, weight, 0, 5, 0)   ; joint point x,y,z
```

### CreateHingeJoint

`CreateHingeJoint(entity1%, entity2%, x#, y#, z#, axis_x#, axis_y#, axis_z#)` → integer

**(ext)** Joins entity1 and entity2 with a hinge at world point x, y, z turning about axis axis_x, axis_y, axis_z, and returns the joint.

```basic
; A door that swings about a vertical axis
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
door = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity door, 1, 2, 0.1
PositionEntity door, 1, 0, 0
EntityPhysics door, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, door, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)   ; point 0,0,0 and axis 0,1,0
```

### CreateFixedJoint

`CreateFixedJoint(entity1%, entity2%)` → integer

**(ext)** Locks the physics bodies of entity1 and entity2 together and returns the joint.

```basic
; Glue a gun onto a vehicle so they move as one
vehicle = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics vehicle, 1, 1
gun = CreateCylinder()
PositionEntity gun, 0, 1, 0
EntityPhysics gun, 1, 4
glue = CreateFixedJoint(vehicle, gun)
```

### FreeJoint

`FreeJoint joint%`

**(ext)** Removes joint.

```basic
a = CreateCube()
b = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics a, 1, 1
EntityPhysics b, 1, 1
joint = CreateFixedJoint(a, b)
FreeJoint joint                      ; the two bodies are separate again
```

### CreateSliderJoint

`CreateSliderJoint(entity1%, entity2%, x#, y#, z#, axis_x#, axis_y#, axis_z#)` → integer

**(ext)** Joins entity1 and entity2 with a slider at world point x, y, z that moves along axis axis_x, axis_y, axis_z, and returns the joint.

```basic
; A piston that slides up and down along the Y axis
base = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics base, 2, 1
piston = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics piston, 1, 4
slider = CreateSliderJoint(base, piston, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)
```

### JointMotor

`JointMotor joint%, speed#, [max_force#=1000]`

**(ext)** Drives a hinge or slider joint at speed (degrees or units per second), pushing with at most max_force.

```basic
; Spin a hinge like a motor at 90 degrees per second
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, wheel, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)
JointMotor hinge, 90, 500            ; speed, strongest push
```

### JointTarget

`JointTarget joint%, target#, [stiffness#=1000], [max_force#=100000]`

**(ext)** Pulls a hinge or slider joint towards target (an angle in degrees or a position in units) like a spring with the given stiffness, pushing with at most max_force.

```basic
; Make a hinge swing to 45 degrees and stay there like a spring
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
arm = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics arm, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, arm, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1)
JointTarget hinge, 45, 1000, 5000    ; target angle, stiffness, strongest push
```

### JointMotorOff

`JointMotorOff joint%`

**(ext)** Turns off the motor or target of joint.

```basic
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, wheel, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)
JointMotor hinge, 90
JointMotorOff hinge                  ; the wheel turns freely again
```

### JointLimits

`JointLimits joint%, min#, max#, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Limits a hinge's angle (degrees) or a slider's position (units) to between min and max; enable 0 removes the limits.

```basic
; A door that opens only between 0 and 100 degrees
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
door = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics door, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, door, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)
JointLimits hinge, 0, 100            ; min, max (enable defaults to 1)
JointLimits hinge, 0, 0, 0           ; enable 0 removes the limits again
```

### JointAngle

`JointAngle(joint%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how far a hinge joint has turned since it was made, in degrees.

```basic
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
door = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics door, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, door, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)
UpdateWorld
```
